Also **extended the `no $queryRawUnsafe` invariant to scan `src/services`** — CLAUDE.md points new domain services there, so the newest financial code in the repo had been outside that rule entirely; rather than allowlist the new code, every raw query in it was converted to parameterised tagged templates. 3 owner-scoped endpoints (no `ownerId` parameter anywhere; another owner's payout returns 404, not 403, so the route cannot be used to probe). 35 new tests (17 backend pure, 18 frontend); `tsc` at its 539-error baseline in both apps, measured by stashing rather than assumed; frontend build + architecture + branding checks pass. **Verified against live Postgres inside rolled-back transactions**: the migration applies, all four read-model queries execute, a replayed gateway insert is a genuine no-op (`INSERT 0 0`), and 8 money-safety assertions hold — subscription revenue never settles, uncaptured money never settles, a FAILED transfer stays counted, another owner's payouts are unreachable, one payment cannot join two payouts, and both conservation identities balance. **Not verified:** Prisma's query engine cannot reach the database from this environment (psql and node-pg both can), so the read model was proven at the SQL level, not through the ORM, and no request has been served end-to-end. Migration 075 is **written but NOT applied**. See [[Decisions#ADR-090|ADR-090]], [[Decisions#ADR-091|ADR-091]], [[Decisions#ADR-092|ADR-092]], [[Business-Rules]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-22 — An approved listing is no longer beyond reach
- **The gap:** `approve()` and `reject()` both hard-require `PENDING_REVIEW`, so the instant a revision became `APPROVED` there was no transition out of it except being superseded by a later approval. An admin who spotted a wrong price on a live listing had one lever — suspend the entire hostel — and **even that was wired to no button**: `suspend-listing`/`reactivate` existed as backend routes *and* frontend API wrappers with zero callers anywhere in the app.
- **Two levers now, separated by blast radius.** **Request changes** leaves the approved revision `APPROVED` so the page does not go dark, and drops a `DRAFT` seeded from the live content into the owner's editor carrying the note and section flags — for a typo or a stale menu, an imperfect listing for another day beats a blank one. **Unpublish** marks the approved revision `WITHDRAWN` and the listing immediately reads as a hostel that has not published details — for a false claim, where "still up while they fix it" is the wrong answer. Neither removes the hostel from Discovery; **Suspend** does, and is finally reachable.
- **`WITHDRAWN` is a new revision status, not a reuse of `REJECTED`.** REJECTED means "never went live"; WITHDRAWN means "was live and came down". Same reasoning that keeps `SUPERSEDED` rather than deleting revisions — anything settling "but the listing said ₹4,500" needs to know which happened. Adding it was cheap because revision-status readers are confined to the two marketing services (verified, not assumed).
- **Request changes refuses while a submission is queued**; **unpublish does not** — the live page and the queued submission are different things. Unpublish always demands a written reason, since it has no flags to lean on.
- **The owner is told either way, and the wording carries the consequence** ("It is still live while you work on it" vs "no longer visible… resubmit to go live again"). `getEditorState`'s `last_rejection` now fires on `WITHDRAWN` too and carries the status — a blank listing whose reason appears nowhere in the product is the exact state this set out to avoid.
- **Admins can correct a hostel's address** (`PATCH /api/platform-admin/hostels/:id`, five fields, `address` correctable but not emptiable). Grouped with the navigation block under one **Location** heading in the drawer.
- Decision logic is pure and tested (`post-approval-transitions.ts`, 11 tests); the service writes are covered by 7 more against the real service with a mocked db, including that the approved revision is untouched when changes are requested.
- **Verified:** backend pure 677 passing (same 2 pre-existing failures), frontend 921, `check:architecture`, production build. **Not verified in a browser** — driving the drawer needs a real ADMIN session.

### 2026-08-22 — Finding the front door
- **One-tap Google Maps navigation, with no Google Maps API.** A manually collected **Place ID** is the single source of truth; the `dir/?api=1&destination_place_id=…&dir_action=navigate` URL is built on the frontend at render time and **never stored** — a saved URL beside a saved Place ID is a second source of truth that goes stale silently, and the failure mode is a student on the wrong street.
- **`hostels.navigation` (jsonb, migration 074 — APPLIED to production 2026-08-22).** `placeId`, `landmark`, `entrancePhoto`, `distanceFromReference`, `referenceName`. Nullable, no backfill. Applied with `prisma db execute`, verified by reading `information_schema` and by a write/read/clear probe through the live API. Not applied to the test project (`qsjrazcbtpmubclkevwi`) — the raw-SQL access path does not require it there.
- **The column is deliberately NOT declared on the Prisma model.** Declaring it made Prisma demand it on every `hostels` read that passes no explicit `select` — ~10 `include:`-only reads out of 147 — and that took down every listing detail page in production before the migration ran. Navigation is read and written through raw SQL instead, so the app works whether or not 074 has been applied and the block appears by itself once it has. See [[Bugs]].
- **Admin-only, and that is the whole design.** Written exclusively by `PUT /api/platform-admin/hostels/:id/navigation`; there is no owner route that reads or writes it, and it is deliberately *not* in `hostel_marketing_revisions.content`, which is owner-authored and moves with the draft/review lifecycle. Putting a Place ID there would let an owner aim Stayo's directions at whatever building they liked. Same side of the line as `listing_status` (ADR-040). See [[Decisions#ADR-088|ADR-088]].
- **The listing leads with the last fifty metres**: "400m from SNIST", the landmark, the entrance photo under **"Look for this entrance"**, and a Get Directions button. `referenceName` is stored rather than hardcoded, so the first hostel near a different college is not a code change. The owner's existing `places[]` list still renders underneath, unchanged.
- **A hostel nobody has located renders no block at all.** `parseNavigation()` never throws and a malformed row degrades to `null` — no button beats a button that opens the wrong building.
- **Approval warns, it does not block.** Both LIVE hostels had no Place ID; a hard gate would have made them unfixable through the normal flow. The drawer's Navigation block sits *outside* the review branch for the same reason — navigation is hostel-level, not revision-level.
- Tests: 14 backend (`hostel-navigation.test.ts`, added to `vitest.pure.config.ts`'s explicit allowlist) + 10 frontend (`hostelNavigation.test.ts`). Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-22-hostel-navigation-phase-1.md`.
- **Verified end-to-end on production:** the listing API served a real `navigation` object the moment the column existed, with no redeploy — the self-healing read path proven, not merely argued. Probe data cleared; all four hostel rows are `NULL`, awaiting real Place IDs from the Place ID Finder. Earlier: frontend 900 tests, backend pure 642 passing (2 failures pre-existing and proven unrelated — `discovery-service.test.ts` fails at import on WhatsApp env config with my edit stashed away; `agreement-requirement.test.ts` likewise untouched), `check:architecture`, production build. **Not verified in a browser, and migration 074 is not applied.**

### 2026-08-22 — The hostel card, made symmetric
- **The "Verified" badge is off the card.** Every hostel on Stayo is verified, so a badge on every card carried no information and cost the top-left corner of every photo. Verification is now stated once, where it is actually a claim about the list — the "Verified hostels" heading above the grid.
- **The card is a square.** `aspect-square` on the card itself, with the photo taking every pixel the text block does not — so the footprint is exactly 1:1 at any column width, not just at the one width two fixed ratios happened to agree on. Cards got shorter and the grid noticeably denser.
- **Three lines of text, not four.** The chip row is gone; its two facts moved into the meta line as text — `⌖ Hyderabad · 4-bed · Meals`, where the location truncates and the two facts after it never do, because a long address must not be what pushes "Meals" off the card. That buys the photo back ~40px on a 290px card. Room types read as `sharingSummary` ("4-bed", or "2-bed +2") — the smallest leads, since it sets the advertised price. The Saved list's compact row still draws chips; it has the width.
- **The bottom row has two sides.** Price sits left and availability right, under a hairline that ends the block rather than letting it trail into white space. "Meals included" came off that baseline entirely — it had been pulling the row off-centre — and now rides the meta line.
- **Vacancy is stated, not counted.** "157 beds free" is a number nobody acts on; past a handful the only question is whether there is room. `availabilityFact()` says *Beds available* (green) at 5 or more, *"3 beds left"* (amber) below that — where the exact count is the reason to enquire today — and *Fully booked* at zero, which the old card rendered as nothing at all.
- **A city an owner also typed into the address is said once.** The card used to draw "Hyderabad, Hyderabad" for most hostels; `locationLabel()` dedupes case-insensitively.
- **Hover does something on a laptop.** The card lifts and warms its border, the photo drifts to 1.05 on its own layer (so the chips and pucks do not move with it), the scrim deepens, the name goes terracotta, and Share/Save fade in — they are hidden until hover at `lg`, which is what takes two opaque discs off every thumbnail in the grid. A saved heart stays visible regardless, since it is then a state readout and not just a control. Touch widths keep both pucks always-on.
- **The card is keyboard-reachable.** Its real control is the title as a `<button>` with a stretched `::after`, so the whole card stays clickable without nesting Share/Save inside another button, and the card takes a focus ring.
- Decision logic is pure and tested (`hostelCardFacts.ts`, 16 tests); the component is a renderer. `HostelCardSkeleton` is square too and splits where the real card splits, so nothing shifts when results land.
- **Verified:** full frontend suite (904 tests), `check:architecture`, production build + branding check. **Not verified in a browser** — no browser-automation tool in this environment.
### 2026-08-20 — Owner Leads tab: Accept / Hold / Reject replaces the no-op "Follow up" button
- `visitor_leads.status` (plain string, no migration) gains `ACCEPTED`/`ON_HOLD`/`REJECTED`, guarded by a new pure `canTransitionLeadStatus()` (`src/services/admissions/lead-transition-guards.ts`). See [[Decisions#ADR-087|ADR-087]].
- **Owner:** the Leads tab's card lost "Follow up"/"Chat" (Chat was already a stub) — the whole card is now tappable, WhatsApp is a real `wa.me` action. `LeadDetailSheet` gained an Accept/Hold/Reject footer: Accept routes to the Add Tenant wizard pre-filled from the lead (name/phone/email/hostel — the wizard previously had no pre-fill mechanism at all); Hold requires and stores a reason as an ordinary `lead_notes` row, with **no tenant notification of any kind**; Reject asks for confirmation, then notifies the tenant.
- **No new API routes.** Accept/Hold/Reject all PATCH the existing `/api/leads/[id]`. The previously-dead `POST /api/leads/[id]/convert-to-invitation` (zero prior callers) is now the Add Tenant wizard's real submit path when it opened from a lead — its existing `converted_tenant_id` guard is what prevents a duplicate tenant on double-accept/refresh.
- **Reject's notification reuses an already-built-but-uncalled template**, `TENANT_ENQUIRY_REJECTED` (`enquiry-template-contracts.ts`), not yet approved in Meta so the send currently no-ops (logged, non-fatal). **Same-day amendment:** Hold originally sent a mirrored `TENANT_ENQUIRY_ON_HOLD` notice — removed by explicit product direction (Hold is an owner-internal note, not tenant-facing); the template, its builder, and the call site were deleted along with their tests, not left dead. Accept/Reject/tenant activation unaffected by the amendment.
- **Tenant activation:** phone-OTP requirement deliberately unchanged (no existing trust chain to safely skip it on). Name/hostel were already locked; the account email field now pre-fills from the invitation (previously always blank) as the one safe, non-weakening improvement.
- One-line fix in `discovery-service.ts`'s `toEnquiryStage()` so a Discover-sourced lead the owner rejects projects `CLOSED` to the seeker, not stuck `REVIEWING`.
- Verified (post-amendment): backend `npm run test:pure` (32 new tests, full suite 567/567 minus 2 pre-existing unrelated failures) and frontend's full suite (826 tests) pass; `check:architecture` passes; frontend production build succeeds; backend `check:invariants` shows the same pre-existing, unrelated "first hostel fallback" failure noted in the entry above, confirmed unrelated by reverting this change's files. **Not verified:** end-to-end against a live database/browser (no `DATABASE_URL_TEST`, no local `tsc` in this environment).
- See [[Features]], [[Business-Rules]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Decisions]].

### 2026-08-20 — Room space: the number nobody publishes
- **Rooms can be measured** (migration **073**, applied): `length_ft`, `width_ft`, `cupboard_per_bed`, `under_bed_storage`, `study_desk`, `windows`, all nullable and CHECK-constrained. Owners enter them in the room sheet; the listing turns them into **floor area per bed** — a 140 sq ft room is 35 sq ft a person at 4-sharing and 23 at 6, the same photograph and a different life. Nobody publishes this, which is why it is worth publishing.
- **Storage is counted objects, never adjectives**: "One lockable cupboard per person · A large suitcase fits under each bed · A study desk for every bed". Every listing claims "ample storage"; none of them can be checked.
- **Three refusals are pinned by tests** (19 in `room-space.ts`): a room nobody measured says nothing rather than showing a default; where rooms of one sharing size differ the listing says so and promises the *smaller*; and storage is only claimed when every room of that size has it. The size wording does not flatter — under 25 sq ft per bed reads "Tight", because a listing that calls that "cosy" is why people distrust listings.
- **Two UI bugs fixed on the listing**: the hostel's name and Share/Save rendered twice at desk width, and the body sheet's `-mt-6` (which lifts it over the phone's full-bleed gallery) was climbing over the desktop photo grid and half-covering "Show all photos". That button now sits in the grid's corner, where Airbnb puts it, and the phone gallery gained its own way into the tour.
- Design note: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-20-feeling-the-room-design.md`. This is its phase 1; 360° capture is phase 3 and deliberately last.

### 2026-08-20 — Photo tour
- **"Show all photos" opens a photo tour**, grouped by the part of the hostel — Rooms, Bathrooms, Mess & kitchen, Common areas, Study & work, Building & outside — with a thumbnail strip to jump by, each section's first photo large and the rest two-up, and any photo opening full screen. Airbnb's shape, Stayo's palette and vocabulary.
- **Photos carry a `category`** (`marketing-content.ts`, no migration — it is JSON content), chosen by the owner in the photos screen and reviewed like everything else. Anything uploaded before this lands in "More photos" rather than disappearing from a grouped view.
- Grouping rules are pinned in `photo-tour.ts` (9 tests): empty sections never render, the order is the order someone decides in, a video stays with the photos of the same place, and the full-screen viewer walks the tour's own order so "next" from the last room photo is the first bathroom photo.
- Planned, not built: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-20-feeling-the-room-design.md` — 360° rooms and, more importantly, making room size *felt* (per-bed floor area, storage as counted objects, a drawn-to-scale plan). Its first finding is that Stayo collects none of the data any of it needs.

### 2026-08-19 — Only residents review, and reviews are per-category
- **Reviews are now restricted to people who lived there.** Current (`ACTIVE`) and former (`FORMER_TENANT`) residents *of that hostel* only; `INVITED`, `CANCELLED` and `EXPIRED` are invitations never taken up. This reverses the same-day decision that `stayed_here` be a badge rather than a gate — labelling the trustworthy reviews left the page open to anyone with an account. Enforced server-side and reported in the payload, so the box says why before anyone writes. Verified against live tenancy rows. See [[Decisions#ADR-086|ADR-086]].
- **Per-category ratings** (migration **072**, applied): Cleanliness, Food & mess, Safety, Staff & support, Value for money, Location — Airbnb's six translated for a year-long stay, with food asked only of hostels that serve meals. The overall star is the **mean of the categories**, never a separate question. The listing shows a per-category breakdown; the admin queue shows each review's scores, with low ones marked, because a 1-star for food under a 4 for location is a different decision from a grumble.
- Tests +8 eligibility, +category coverage in the summary.

### 2026-08-19 — A signed-in user could not post a review, and cross-surface logins are announced
- **Writing a review returned "Sign in to continue" to people who were signed in.** `PUBLIC_ROUTES` is prefix-matched and `/api/discover/hostels` is public, so `POST .../reviews` inherited that — and the public branch of `middleware.ts` *strips every identity header and never sets `x-auth-mode`*, making `getSession` return null by design. Reading reviews must stay public; writing one cannot. `requiresSessionDespitePublicPrefix` states that exception (5 tests). Verified: POST with no session now returns the middleware's "Authentication required" instead of the route's message, and GET is still 200. See [[Bugs]].
- **An owner signing in on Discovery is told before being moved.** Both doors used to bounce the wrong-side account to the other app silently, mid-redirect — which right after typing a password reads as a bug or as somebody else's account. `crossSurfaceHandoff` decides the destination and the sentence; both surfaces show it for 1.6s and then go. The redirect itself is unchanged: it was the right destination, just unexplained.

### 2026-08-19 — Footprints, a way back, and share from a card
- **Footprints follow the cursor across Discovery** — and are painted *underneath* the page (`fixed z-0` under content at `z-1`), so a print can only ever land on the graph-paper ground in the margins, never across a hostel photo. Off on touch, off under `prefers-reduced-motion`, off below 1024px, `pointer-events: none`, six prints maximum, one rAF per move. `footprintTrail.ts`, 11 tests.
- **Arrow keys in the photo viewer were dead.** One effect with no dependency array re-ran on every render and reset `scrollLeft` to the frame the viewer opened on, so every move undid itself. Split into a mount-only jump and a re-bound key handler; verified in a browser (1→2→3→2, and the on-screen arrow).
- **There was no way back from a listing at desk width** — the only Back button lived over the phone gallery, which is hidden there. There is one in the desktop header now, and it falls back to `/discover` when the page was opened from a shared link (`location.key === 'default'`), where `navigate(-1)` had nowhere to go.
- **Share on hostel cards**, beside Save.
- Reviews stand down entirely if their endpoint errors — until migration 071 runs, a write box that throws when someone finishes typing is worse than no section.

### 2026-08-19 — The listing page, rebuilt on Airbnb's shape
- **Title above the photos, and a photo grid** at desk width: one large frame plus four, with "Show all N photos" opening a full-screen viewer (`MediaLightbox`, same scroll-snap track as the phone gallery, arrow keys and Escape). A phone keeps the immersive full-bleed carousel.
- **Who runs it.** The payload now carries `host` — the owner as "Ravi K." and "On Stayo since March 2026". Name only: a public listing is not where a phone number goes, and a PLATFORM_LISTED hostel says "Listed by Stayo" rather than naming the sentinel profile.
- **Highlights and About are finally rendered.** Both were collected in the marketing editor, reviewed by an admin, and displayed nowhere.
- **Tapping "Log in" opens the sign-in sheet** instead of routing to `/profile`, whose entire signed-out content was a second sign-in button — two taps and a page load for one intent. `AppNavTab.action`, covered by tests.

### 2026-08-19 — The enquiry-rejected WhatsApp template is actually sent
- **`TENANT_ENQUIRY_REJECTED` had existed as an approved template since the enquiry work shipped and nothing ever sent it** — a Discovery applicant whose enquiry was turned down simply heard nothing, forever. It now fires when an owner moves that lead to `LOST`. `OWNER_ENQUIRY_RECEIVED` was already wired and is unchanged.
- **Only for enquiries that came through Discovery** (`seeker_profile_id`): those people have a Stayo account and used the platform to ask. A walk-in lead marked lost never opted into being messaged.
- **No "accepted" message, by decision.** An approved enquiry is followed by an invitation, and the invitation *is* the acceptance — a second notification would say what the invite already says.
- **The reason is translated, never repeated.** `JOINED_COMPETITOR` and `NO_RESPONSE` are deliberately unmapped — the first is the owner's read of someone else's decision, the second an accusation — and fall through to the template's neutral wording. `rejectionReasonText`, 4 tests. See [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-19 — Tenant screens: fixed headers, and the dark slabs removed
- **Screen titles stay put.** My Room / My Menu / Payments rendered their title as the first item in the scrolling column, so the name of the screen you were on left the page as soon as you looked at anything on it. One `TenantPageHeader`, sticky, translucent over the graph-paper ground.
- **The dark headers on Home and Profile are gone.** Each spent ~120px of a phone screen and a radial glow to say a name — on Home, above "rent due". Both now sit on the app's own paper ground with the terracotta monogram carrying the brand instead of a slab of ink, and both are sticky.
- **Home greets by time of day** ("Good evening, Sharan") rather than repeating the product name at someone who lives there — pure `tenantGreeting.ts`, 4 tests.
- **Share and Save came off the photograph** on a hostel listing. Three opaque pucks sat in the middle of the subject of every cover photo; they are pills beside the hostel's name now, labelled from `sm` up. Only Back stays over the image — you need a way out before you have scrolled — over a gradient rather than as a white disc.

### 2026-08-19 — Hostel reviews, and a laptop layout for the listing
- **Reviews on a hostel listing**, written by signed-in accounts and **published only by an admin** (`/admin/reviews`). Every review lands PENDING; editing a published one sends it back. See [[Decisions#ADR-086|ADR-086]], migration **071 (needs applying)**.
- **The listing says why it has no reviews** rather than showing a blank: Stayo checks each one before it appears. And it never prints an average below three reviews — two reviews averaging 3.0 carry a number's authority and a sample of two.
- **Laptop layout for the listing page**: two columns from `lg` — the listing on the left, a sticky price/Enquire card on the right, replacing the bottom bar that was pinned below the fold of a 900px window.
- New: `GET/POST /api/discover/hostels/[slug]/reviews`, `GET /api/platform-admin/reviews`, `PATCH /api/platform-admin/reviews/[id]`. See [[APIs]].
- Tests +12 backend pure.

### 2026-08-19 — Listing media, the review cycle, and the kitchen menu
- **A phone multi-select of photos no longer fails.** The client batched a whole selection into one request, so ten legal 4MB photos became a ~40MB body rejected above every size check we had — the owner was told a limit was exceeded that nothing had crossed. One file per request now, photos downscaled in the browser first. See [[Bugs]].
- **Videos** upload alongside photos (MP4/WebM/MOV, 60MB), play inline in the Discovery gallery, and can never be the cover — the cover is the search card's still and a shared link's `og:image`. `content.photos` gains `kind` and `thumbnail_url`, both back-compatible.
- **Reordering** on the photos screen: ‹ › on every tile, plus drag on a desktop. The gallery's order is the order visitors swipe through.
- **A live listing used to call itself "Draft."** The page read one field (`draft.status`), which after approval is `DRAFT` because there is no open revision. `listingLifecycle` derives the real state from three facts and shows a Draft → With Stayo → Live tracker, so a submission is visibly received and unsent edits are visibly not live. See [[Bugs]].
- **Admins can see what changed.** The review drawer diffs the submission against the live version — per section, before → after, price changes called out on their own, added/removed media, a changed cover, reordering, and which days of the mess menu were edited. The data was already in the payload; nothing had rendered it.
- **"Copy this hostel's kitchen menu"** fills the listing's mess editor from the published Food schedule, as an editable draft that still goes through review ([[Decisions#ADR-085|ADR-085]]).
- **Admin edit parity**: `photos`, `submit` and `withdraw` took a bare owner id, so an admin authoring a listing could save text but not upload a photo or submit it. All three take a `MarketingActor` now.
- Tests +37 frontend (797→834), +6 backend pure.

### 2026-08-19 — Share a hostel
- **`yourstayo.com/h/:slug`** — a share link whose preview card carries the hostel's cover photo, name, city and starting price in WhatsApp, Instagram, Telegram and anything else that unfurls a URL. A `vercel.json` rewrite (placed above the SPA catch-all) sends it to a new `GET /api/discover/share/[slug]`, which renders HTML; the SPA cannot do this itself, since it is one `index.html` for every route and crawlers do not run JS. See [[Decisions#ADR-084|ADR-084]].
- **Share buttons** on the Discovery listing page (beside Save) and the owner marketing page (enabled only once the listing is approved and has a public slug). `navigator.share` where it exists — the OS sheet is the only route to Instagram — and clipboard + toast where it does not. A dismissed sheet is a no-op.
- An unlisted slug renders **no hostel name and no hostel photo**, `noindex`, 404: a suspended hostel must stop unfurling in chats it was already pasted into.
- `middleware.ts` gains `/api/discover/share` in `PUBLIC_ROUTES` (narrower than `/api/discover`, which would expose enquiry history).
- Tests +19 backend pure, +10 frontend (788→797). Verified in a browser: fetched with a WhatsApp user-agent the tags resolve to the hostel's ImageKit cover, and clicking the button copies `/h/<slug>` and toasts.
- Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-19-hostel-share-links-design.md`.

### 2026-08-19 — Hostel photos, on both Discovery surfaces
- **Cards showed a placeholder for every hostel.** `toCard()` read `hostels.admission_photos`, which nothing writes — photos live on the APPROVED marketing revision. New shared `listingPhotos()` in `listing-projection.ts` (approved photos win, **cover first**, honouring the `is_cover` flag that `marketing-content.ts` has always guaranteed and nobody read), applied to cards/saved/enquiries via `fillCoverPhotos()` **after** pagination — the search scans up to 500 candidates to build facets and must not load 500 revisions to read one field. Cards carry the cover only. See [[Bugs]].
- **The listing gallery had no way to reach photo 2.** One background image, controlled by three 3px indicator bars that sat under the body sheet. It is a native scroll-snap track now — swipe on touch, trackpad on a laptop, arrows at `md`+ — with the index from a pure `photoIndexFromScroll` (5 tests), indicators lifted clear of the sheet, bigger tap targets, and a scrim so white indicators survive a bright photo.
- Listing body and enquire bar capped at 860px, like the rest of Discover.
- Tests +5 frontend (783→788), +6 backend pure. Verified end to end in a browser: the API returns the cover, and the gallery was driven for real (swipe → "3 / 3", dot → "1 / 3").

### 2026-08-19 — Discover at laptop width
- **`/discover` was a phone layout stretched to the browser window** — one ~1400px-wide hostel card per row, a phone-sized hero, and a full-bleed bottom bar carrying two items. Explore and Search now share `PAGE_SHELL` (max 1180px) and `RESULTS_GRID` (1 → 2 → 3 → 4 columns) from `discoverTheme.ts`, so the two pages cannot disagree about page width or column count.
- **Explore at `lg`**: a top bar (wordmark · "List your hostel" → `/owners` · account/log-in), an Airbnb-style **Where / What** search bar with the city facet as a popover under it, centred quick filters, and a **"Why look here and not on a listings site"** strip stating the three claims the data can back — verified before listing, live rent and vacancy, enquiry straight to the owner. A visitor arriving from a shared link was previously told nowhere on the page what Stayo is.
- **The signed-out account tab is a person icon now** (`CircleUserRound`), not lucide's `LogIn` arrow-into-door — the same glyph most apps use for *sign out*, so it read as "leave" to someone who had never signed in. `AppBottomNav` also becomes a centred floating dock from `lg` up.
- **Search**: capped width, the same results grid, and its filter bottom sheet becomes a centred dialog at `lg`. `HostelCard`'s photo is a fixed 186px only at phone width and `sm:aspect-[4/3]` above it, since a fixed height letterboxes a grid tile.
- **White wordmark on the ink header** — `public/stayo-wordmark-white.svg`; the terracotta original stays for light grounds.
- **The phone hero lost three competing tiers.** It stacked wordmark, a "DISCOVER ON STAYO" eyebrow, an 18px bold city line, the headline, a subtitle and a two-line search field. The eyebrow is desktop-only, the search field is one line (its second line listed available cities right under a control showing the selected city — two location readouts, neither obviously current), and the **city picker moved into the filter row as its first chip**, since "where" is a filter. Phone hero is now wordmark → headline → one line → search.
- Verified in a browser at 390px and 1440px, including the city picker's open state at both; frontend suite 783 tests and `check:architecture` pass. See [[Frontend]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-19 — Meal Timings: owner-configured serving windows, tenant Next Serving card
- New `meal_timings` JSON key in `hostels.preferences_config` — start/end/enabled per meal type, zero migration. Replaces the hardcoded frontend-only `MEAL_TIMES` constant (`owner-food/weekGrid.ts`), which is removed along with `mealSlotAt`. See [[Decisions#ADR-083|ADR-083]].
- **Backend:** new pure `lib/services/food/meal-timings.ts` (normalize/validate, no I/O — tested under `npm run test:pure`, 18 new tests); new `GET/PATCH /api/hostels/[id]/meal-timings` (owner) and `GET /api/food/tenant/meal-timings` (tenant).
- **Owner:** new `/owner/food/meal-timings` screen (toggle each meal, edit start/end, save-only-when-dirty, confirmation toast), linked from `/owner/food`. The Weekly Schedule grid gained a read-only meal-time legend — never re-entered per cell. `TodayCard` and `OwnerHomeDashboard`'s food line both switched from the hardcoded hour to real per-hostel timings.
- **Tenant:** new `NextServingCard` (meal, dish, time range, live "Starts in 42 min"/"Serving Now" countdown) mounted on both `/tenant/food` and `/tenant/home`. Today's Meals on the Food page now includes Snacks (previously missing) and shows time + a Served/Serving now/Upcoming status pill.
- Verified: full frontend suite (803 tests) and backend `test:pure` (534 tests, 2 pre-existing unrelated failures untouched) pass; `check:architecture` passes; production build succeeds; both new routes smoke-tested against the running dev server (401, not 500).
- See [[Features]], [[Business-Rules]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Food]], [[Decisions]].

### 2026-08-16 — Marketing review, finished; and Stayo-listed hostels
- **The review loop is real.** The admin drawer now shows every section an owner submitted — basics, photos, bed tiers, amenities, getting around, the full 7-day mess grid — each independently flaggable with its own note. Send-back carries structured flags and the owner's notification names the sections. Replaces a `window.prompt`. See [[Decisions]] ADR-082.
- **Preview shares the live code path.** `getListing`'s inline mapping became `listing-projection.ts`; `/admin/listings/preview/:revisionId` renders the real Discovery `ListingPage` through it. One renderer, so preview cannot drift from what ships.
- **Stayo-listed hostels** (migration 068): `hostels.listing_source`, `claimed_at/by`, a Stayo-listed tab with creation and an assign-to-owner flow. `owner_id` stays NOT NULL via an inactive sentinel profile.
- **Platform listings never advertise live vacancy** — no real rooms exist, so `availability_confirmed` is false and bed tiers read as an advertised claim. A missed `select` would have shipped the opposite; caught while wiring the projection.
- **Enquiries on unclaimed listings raise a sales lead**, named after the hostel and carrying no phone (a business's number is not a consented contact).
- Tests +45 backend, +2 frontend. Migrations 067 and 068 remain **unapplied outside the test database**.


### 2026-08-16 — Leads section + the merged owner lifecycle (migration 067)
- **One lifecycle, not two.** The design shipped a sales funnel while the code had an activation funnel; they are halves of one journey. `PlatformLeadStatus` gains `CONTACTED`, `DEMO`, `NEGOTIATING`. Ownership stays split and is enforced where it always was — `MANUALLY_SETTABLE_STATUSES` now covers the admin-driven stages, `APPROVED` onward remains system-only. `UNDER_REVIEW` rows migrate to `CONTACTED`; the value is retained since Postgres cannot drop an enum member. See [[Decisions]] ADR-081.
- **A prospect must never read "Negotiating" about themselves.** `lead-stage-mapper.ts` drives the public `/enquiry/:token` page, so all three new stages collapse into "Under review". Four regression tests pin it, including one asserting the words never appear anywhere in the rendered timeline.
- **New tables** (`migrations/067_lead_crm.sql`): `platform_lead_activities` (outreach log — call connected / no answer / email sent) and `platform_lead_notes` (thread that survives a handover). `platform_leads` gains discovery answers, qualification fields, `estimated_value`, and a structured `lost_reason` enum. See [[Database]].
- **New endpoints**: `GET/POST .../leads/[id]/activities`, `GET/POST .../notes`, `PATCH .../qualification`, `POST/DELETE .../lost`, `GET /leads/insights`. See [[APIs]].
- **Why the lost reason is an enum and discovery is not**: `pain_point`/`current_tooling` are free-form by design (a reworded option creates a new distinct value), so insights *counts* lost reasons and *lists* discovery quotes. Bar-charting free text would invent categories out of phrasing.
- Logging outreach deliberately does not advance the stage — three "no answer" entries are not progress, and auto-advancing would take the decision from the admin who made the call.
- Tests +16 frontend (737→753), +4 backend. See [[Features]], [[Frontend]].

### 2026-08-16 — Admin Overview screen, on real data
- Built the design's OVERVIEW section: six KPI cards, the owner-acquisition funnel, "Waiting on your review", and Live activity. Sources: `/platform-admin/dashboard`, `/leads` counts, pending owner-documents, pending hostels, `/platform-admin/notifications`.
- **The funnel is cumulative by construction** — a `LIVE` lead is counted as having passed through every earlier stage. Counting statuses in isolation would draw a funnel that widens at the bottom as leads progress, which is the standard way these charts mislead. Pinned by a monotonic-narrowing test.
- **Two deliberate departures from the mockup**, both to avoid misreporting: the revenue card reads "Revenue this month" because `/dashboard` returns month-to-date (the design says "today" — same number under that label overstates daily revenue by ~30x); and the 14-day daily-gross bar chart states that daily revenue is not measured, since no endpoint returns a date series.
- The design's "Landing visitors" funnel row is omitted — it needs web analytics the platform does not collect, and inventing it would make every conversion rate below it fictional. See [[Decisions]] ADR-080's honest-gap rule.
- Tests +17 (724→741). See [[Frontend]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-16 — The admin console, rebuilt from scratch
- **Replaced the entire platform-admin console** with the new `Stayo Admin.dc.html` design. `AdminAppShell` and all 9 `Admin*Page.tsx` files deleted (~3,400 LOC); new shell at `platforms/admin/layout/AdminConsoleShell.tsx` with a dark 250px sidebar, four nav groups, an addressable detail drawer and a toast host. See [[Decisions]] ADR-080, [[Frontend]], [[Features]].
- **Eleven routes**, with four redirects so old links survive: `documents→kyc`, `hostels→listings`, `marketing-reviews→listings?tab=content`, `more→settings`.
- **Real screens**: Owners (list + drawer on `/platform-admin/owners`), Hostel Listings (approve/reject + the marketing content-review queue folded in as a tab), KYC Approvals (documents grouped by owner, approve/reject with a required rejection note).
- **Layout-only, by decision**: Overview, Leads, Revenue, Settlements, Subscriptions, Reports & Bugs, Broadcasts, Settings — each renders `NotWiredYet` rather than fabricated data. Settlements and Reports have no backend at all; the rest are queued for the next plan.
- **Two regressions caught during the rebuild, not shipped**: the console's only sign-out control lived on the deleted More page and is now in the sidebar footer (`logoutIntegrity.test.ts` covers it, 8→9 generated cases); and the old console rendered Playfair Display where the design specifies Manrope, because it reused the shared `.font-display` token — the console now has its own `.font-admin` scope.
- **Tests**: +33 (699→724 across the rebuild), all pure-logic modules — nav model, drawer URL state, owner row shaping, listing tabs, KYC grouping.
- Spec and plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-admin-console-rebuild-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-16-admin-console-foundation.md`.
### 2026-08-16 — Profile → Raise a Ticket: a second, Stayo Admin-bound ticket system
- New, fully independent ticket system for reporting Stayo app/website problems — see [[Decisions#ADR-079|ADR-079]] for the full reasoning, including why an earlier proposal to reuse `/tenant/complaints` was rejected.
- **Schema:** new `platform_support_tickets` model + migration `20260816120000_add_platform_support_tickets` (idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, applied to the dev database). Modeled on `owner_documents`'s shape — `profile_id`-scoped, `status` (`OPEN`→`RESOLVED`, admin-only), `resolved_at`/`resolved_by`/`admin_note`. Deliberately **no `owner_id`, no `hostel_id`, no relation to `tenants`**.
- **Backend:** `GET/POST /api/profile/support-tickets` (any signed-in profile, no tenancy requirement); `GET /api/platform-admin/support-tickets?status=` + `POST .../[id]/resolve` (ADMIN-only, mirrors the `owner-documents` route pair).
- **Profile UI:** new standalone "Raise a ticket" section in `DiscoverProfilePage.tsx` (not folded into Support & settings) → new `SupportTicketsPage.tsx` at `/profile/tickets`.
- **Admin UI:** new `AdminSupportTicketsPage.tsx` at `/admin/support-tickets`, reached via a new row on `/admin/more` (not a 7th `ADMIN_TABS` slot — that list stays pixel-matched to `Stayo Admin.dc.html`).
- `tenant_service_requests`/`complaints` (the existing tenant → owner system) are completely untouched — no shared code, no shared table.
- **Verification:** `apps/backend/npm run check:invariants` and `apps/frontend/npm run check:architecture` both pass; full frontend `npm run build` (architecture check + `vite build` + branding check) succeeds; both new route families smoke-tested against the running dev server (401 unauthenticated, not 500). Backend vitest suite could not run — same pre-existing `.env.test`/`DATABASE_URL_TEST` gap noted below, unrelated to this change.
- See [[Features]], [[Business-Rules]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Decisions]].

### 2026-08-16 — Explore/Dashboard/Profile restructure: one shared nav, one common profile, Google-only tenant signup
- Full restructure per explicit product direction — see [[Decisions#ADR-078|ADR-078]] for the complete reasoning (supersedes [[Decisions#ADR-068|ADR-068]]'s "no Complaints tab" call).
- **Nav:** new `app/nav/{appNavConfig,useAppNav}.ts` + `app/layouts/AppShell.tsx` + `app/components/AppBottomNav.tsx` — one shared, state-dependent bottom nav (Explore/Profile, or Explore/Dashboard/Profile with a live tenancy) replacing the two independent ones (`DiscoverShell`'s 4 tabs, `TenantAppShell`'s 5). `TenantAppShell` repurposed into the Dashboard's inner sticky-top tab strip. `tenant_status` threaded through `/auth/me` and every login path.
- **Dashboard:** tabs are now Home/Money/Room/Food/**Complaints** (new `TenantComplaintsPage`). `TenantProfilePage` deleted — Personal/Contact/Emergency/Academic/Documents/Support absorbed into the shared Profile hub.
- **Profile:** `DiscoverProfilePage`/`ProfileEditPage` promoted app-wide via new `ProfileRoutes.tsx` (`/profile/*`), gaining tenancy-scoped details (governed phone/email routing unchanged, reusing `ProfileEditScreen`/`buildProfileEditConfigs`), a new `/profile/documents` page (vault + per-tenancy KYC shown separately), and Saved/Enquiries (moved off primary nav). New canonical `completion_percent` on `profile-identity-service.ts`, read by both the Profile hub's nudge card and the new Dashboard `ProfileCompletionNudge`.
- **Routing/guards:** `ProtectedTenantRoute` drops the `is_profile_completed` hard-block (replaced by the nudge card) and gains a tenancy-liveness check it was missing. `AuthCallbackPage` no longer sends a no-tenancy TENANT to `/tenant/home` unconditionally (a real, pre-existing bug).
- **Auth:** tenant signup is Google-only now (`LoginModal`'s password/phone-OTP signup form removed). New `POST /api/auth/google/provision` + `lib/auth/supabase-provision.ts` — a narrow, separately-tested auto-provisioning path that does not modify or weaken `resolveSupabaseSession()`'s existing "never auto-provisions" invariant (`tests/auth-hardening-security.test.ts` passes unchanged; new `tests/auth-google-provisioning.test.ts` covers the addition). Phone verification moved from signup-time to enquiry-time (`EnquiryPage` gates on `phone_verified`, asks at most once) — reuses the existing OTP infrastructure and `profile.phone`/`phone_verified` columns, **no schema changes**. `PATCH /api/profile` fixed to re-arm `phone_verified` on a real phone change and return `409 PHONE_TAKEN` instead of a raw 500.
- **Dev database:** separately, caught the dev Supabase project up from 34 pending migrations to fully applied (82/82) — years of hand-applied SQL had drifted from the tracked migration history. Each failure was individually verified against the live schema before fixing (12 migration files corrected — idempotency fixes, one stale enum-value reference, one incomplete CHECK-constraint allow-list, one dropped empty orphan table). No production database touched.
- **Not run:** the new backend test suite — this environment has no `.env.test`/`DATABASE_URL_TEST` configured anywhere in the repo, a pre-existing gap that blocks the *entire* vitest suite, not just the new file.
- See [[Features]], [[Business-Rules]], [[APIs]], [[Decisions]].

### 2026-08-15 — Real photo upload, multi-select amenities, the Discover grid, and footprints on touch
- Four owner-reported follow-ups from the marketing-page rebuild and the front door.
- **Photos are uploaded from the device now, not pasted as URLs** (`marketing/PhotosScreen.tsx`, new `POST /api/owner/hostels/[id]/marketing/photos`). File picker plus desktop drag-and-drop over the whole screen, multi-select, placeholder tiles while a batch is in flight, and per-file validation matched to the server's (JPG/PNG/WebP, ≤8MB, ≤10 per request, 24 per listing). Uploads go to ImageKit `/hostel_listings` via the existing `lib/imagekit.ts`, following the `hostels/[id]/logo` route's shape. **The URL field is gone** — it was an interim the previous entry flagged and should not have shipped in an owner-facing surface.
  - The endpoint **writes no revision**: it returns URLs, and the draft persists them on the next save. Writing them server-side too would give the photo list two sources of truth and leave a discarded edit's photo on the listing. It carries the same `PENDING_REVIEW` 409 lock as `PUT`.
  - A partly-failed batch keeps whatever landed rather than discarding the lot, and uploads run sequentially — firing ten multi-megabyte images at the provider at once is how a whole batch fails together on a phone connection.
- **The amenity sheet is multi-select** (`marketing/AmenitySheet.tsx`). It used to add one amenity and close, so listing six meant six trips through the same sheet. Suggestions are now toggle chips, typed names join the same selection instead of committing on their own, and one "Add N to listing" commits and closes. Enter commits a typed name to the selection (`SheetInput` gained `onEnter`).
- **Fixed — Discover was the one product surface with no graph-paper ground.** `GRID_GROUND` had been defined in `discoverTheme.ts` since Discover was built but was **never referenced anywhere**; `DiscoverShell` painted flat `C.paper`. Applied it there, and stripped the redundant opaque `background: C.paper` from nine full-height page roots that sat inside the shell and hid it. The two genuinely-layered paper surfaces (SearchPage's filter sheet over its scrim, ListingPage's body sheet over the hero photo) keep theirs.
- **Fixed — the welcome screen's footprints did nothing on mobile** (`FootprintTrail.tsx`). It bailed on `!(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`, i.e. every touch device, so `/` was completely still on a phone. Now: a dragged finger leaves prints exactly as a cursor does (`pointermove` already covers touch), **and** coarse-pointer devices get an ambient stroll — a walk across the screen every few seconds, laid one print at a time so it reads as walking. `pointerdown` resets the trail origin, so lifting a finger and touching elsewhere starts a new walk instead of drawing a stride between two unrelated touches. Still dropped entirely under `prefers-reduced-motion`; the print geometry, seam colouring and cleanup are unchanged and shared by both paths.
- **Verification:** frontend build clean (architecture + branding checks pass), frontend typecheck error count unchanged at its pre-existing 46, backend `marketing-approval` 24 + `discovery-service` 20 passed. **The upload path is not verified end to end** — no real file has been pushed through ImageKit from this editor, and the mobile footprints are not confirmed on a physical device.
- See [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Frontend]].

### 2026-08-15 — Owner marketing page rebuilt to its design; mess menu added to it and to the Discovery listing
- The owner's Hostel → Marketing page was a flattened version of `Stayo App.dc.html`'s `HOSTEL: MARKETING` section (lines 1648–1835): inline-edit rows instead of the design's sheets, no Photos manager, no tenant Preview, no Template card, and **no Mess menu section at all**. Rebuilt 1:1 against the design, and the matching "Food & mess" section added to the Discovery listing from `Stayo Discover.dc.html`. Both designs were read directly from the Claude Design project via the design MCP rather than from screenshots.
- **Owner page** (`features/hostel-drilldown/pages/HostelMarketingPage.tsx`, new `features/hostel-drilldown/marketing/*`): all nine sections in the design's order — Status (dark `#2A2521` card, toggle, stat row, Preview) → Template → Photos → Beds & pricing → **Mess menu** → Basics → Amenities → Getting around → Reviews → gradient save bar. The design's seven modals are real: `TemplateSheet`, `BasicsSheet`, `AmenitySheet`, `BedSheet`, `PlaceSheet`, `MessMenuSheet` (day-tabbed weekly editor), plus full-screen `PhotosScreen` and `PreviewScreen`. Sheets are built on the existing shared `BottomSheet` in `hideHeader` mode rather than a second scrim/drag implementation — only the header differs from the default.
  - The palette needed almost nothing new: `styles/tokens/product.css` already *is* the design's palette (`--primary` is `#B46A55`, `--border` is `#EFE6DA`, `--muted-foreground` is `#8A7F75`). The dozen accents with no token — the ink card, warm icon tiles, locked-review greys — are pinned in one `marketingTheme.ts`, same pattern as `discoverTheme.ts`.
- **Mess menu** ([[Decisions#ADR-077|ADR-077]]): a `mess` block inside marketing content — `{provided, type, meals[4], week[7]}` — carried through the same review cycle as the rest of the listing. **Deliberately not** wired to the operational `food_schedules` tables, which change monthly and by resident poll with no review; see the ADR for the trade and for what to do if the duplication proves annoying. No migration: `content` is a JSON column, and `normaliseContent` pads/repairs old revisions on the read path so nothing written before this parses badly.
- **Discovery listing** (`app/pages/discover/ListingPage.tsx`): "Food & mess" section between the amenity grid and Getting around, exactly where the design puts it — green type pill, Mon–Sun chips, white card of meal rows. Hidden entirely when the hostel serves no meals.
- **Admin review** (`AdminMarketingReviewsPage.tsx`): a Mess block in the submission diff, so a reviewer can read the whole week before approving it.
- **Three design details deliberately not reproduced literally**, all for the same reason — the design shows data Stayo doesn't have, or an action that would bypass review: its status toggle *publishes*, here it submits/withdraws and the caps label carries the real revision state; `1,240 views · 30d` renders as an em dash because no listing-view tracking exists anywhere; `★ 4.8 · 126 resident reviews` renders as "No reviews yet". Layout, spacing and palette are the design's in all three.
- **Verification:** frontend `npm run build` clean (architecture + branding checks pass), no typecheck errors in any touched file; `tests/marketing-approval.test.ts` 24 passed and `tests/discovery-service.test.ts` 20 passed, including new cases for week padding, the fixed meal set, the menu round-trip, and the four ways the listing's `mess` can come back null. **Not exercised against a live database** — no owner has saved a real menu end to end.
- See [[Features]], [[Decisions#ADR-077|ADR-077]], [[APIs]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Frontend]].

### 2026-08-15 — Tenant dashboard: real rent-due card on Home, "My tickets" Track/Assigned/Completed list
- Owner-reported two gaps against the live app: no payment section reachable from Home, and the complaint/ticket system needed an explicit tracked-state list rather than one inline card. Investigation found both were partial regressions, not missing features — `useTenantFinancials.ts`'s own doc comment and [[Features]]'s tenant-app entry already documented Home sharing the rent-due hero with Money, but `TenantHomePage.tsx` never actually rendered it (a stale doc-comment there explicitly said otherwise); and Room's ticket tracker only ever surfaced `MAINTENANCE`-type requests (`activeTicket` filtered by `type === 'MAINTENANCE'`), so a `ROOM_CHANGE`/`CLEANING`/etc. request had no visible progress and there was no way to see more than one open request at a time.
- **Home** (`TenantHomePage.tsx`): added the rent-due hero card back, matching `Stayo Tenant.dc.html`'s `rentPending` card (period, amount, "Was due" date, Pay button) — shown only when `useTenantFinancials().amountDue > 0`, reusing that hook + its `PaySheet` instance so Home can never disagree with Money on what's owed.
- **Tickets** (`useTenantRoom.ts`, new `platforms/tenant/components/overlays/TicketsListScreen.tsx`): `activeTicket` is now the most recently raised open request of any type, not maintenance-only. New full-screen "My tickets" list groups every request into three tabs — **Track** (RAISED), **Assigned** (ASSIGNED/IN_PROGRESS), **Completed** (RESOLVED/REJECTED) via `bucketForStatus()`, the one place to adjust the grouping. Reachable from Room's "N open · view all" link (shown once >1 request is open) and Profile's "View all activity" (the design source already stubbed this exact button as `viewAllTickets` but never built a destination for it).
- Verified live end-to-end via Playwright against the real dev backend/test tenant: raised a real maintenance ticket, confirmed it landed in Track with the correct pill, confirmed Assigned/Completed empty states, opened the ticket's detail from the list (reuses the existing `buildServiceRequestDetailConfig` stepper) with no console errors. The Home rent-due card itself renders correctly-hidden for this test tenant (₹0 due, same as Money already showed) — not exercised with a nonzero balance in this pass.
- See [[Features]], [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-15 — Food Polls: applied the missing `food_polls` migration to production
- Owner-reported via console screenshot: every Food Polls request 500'd on `yourstayo.com` with `The table public.food_polls does not exist in the current database`. The `20260808130000_add_food_polls` migration (2026-08-08) had only ever been applied by hand to the dev database, never to production — same root-cause class as the `tenants`-migration outage the day before (see [[Bugs]]).
- Fix: applied the migration's SQL directly to production (two enum types, three tables, four indexes, five FKs), via the non-pooled `DIRECT_URL` since the pooled connection rejects multi-statement raw queries. Verified against `information_schema`/`pg_constraint` post-apply. See [[Bugs]], [[Database]].

### 2026-08-15 — Welcome screen tuned for laptops, cursor footprints, and sign-in stops leaving Discover
- **Fixed — `/` was tuned for phones and fighting large screens.** The content column was `max-w-2xl` (672px) centred in a 1920px viewport — **1,248px of dead space** — and the headings were capped at `3.25rem`, so they hit their ceiling on a laptop while hitting their *floor* on a phone. Headings now scale to `4.5rem`/`4.25rem`, the column widens to `max-w-4xl` on `lg`, and both clusters sit closer to the seam. Verified by rendering at 1920×1000: headings up from ~52px to ~72px with both panels' full content clearing the seam.
  - The upper bound is set by the **collapsed** state, not the neutral one: when one side is hovered the seam climbs to 34%, and the other panel's badge + both heading lines still have to clear it. That, not taste, is why the cap is 4.5rem.
- **Added** cursor footprints on `/` (`FootprintTrail.tsx`) — prints alternate left/right, offset perpendicular to the direction of travel so a diagonal walk leaves a gait rather than two rails, and are inked dark on the cream half / warm-light on the near-black half using the same seam geometry the panels clip to. Written imperatively (DOM nodes + CSS, removed on `animationend`) so a pointermove handler never re-renders the front door. Desktop-only (`hover: hover and pointer: fine`) and dropped entirely under `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- **Added** a welcome chime on `/` (`public/stayo-welcome.mp3`, ~4s, played at 0.35 volume by `welcomeChime.ts`). Plays when the splash plays — once per browser session, off the same `sessionStorage` flag — and is skipped under `prefers-reduced-motion`. Stops on unmount so it cannot follow a visitor onto `/owners` or `/discover`.
  - ⚠️ **It will be silent for most first-time visitors, and that is the autoplay policy, not a bug.** Browsers block audio without a prior user gesture, and the splash runs on page load. Returning visitors usually *do* hear it (Chrome's per-origin Media Engagement Index eventually permits autoplay). A rejected `play()` is swallowed deliberately: queuing the chime until the first click would fire a "welcome" three seconds late over a screen the visitor is already reading. **Guaranteeing it means putting it behind a gesture**, the way the onboarding flow's ambient audio already is ([[Decisions#ADR-072|ADR-072]]) — a product decision, not a technical one.
  - Its effect is deliberately mount-scoped rather than living in the splash effect: that one re-runs when `splashDone` flips, and its cleanup would have cut the 4s audio off at 1.6s.
- **Added** a hover lean: the chosen panel's content shifts 10px toward the seam on `lg`+. On a phone the seam moves inches from your thumb; on a 1920px screen it's a slow diagonal far from where the eye is, so the screen felt inert while it was in fact responding.
- **Fixed — Discover's sign-in dumped tenants on the owner marketing page.** Every sign-in point (`Saved`, `Enquiries`, `Profile`, the heart, the enquiry form) navigated to `/login`, which renders `LandingPage`. Owner-reported. Discover now opens the **same** `LoginModal` in place via a new `DiscoverAuthProvider`, in `mode="tenant"` on the signup tab; a save that triggered the prompt completes after signing in rather than being lost. See the amendment on [[Decisions#ADR-035|ADR-035]] — one login *component*, not one login *URL*.
- **Verified:** rendered at 1920×1000 and 1440×900 in headless Chrome; `vite build`, branding and `check:architecture` clean; `tsc` clean across all touched files. **The footprint trail and the hover lean could not be verified in a static screenshot** — both need real pointer movement — and the login modal was not click-driven, only confirmed to render without error.
- See [[Features]], [[Decisions#ADR-035|ADR-035]], [[Decisions#ADR-071|ADR-071]].

### 2026-08-15 — Residency history: a tenant's record, shared on their terms
- **Added** `/discover/profile/history` — the tenant's own stay history (hostel, dates, duration, room, sharing, rent, whether it settled) **and** the controls for who can see it, on one screen. Showing someone their record without showing them its readers erodes trust rather than building it.
- **Added** `GET /api/profile/residency-history`, `GET|POST /api/profile/residency-history/disclosures`, `GET /api/owner/tenant-history`, `POST /api/owner/tenant-history/request`. See [[APIs]].
- **Added** migration `065_residency_history_disclosure.sql` — one small table. **The history itself needed no new storage**: since migration 062 a `tenants` row *is* one tenancy, and `room_allocations` + `move_out_requests` carry the rest. It had never had a reader.
- **Added** `TenantHistoryPanel` on the owner's tenant detail → Stay tab.
- **This narrows [[Decisions#ADR-053|ADR-053]] without reversing it.** That ADR refuses to name another owner's hostel because "any owner could enumerate a competitor's roster, and a person's address, by typing the right email" — a stalking control, not just a competitive one. Disclosure is now **earned by engagement** (an open enquiry, or a tenancy at that hostel) or by the tenant's explicit approval; an owner who merely types an identifier still learns nothing, including whether the person has any history at all. See the amendment on ADR-053.
- **The tenant's "no" outranks engagement.** `REVOKED`/`DECLINED` beats a tenancy that would otherwise disclose automatically. Access is otherwise derived rather than stored, so it cannot go stale or leak through a forgotten grant — when an enquiry closes, access ends by construction.
- **Facts only.** `exit_reason`, `exit_notes` and `tenant_behavior_scores` are never projected: one owner's unreviewed opinion must not follow a person across every hostel on Stayo with no right of reply. Asserted by test. An invitation never taken up is filtered out — it is not a stay.
- **Not delivered as asked, stated before building:** history cannot appear while an owner *composes* an invite, because the invitee has not responded and showing it there would rebuild exactly the lookup oracle ADR-053 blocks. Owners request instead (`/request`), and the tenant answers; a request cannot re-open a refusal.
- **Verified:** 19 new tests pass (`test:pure` now 411 passing); `check:architecture`, `vite build` and branding clean. **Not verified against a live database** — migration 065 needs one real run — and **not driven in a browser**. Pre-existing and untouched: the same 2 `agreement-requirement.test.ts` failures, the `check:invariants` false positive on `validated.error.issues[0]`, and 2 `tsc` errors in `TenantDetailPage.tsx` confirmed present on a clean tree.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-075|ADR-075]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Database]], [[APIs]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-15 — The portable Stayo profile: fill it in once, before you enquire anywhere
- **Added** `profile_identity` (1:1 with `profiles`), `identity_documents` (the vault) and `identity_document_shares` (per-hostel access *and* per-hostel verdict) — migration `064_portable_profile.sql`. Nothing dropped, nothing altered, no backfill in the migration. See [[Database]].
- **Added** `/discover/profile/details`, the profile editor, reachable with **no hostel involved**. That is the requirement, stated by the owner: a tenant completes their details before enquiring, and onboarding then reads them as defaults instead of asking again.
- **Added** `GET|PATCH /api/profile/identity`, `GET|POST /api/profile/documents`, `POST|DELETE /api/profile/documents/shares`, `GET /api/owner/document-shares`, `PATCH /api/owner/document-shares/[shareId]/verdict`, `GET /api/tenants/me/onboarding-prefill`. See [[APIs]].
- **Added** `npm run backfill:profile-identity` — **dry-run by default**. A person with two past tenancies can hold two conflicting `college_name` values and neither is wrong, so the script reports every conflict it resolved and every value it discarded rather than an `UPDATE` deciding silently inside a migration. `tenants` keeps its columns, so the losing values stay inspectable.
- **Changed — owner workflow.** Person-level fields (`college_name`, `date_of_birth`, `gender`, `photo_url`, `roll_number`, `course`, `year_of_study`, `branch`, `section`, `office_*`, `job_role`, `profile_type`) moved from Category A to **Category B** in `field-classification.ts`: owners now *propose* these and the tenant approves. Without it, one owner would silently rewrite a person's record at a hostel they have nothing to do with. Raised as a real cost and approved before building.
- **Changed** `POST /api/tenants/me/complete-profile` writes `profile_identity` inside the same transaction as the tenancy row, so someone who never opened the profile screen still gets their *second* hostel prefilled.
- **Documents are uploaded once, verified per hostel.** An owner sees a document only through a live share for a hostel they own; Owner A's verification never becomes Owner B's. This settles a contradiction in the design source, whose verify screen promises "shared only with the owner you enquire to" while its profile screen promises "verified once, reuse anywhere". See [[Business-Rules]].
- **Fixed:** `agreement-generation-service.ts` read `permanent_address`, `tenant_email` and `tenant_phone` **live** at render time. Survivable while identity was frozen per tenancy; with an editable profile it meant regenerating an old agreement could print an address the signatory never agreed to. Now snapshotted into `content_snapshot` at signing, snapshot-first on read, with the old live read kept as a fallback for agreements signed earlier.
- **Fixed before it shipped:** the tenancy fallback initially used `orderBy: { status: 'asc' }`, which prefers `INVITED` over `ACTIVE` because that is the order `TenantStatus` declares them — it would have taken identity from a tenancy the person never activated. Precedence is now explicit and shared with the backfill script, pinned by a test.
- **Out of scope, confirmed with the owner:** parent/guardian logins. Guardian remains fields on the tenant's own profile.
- **Verified:** 30 new tests pass (46 more running in `test:pure` overall, now 392 passing); `check:architecture`, `vite build` and the branding check clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean across all new files. The new tests were added to `vitest.pure.config.ts` rather than the default config, because `.env.test` points at the same Supabase project as dev. **Not verified against a live database** — migration 064 and the backfill need one real run — and **not driven in a browser**. Pre-existing and untouched: the same 2 `agreement-requirement.test.ts` failures and the `check:invariants` hit on `app/api/tenants/me/*`, the latter a false positive on `validated.error.issues[0]`.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-074|ADR-074]], [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Business-Rules]].

### 2026-08-15 — Stayo Discover: the tenant half of `/` now leads somewhere
- **Added** `/discover` — a public hostel marketplace with its own 4-tab shell (Explore / Saved / Enquiries / Profile), ported from `Stayo Discover.dc.html`. Browse, search, filter and listing detail are public and signed-out-reachable; Saved, Enquiries and Profile are the seeker's own.
- **Added** `GET /api/discover/hostels`, `GET /api/discover/hostels/[slug]`, `GET|POST /api/discover/enquiries`, `GET /api/discover/enquiries/[id]`, `GET|POST /api/discover/saved`, `DELETE /api/discover/saved/[hostelId]`. See [[APIs]].
- **Added** migration `063_discovery_phase_a.sql` — `visitor_leads.seeker_profile_id` (nullable FK) and the `saved_hostels` table. Both additive. See [[Database]].
- **Changed** `WelcomePage`'s tenant panel: the `aria-disabled` "Browse hostels" pill labelled *Coming soon* is now a real commit into `/discover`. The file's header comment, which documented the dead end as deliberate, is updated.
- **Enquiries are `visitor_leads` rows** (source `DISCOVER`), landing in the owner's existing inbox, scoring and funnel — no parallel entity, no second place for an owner to look. Approval routes into the pre-existing `convert-to-invitation`, so an accepted enquiry becomes a real tenant invitation feeding onboarding.
- **No new signup was built.** `authService.selfSignUpTenant()` already created precisely this account and already described itself as a marketplace account; the phase built the screen against it. An email-OTP path was designed and discarded — see [[Decisions#ADR-073|ADR-073]] point 8.
- **Nothing is faked.** Star ratings, review counts, amenity grids and distance-to-campus are omitted (the columns do not exist) rather than seeded; unpriced rooms read "Price on request", never "₹0". Listings look thinner than the prototype on purpose.
- **Fixed, found by the compiler while building:** `getListing()` and `createEnquiry()` spread the shared visibility predicate *after* `public_slug`, so its own `public_slug: { not: null }` overwrote the slug being looked up — every detail request would have matched an arbitrary listed hostel. Pinned by a regression test.
- **Fixed:** `GET /api/auth/me` set `is_profile_completed` only inside its `if (tenant)` branch, so a TENANT with no tenancy — every seeker — got it back `undefined` and any guard reading it bounced them to `/complete-profile` on reload. See [[Bugs]].
- **Scope:** this is phase A of four. The portable profile (B), owner listing content (C) and reviews (D) are separate and unbuilt; identity and documents still hang off `tenants`, which is one row per tenancy, so nothing is portable yet.
- **Verified:** 16 new backend tests pass; `check:architecture`, full `vite build` and the branding check are clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean across all new files. **Not verified against a live database** — the migration and both tables need one real run — and **not driven in a browser**. Pre-existing and untouched: 2 failures in `agreement-requirement.test.ts` and 1 `check:invariants` failure in `app/api/tenants/me/*`, both confirmed present on a clean tree.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-073|ADR-073]], [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-15 — All five onboarding stages matched to the design screenshots
- Owner-supplied screenshots of the Claude Design rendering, stage by stage, with the instruction that Welcome / Identity / Agreement / Account / Move In each match exactly.
- **Identity** — the empty profile-photo state is the design's solid terracotta disc (it was a cream well with a camera glyph in it); the guardian *lock / Modify* card is gone in favour of the design's plain `GUARDIAN FULL NAME` input; `GUARDIAN MOBILE` loses the asterisk and the helper line beneath it, neither of which the design has. `isGuardianLocked` state removed from `ActivationPage` with its last consumer.
- **Agreement** — the five acknowledgements moved *inside* the agreement document as a closing numbered section ("7. Acknowledgements") instead of a second cream card beside it. The design has one document box; this restores that silhouette without dropping the consent. See below.
- **Move In** — the track now reads "0 steps left" on the last stage, matching the design literally, rather than the "Last step" wording introduced earlier as a tidier alternative.
- **Welcome** and **Account** needed no further change — Account was stripped to the design earlier the same day, Welcome already matched.
- **The acknowledgements were kept, and this is settled rather than deferred.** `activation-workflow-service.ts`'s `acceptRules()` validates all five server-side and writes a `tenant_policy_acceptances` row stamped with the tenant's IP, user agent and typed name. Removing the UI would not remove the requirement — it would post `{all: true}` for someone never shown the statements, fabricating a consent record. Folding them into the document was the way to satisfy the design's layout without that. Confirmed with the owner: when a hostel has `agreement_required: false` the backend blocks `RULES` *and* `AGREEMENT`, so nothing agreement-shaped — acknowledgements included — runs for those hostels at all.
- **Verified:** `check:architecture`, full `vite build` + branding check clean; `tsc --noEmit` unchanged at the flow's 5 pre-existing errors. **Not verified in a browser.**
- See [[Decisions#ADR-072|ADR-072]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-15 — Onboarding Step 4 stripped to the design, removing a card that claimed an unsigned agreement was signed
- Owner-reported with side-by-side screenshots: on a hostel with `agreement_required: false` the flow correctly showed **"Step 3 of 4"** with the Agreement node dropped from the track — and then Step 4 rendered an "Onboarding Verification Checklist" whose third row read **"3. Hostel Agreement Signed — Signed as 'Prakash'"**, above a "Signed Rental Agreement / Digitally Signed / View / Generating PDF…" card. Nothing had been signed; the agreement stage never ran.
- **Root cause:** neither surface was gated on a signature existing. The checklist row was hardcoded with a green tick and fell back to `ctx.profile.name` when `tenant_signature_name` was absent, so it rendered a *name* as if it were a signature. The preview card was gated on `ctx.agreement` merely existing — which it does even when signing isn't required — and printed the literal "Digitally Signed" whenever no signature name was present. See [[Bugs]].
- **Fix, and the wider point:** both were removed outright rather than re-gated. The design's Step 4 is the lock header plus one password card and nothing else — its own `checklist` array is defined but never rendered anywhere in the source. This reverses the standing "keep extra backend-useful surfaces, restyle them" policy for these two, by explicit user direction (the third time that policy has been overridden for this flow — see [[Decisions#ADR-070|ADR-070]]'s amendments). `AgreementPreviewModal` lost its only consumer and was deleted from `steps/shared.tsx`; the frozen `portal/pages/ActivateAccountPage.tsx` keeps its own local copy and is untouched. `PasswordActivateStep` no longer takes `ctx` or `accountPhone`.
- **Two more instances of the same bug class, found by auditing the other stages for it:** Step 1's body eyebrow was hardcoded `Step 1 of 5`, so it contradicted the header's "Step 1 of 4" on exactly the agreement-off hostels above — now derived from `agreement_required`. And the guardian card's "Synced and locked from agreement signing." predates [[Decisions#ADR-070|ADR-070]] putting Identity *before* Agreement, so it credited a step that hasn't run yet; the name comes from the owner's invitation, and the copy now says so.
- Also removed: the permanent filler line under Identity ("Address, academic, and work details can be completed later…"), which has no design counterpart. The transient "Draft restored from this device." / "Saving draft…" states remain.
- **Deliberately kept, and why:** the 5 rules acknowledgements on Step 3 (removing the UI would auto-accept a legal consent on the tenant's behalf — a consent question, not a design one), the 4th `ACADEMIC_YEARLY` billing option on Step 1 (a real selectable backend value; dropping it would remove a capability), and Step 4's activation progress bar (renders only while the real `ACTIVATE` call is in flight, which the design has no equivalent of because it has no async).
- **Verified:** `check:architecture`, full `vite build` + branding check clean; `tsc --noEmit` adds no new errors. **Not verified in a browser.**
- See [[Decisions#ADR-072|ADR-072]], [[Bugs]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-15 — Tenant onboarding re-synced against the approved `Stayo Onboarding.dc.html`, animations included
- User-directed: re-read the approved design straight from Claude Design project `3f2fbde6` (`Stayo Onboarding.dc.html`, plus its `image-slot.js` / `ios-frame.jsx` / `support.js` scaffolding, confirmed to be canvas plumbing and not product design) and diffed it line by line against the shipped flow. The flow was already a deliberate port; this pass closes the gaps that port left, treating the animations as part of the spec rather than decoration.
- **The primary action was below the fold on every step.** The design pins a glass action bar to the bottom of the flow over a `transparent → #F6F1EA` scrim; every step instead rendered its own bar inline at the end of its content. New `StepActionBar` + `PrimaryActionButton` (`steps/shared.tsx`) are used by all four steps, and `ActivationLayout` reserves the design's 108px of bottom padding. See [[Bugs]].
- **Keyframes were reachable only by accident.** Step bodies referenced `obFade`/`obUp` inline while the only file defining them was imported by the intro screen. Consolidated into one `onboarding.css` imported by both `ActivationIntroScreen` and `ActivationLayout`; `ActivationIntroScreen.css` and `ActivationProgress.css` deleted (they had each redefined the same walk cycle under different names). See [[Bugs]].
- **The journey-track avatar was missing half its behaviour.** Added the design's `bored` state — 11s idle and the tenant sits down and dozes (`obBreathe`/`obZzz`), waking on any pointer/key/wheel/touch or step change — plus `avatarLook()`'s gender variation (hair/top/arm re-skin, long hair, skirt), now genuinely available because ADR-070 moved Identity ahead of Agreement. The bob/breathe transform now animates something: the design applies it to a wrapper whose absolutely-positioned parts escape to the *grandparent*, so in the source the figure doesn't actually bob.
- **The signature pad is now the design's full-screen sheet** (`steps/SignatureSheet.tsx`): back tile / title / pencil badge top bar, name + relationship rows, a canvas that fills every remaining pixel with the dashed baseline and "✕" mark, a Clear pill, and a Cancel / Apply footer whose primary stays washed-out (`#D8B3A2`) until both a name and a scribble exist. Replaces the generic slate dialog that wrapped the owner-side `@shared/ui/inputs` `SignaturePad` (fullscreen/rotate controls, indigo confirm — none of it in this design). Strokes are flattened onto white on export, so the agreement PDF is unaffected.
- **Agreement step**: unsigned tenant row is the design's solid terracotta card with the `sigGlow` pulse and a nudging arrow (was a flat dashed card), signed states use the design's mint fills (`#F1FAF4`/`#DCEFE4`), the guardian row is offered to every tenant rather than only students (it is marked Optional in the design; only the guardian *contact* fields on Identity are student-mandatory), and the submit button regains its circled-check icon.
- **Intro scene**: removed a roof that isn't in the design, added the cyclist's wheel spokes, the suitcase's wheels, the 5th star and the design's exact star/cloud positions, dropped a greeting-pill avatar badge the design doesn't have, and replaced the single beep on the sound toggle with the design's real ambient loop — three door thunks and a reception bell at fixed offsets into the 14s cycle plus footstep ticks, phase-locked to the live door animation's `currentTime`.
- **Flow chrome**: the time-of-day sky now carries its stars and clouds through to the wizard (only the gradient did before), and the sun pulses on the design's `obGlow 3s` rather than Tailwind's `animate-pulse`.
- **Identity**: inline "N more digits to verify" hint, inline OTP failure row under the code box (red border + message, instead of only the top banner), and the back tile matches the design's 44px cream tile with the design's arrow.
- **Deliberate departures from the design, all pre-existing policy:** 6-digit OTP (backend), `<select>`s and `<input type="date">` in place of tap-to-cycle rows, the 5 rules acknowledgements and the verification checklist the design's mockup has no UI for, 8-character password minimum, and "Last step" instead of the design's literal "0 steps left". The design's `activated` overlay is dead state in the source (nothing ever sets it) and is not ported.
- **Verified:** `check:architecture` passes, full `vite build` + branding check succeed, `tsc --noEmit` introduces no new errors in the touched files (the flow's 3 pre-existing ones are untouched and unrelated). **Not verified in a browser** — no visual confirmation of the sticky bar against a real keyboard, the signature canvas on touch, or the audio loop's phase lock.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-072|ADR-072]], [[Features]], [[Bugs]], [[Frontend]].

### 2026-08-15 — Add Hostel drops the animated building for the standard owner grid
- User-directed: the illustrated `HostelScene` behind the Add Hostel form (a building that gained a storey per floor added, grew windows as rooms appeared, lit them once configured) is replaced by the graph-paper grid every other owner surface uses — `#EBDCCF` 1px lines at 52px, copied verbatim from `OwnerAppShell` so it matches `TenantDetailPage` and `HostelDrilldownLayout` exactly rather than being re-derived.
