- Removed with it: the gradient scrim that existed only to keep the form legible over the illustration, the `scene` memo, the `activeFurnished` computation feeding it, three imports, and the `z-[1]`/`z-[2]` stacking layers that no longer had anything to stack against.
- **`builderScene.ts` + `builderScene.test.ts` deleted** (10 tests, suite 694 → 684). Checked before removing: the module's only remaining reference was its own test file — it existed solely to map builder stage/floor state onto the scene. `HostelScene` is untouched and still live; the onboarding wizard renders it.
- The 2026-08-12 narrow-viewport crop bug in [[Bugs]] was filed against this screen and is now moot here; its fix still matters for onboarding, which is `HostelScene`'s only caller now.
- **Verified:** 684 tests pass, `vite build` + `check:architecture` + branding check clean, `tsc --noEmit` clean across the touched file. **Not verified in a browser.**
- See [[Features#Add Hostel — the owner builds their property, floor by floor|Features]], [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant activation: removed two stale "emergency contact required" gates that blocked every activation
- Owner-reported: activation flagged emergency contact as required despite it being explicitly dropped from the Identity screen earlier the same day (ADR-070 amendment). Root cause: that amendment only updated the Identity step's own submit validation (`saveProfile()`); `computeState()`'s tier-1-required list and `activate()`'s own hard check in `activation-workflow-service.ts` still required `tenant.phone_3`, which nothing populates anymore — so `profile_completed` could never be true and final activation always threw `VALIDATION_ERROR: Emergency contact phone number is required`, for every tenant.
- Fix: `phone_3` removed from `missingTier1` in `computeState()` (already tracked as tier-3/optional) and the hard throw in `activate()` deleted. Emergency contact is now optional at every stage, matching the Identity screen's actual behavior. See [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant activation: journey-track avatar now follows the Welcome→Identity local phase switch
- Owner-reported bug (screenshot): the wizard's progress track (step pips + bobbing avatar) stayed on "Welcome" after continuing into the Identity screen. `WelcomeIdentityStep.tsx` switches Welcome→Identity via local component state only (no backend call until submit), which the progress track — keyed purely off the backend `activation_state.current_step` — had no visibility into.
- Fix: `localPhase` state lifted from `WelcomeIdentityStep.tsx` up into `ActivationPage.tsx`; the page now overrides the progress track's visual step to `PROFILE` whenever `activeStep === 'ACCOUNT' && localPhase === 'identity'`. See [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-14 — `/` is now an audience chooser; the owner marketing page moved to `/owners`
- **The root was pitching at the wrong half of its visitors.** Stayo serves students looking for a bed and owners looking for software, and `/` was `LandingPage` — occupancy dashboards, automated late fees, WhatsApp rent bots. A student arriving from a search or a shared link landed in all of it.
- **New `WelcomePage` at `/`**, ported from `Stayo Welcome.dc.html` (Claude Design project `3f2fbde6`): a full-viewport diagonal seam, tenant above / owner below, that slides 66/34 toward whichever side you hover or tap and expands that side's pitch. Built with `StayoMark`/`StayoWordmark` rather than the design's PNG uploads, so no new assets ship and the mark inherits `currentColor`; lucide icons in place of the design's inline SVG symbols. Keyframes live in a co-located `welcome.css`, prefixed `stayo-welcome-`.
- **`LandingPage` moved to `/owners`.** "Start free" hands off there. `/login` still renders it with the popup open — ADR-035 untouched.
- **Redirects and logo links resorted**, because `/` stopped being a destination and became a fork. The rule: **logos mean "front door" → `/`; content CTAs and route guards → `/owners`.** `LandingPage`'s own logo (an in-page `#top` anchor until now, since this page *was* `/`), `MarketingLayout` and `CompanyPage` → `/`. The owner-lead dead-ends (`EnquiryStatusPage`, `OwnerLeadInvitePage`), mid-flow onboarding chrome (`JourneyShell`, `OwnerOnboardingWizard`), `RequireMockOwnerJourney` and `LandingPage`'s `/login`-modal-close redirect → `/owners`. Auth failures (`ProtectedRoute` with no session, `ProtectedTenantRoute`) → `/login`, since asking someone whose session just expired which audience they belong to instead of letting them sign back in loses the thread. Left at `/` on purpose: the 404 CTA, the receipt-verification shell (tenant-facing), and `ProtectedRoute`'s *wrong-role* branch — there we genuinely don't know which surface they want, and the chooser is that question.
- **Picking a side is reversible, and `/` renders for everyone.** A first pass hard-redirected signed-in owners from `/` to `/owners`; that was **caught in review and reverted the same day** — it made the tenant side permanently unreachable for the one person who most needs it (an owner can't browse hostel listings if the front door ejects them) and it defeated the logo link, since a signed-in owner clicking it bounced straight back. The session now changes the owner CTA instead of removing the choice: "Go to dashboard" → `/owner/home` for an owner with a hostel, "Start free" → `/owners` otherwise.
- **"Start free" now lands you *in* the enquiry, not in front of a popup asking if you're an owner.** `/owners` carries an `OwnerEnquiryPrompt` ("Are you a hostel owner?") that fires on scroll — the exact question you answer by tapping the owner panel. The welcome screen now hands `{ declaredOwnerIntent: true }` to `/owners` via router state; `LandingPage` opens `HostelLeadModal` on the first paint and the prompt is suppressed. New optional `declaredOwnerIntent` field on `EnquiryPromptInput`, checked ahead of every other rule (the question is answered, not badly timed), +3 tests including one pinning absent ≡ `false` so existing callers are provably unaffected. The state is consumed with a `replace` navigation so history back/forward can't re-open the conversation.
- **The tenant panel is deliberately inert**: it expands and reads its pitch, but "Browse hostels" is a non-navigating `aria-disabled` pill labelled "Coming soon". The hostel-browsing page behind it (cluster listings) is designed later; there is no separate tenant marketing page planned. Its own way back to `/` gets designed with it.
- **Two deliberate deviations from the design source.** Splash cut 2.65s → 1.6s, shown once per browser session and skipped under `prefers-reduced-motion`. The commit overlay's rotating ring became `StayoLoader` — rotating spinners are absent app-wide by design.
- **SEO cost, stated rather than absorbed:** `/` is no longer the crawlable owner-acquisition page and its accumulated ranking doesn't transfer to `/owners` cleanly. `sitemap.xml` gives `/owners` the 1.0 and demotes `/` to 0.7; no 301/canonical was added, since `/` is a genuinely distinct page now, not a duplicate.
- **Verified:** `check:architecture` passes, 694 frontend tests pass (3 new, pure), full `vite build` + branding check succeed, `tsc --noEmit` clean across every touched file (the repo's pre-existing errors elsewhere are unchanged). **Not verified in a browser** — no visual confirmation of the seam geometry, the splash timing, or the panel-expand transitions on a real viewport.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-071|ADR-071]], [[Features]], [[Frontend]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant activation wizard: Identity now precedes Agreement (ADR-070)
- A second design-source folder, `stayo onbaording/` (distinct from `Stayo SaaS redesign/`, used for the fix above), turned out to be the one the wizard should have matched — user pointed this out directly. Diffing the two `Stayo Onboarding.dc.html` files showed a real flow change: Welcome trims to Room Allocation + Billing Cycle only; mobile-OTP/Gmail verification and a new circular profile-photo upload move into a combined Identity screen that now comes *before* Agreement.
- **Backend (`apps/backend/src/services/tenants/`)**: confirmed via full read that Agreement-before-Profile was a hard server-side gate (`activation-workflow-service.ts`'s `assertTransition()`, HTTP 409 `INVALID_TRANSITION`), not a UI convention, and confirmed no data dependency blocks reversing it either direction. `agreement-requirement.ts`'s `ALL_ACTIVATION_STEPS` reordered to `ACCOUNT → RULES → PROFILE → AGREEMENT → ACTIVATE`; `assertTransition()`/`blockedSteps()` swapped symmetrically.
- **Frontend**: `AccountStep.tsx` + `ProfileStep.tsx` merged into one new `WelcomeIdentityStep.tsx` (originals deleted) — renders for either the `ACCOUNT` or `PROFILE` backend step, the same one-component-two-backend-steps pattern `AgreementStep.tsx` already used for `RULES`+`AGREEMENT`: a local `welcome`/`identity` UI phase (no backend call) lets the tenant view Room+Billing before Identity, then submitting Identity chains `ACCOUNT` then `PROFILE` behind one tap. `ActivationProgress.tsx`'s node order swapped to match (`Welcome → Identity → Agreement → Account → Move In`). Back-button targets in `AgreementStep.tsx`/`PasswordActivateStep.tsx` relabeled to match the new order.
- **Also added, per user approval**: a dual-brand header (Stayo icon × hostel logo/initials badge, "You're joining {hostel}") on the intro screen and wizard chrome, using the real `stayo-icon.png` asset (copied to `apps/frontend/public/`) and `ctx.hostel.logo_url`/`name`; decorative "Checking in…/Checking out…" speech bubbles on the intro building scene.
- All backend-required fields the newer design mockup doesn't show (guardian OTP, profile-type selector, emergency contact) were kept, restyled into the merged screen. No change to OTP flows, guardian verification, signature capture, or activation submission logic beyond the step-order wiring itself.
- **Verified:** backend `check:invariants`/`check:activation-invariants` clean (pre-existing, unrelated dev-data failures noted — not caused by this change); no `.env.test`/test DB configured in this environment, so `activation-workflow-service`'s vitest suite could not be run here. `npm run build` clean. Intro → Welcome → Identity confirmed live via headless-Chromium screenshots against a real dev-DB pending invitation, zero console/API errors.
- **Same-day amendment**, after comparing a real design-source screenshot: dropped the Profile Type selector (safe — `profile_type` already defaults to `STUDENT` and is editable later from the tenant portal), the Guardian Relation dropdown (required relaxing `saveProfile()`'s backend validation — relation is no longer required at the Profile step, only name + verified phone, since nothing before Agreement collects it anymore), and the 4th "Prefer not to say" gender option — none of these appear in the design's Identity screen. See [[Decisions#ADR-070|ADR-070]] for the full reasoning.
- **Second same-day amendment**: Emergency Contact removed from the Identity screen outright, by explicit instruction. `saveProfile()`'s `emergencyPhone` requirement relaxed the same way — no longer required to complete Profile, still format-validated if supplied. No relocation needed (unlike Profile Type/Guardian Relation) since emergency contact was never asked anywhere else in the flow; collect it later from the tenant portal profile if needed.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-070|ADR-070]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant activation wizard: Steps 1/3/4/5 brought to exact design fidelity
- User reported the activation wizard's intro animation was right, but the card sections/text/placeholders elsewhere looked different from `Stayo Onboarding.dc.html`. Investigation confirmed: the 2026-08-12 redesign (see [[Frontend]] and the tracker's Phase 5) fully matched the design's inline hex colors/cards/copy for the intro splash, the wizard chrome, and Step 2 (`AgreementStep.tsx`) only — Steps 1, 3, 4, and 5 had been left on the pre-redesign generic Tailwind semantic-token look, with several labels/placeholders drifted from the design's copy.
- Rebuilt `AccountStep.tsx` (Step 1), `ProfileStep.tsx` (Step 3), `PasswordActivateStep.tsx` (Step 4), and `WelcomeSummaryStep.tsx` (Step 5) to the same inline-hex-style, exact-copy treatment `AgreementStep.tsx` already used — read directly from the `.dc.html` source, not approximated. Notable fixes: Step 1's Room Allocation tiles now read `Room`/`Rent`/`Joins` (were "Monthly Rent"/"Joining Date"), the mobile field gained the design's inline `+91` prefix, and the mobile placeholder became `10-digit mobile number`; Step 3's fields moved onto the cream/white card language with uppercase micro-labels; Step 4's password/checklist/agreement cards were reskinned; Step 5 gained the design's "Download signed agreement" row (previously missing) and its dark `#1B1714` "Enter Stayo" CTA (was the primary accent color).
- All backend-required fields the design mockup doesn't show at all (guardian OTP verification, profile-type selector, emergency contact, photo upload, the onboarding verification checklist, the agreement-preview/download card) were kept — per explicit user decision — restyled into the design's card language rather than removed, the same treatment `AgreementStep.tsx` already gave its own extra rules-acknowledgement checklist.
- No props, state, or business-logic changes anywhere — OTP flows, guardian verification, profile-draft autosave, photo upload, signature capture, and activation submission are all unchanged. Presentation-only.
- **Verified:** `npm run build` clean (`check:architecture` + `vite build` + branding check). Intro screen and Step 1 confirmed live via a headless-Chromium screenshot against a real dev-DB pending invitation. Steps 3–5 verified by direct comparison against the design source only — reaching them live requires a real OTP delivered via WhatsApp/SMS in this dev environment, and no dev-bypass code exists.
- See [[Frontend]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-14 — Production outage: every authenticated request 500'd after the tenant-profile deploy; `blood_group` removed
- **Symptom:** owners couldn't get past the marketing page. `GET /api/auth/me`, `GET /api/owner/hostels` and `POST /api/auth/activity` all returned **500** in the browser, while the same routes returned a clean **401** unauthenticated and `/api/health` reported `database: connected` with the canonical Supabase `project_ref`.
- **Cause:** the deploy (`ea0cccb`, merge of `7682562`) shipped four new `tenants` columns whose migrations were never run against the production database — nothing in the pipeline applies them. `getSession()` → `resolveSupabaseSession()` → `getActiveTenancy()` issues a `tenants.findMany` with **no `select`**, so Prisma asked for columns Postgres didn't have on *every authenticated request, for every role*. Full detail in [[Bugs]].
- **`blood_group` is gone rather than added back** (migration `20260814180000_drop_tenant_blood_group`, `DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS` since the databases were in different states): it came from `Stayo Tenant.dc.html`'s Personal-information card, not from anything StayO operationally needs. Removed from `schema.prisma`, `TenantProfileUpdateSchema`, `tenant-service`'s `tenantFields` allowlist, `getTenantPortalProfile()`, and the tenant Profile tab's view + edit configs. `nationality`, `pan_number` and `expected_completion_date` stay.
- **The remaining three columns (`nationality`, `pan_number`, `expected_completion_date`) were then applied to production by hand**, via the Supabase SQL editor against the canonical `ap-south-1` project — same-day, once the owner confirmed the outage from a DevTools screenshot. See [[Bugs]] for the exact SQL. Login/`/api/auth/me`/`/api/owner/hostels` confirmed working afterward.
- **Verified:** 691 frontend tests and 348 pure backend tests pass; `tsc --noEmit` clean of anything touching these files (pre-existing errors in `src/portal` and implicit-`any` noise unchanged). **The production database state itself was not verifiable from the repo** during the code-fix step — the local `.env` points at a different host than the canonical project — but was confirmed fixed once the owner applied the migration directly and logged in successfully.
- See [[Database]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-13 — Errors now say why, and what to do next
- **Every error surface read the same way:** `error?.response?.data?.error?.message || 'fallback'` — one sentence, in a toast, written across **137 call sites**. It said *what* failed, never *why* or *what to do*, each site invented its own wording, and the backend's `code` was discarded everywhere. Only **3** places in the entire UI branched on it.
- **New `shared/errors/`**: `resolveError(error, context)` turns a failure into `{ title, why, nextStep, severity, action?, code }`. The backend is unchanged — it already emits `code` + `metadata`; sentences live in the UI that renders them.
- **Severity picks the vessel, once:** `recoverable` → toast, `needs-step` → `<ErrorNotice>` inline where the user was working, `blocking` → `<ErrorDialog>`. A next step shown in a toast that self-dismisses after 2.4 s is not guidance — the user who looks away cannot get it back.
- **Generic codes get meaning from context, without a backend sweep.** `FORBIDDEN` is emitted 334 times and `VALIDATION_ERROR` 240; the call site names its flow, so `FORBIDDEN` during invite reads "You can't invite to that room" while during payment it reads "You can't record that payment".
- **The unknown case still guides.** An unmapped code keeps the server's message as the title (it is the most accurate description available) but never as the *guidance*, since backend messages are written for developers. Every branch ends with something to try, and **always prints the code** — "Something went wrong" tells the user nothing and support less.
- **Metadata interpolation** makes guidance specific ("Room 101 already exists") with no prose in the backend; a placeholder with no value is dropped rather than rendered raw.
- **Migrated the flow we had just proved was confusing** — the tenant activation wizard's five error paths, including the guardian OTP failure that read only "Failed to send OTP". The remaining call sites are untouched and keep working; they will be migrated per-flow rather than in one sweep.
- **Verified:** 691 frontend tests pass (17 new, pure), `vite build` succeeds, no new type errors (4 pre-existing in `ActivationPage` are another session's and unchanged). **Not verified in a browser.**
- See [[Frontend]].

### 2026-08-14 — Fixed a top-of-page dead-space gap on Room/Food/Profile tabs
- User-reported screenshots showed `My Room`/`My Menu`/`Profile` headers sitting well below the top of the screen, exposing a chunk of the shell's background grid pattern above them — Home and Money didn't have this gap.
- Cause: `Stayo Tenant.dc.html`'s source reserves 60px of top padding on every screen's header to simulate a phone status bar inside its fixed device frame; `TenantHomePage.tsx`/`TenantMoneyPage.tsx` had already been adapted to `pt-6` for the real frame-less app, but `TenantRoomPage.tsx`/`TenantFoodPage.tsx`/`TenantProfilePage.tsx` still carried the raw, unadapted `pt-[60px]` value.
- Fixed all three to `pt-6`, matching Home/Money. Verified live via Playwright. See [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant Profile detail screens: restored exact design + narrowed governance to phone/email only
- Same-day follow-up to "Tenant Profile: editable forms + a new owner-approval workflow" below, driven by 8 side-by-side screenshots (this app vs. `Stayo Tenant.dc.html`) showing the previous pass had replaced the design's read-only card layout with a generic always-open input form, and had a field-mapping bug in Emergency Contact.
- **View/edit toggle restores the exact design.** `ProfileEditScreen.tsx` now defaults to a read-only card view (label/value rows, a person-card for Emergency Contact, a "Verified" pill on Personal Information) — reusing `DetailScreen.tsx`'s row-rendering visuals, factored out into a new shared `Section.tsx` so both stay pixel-identical. Tapping the screen's own design-copy bottom button ("Request a correction" / "Update contact details" / "Update emergency contact" / "Request an update") switches to the editable form *inside the same card layout*; saving returns to the read-only view.
- **Governance narrowed to phone + email only** — a further correction to the same-day rule below: date of birth and address (briefly governed earlier the same day) are back to direct-editable, matching every other field. `GOVERNED_FIELDS` in `tenant-service.ts`, the `/profile-requests` routes, `GOVERNED_PROFILE_FIELDS` in `useTenantProfile.ts`, and `OwnerProfileRequestsPage.tsx`'s field-label map all updated to match.
- **Emergency Contact field-mapping bug fixed**: was editing `profile.emergency_contact` as a "Contact name" text field — that column is actually a phone number synced with `tenants.phone_3`. Now uses the real `guardian_name`/`guardian_relation` columns (already read elsewhere, never wired to self-edit) for "Contact person's name"/"Relationship", alongside `guardian_phone`/`phone_2` and the correctly-relabeled `phone_3` "Alternate phone".
- **Contact Details' address fields corrected** to `profile.city`/`state`/`pincode` (matching the design's City/State/PIN rows) instead of `tenants.permanent_address` (a different single-string field the screen shouldn't have used).
- **Aadhaar routes to the document-upload flow instead of a text field** — tapping it in edit mode calls the same `uploadDocument` mutation Documents already uses, masked-display value (`XXXX XXXX 1234`) sourced from `identification_documents`.
- **New `expected_completion_date` column** on `tenants` (migration `20260814140000_add_tenant_expected_completion_date`) for Academic Details' "Expected exit" row, alongside `guardian_name`/`guardian_relation` added to `updateTenantSelfProfile`'s direct-editable field list.
- **Found and fixed two real, pre-existing bugs while verifying live** (see [[Bugs]] for full detail): (1) `TenantProfileUpdateSchema` was silently `undefined` at runtime for every tenant-profile PATCH route — a stale re-export in `lib/validators/index.ts` pointed at a directory that never had this schema, so direct-field saves have likely never actually worked before today; fixed by moving the schema to the file the import chain really resolves to. (2) `getTenantPortalProfile()` never returned `blood_group`/`nationality`/`pan_number`/`guardian_name`/`guardian_relation`/`guardian_phone`/`expected_completion_date` in its response — saves succeeded but the view screens always showed "—" for these fields regardless.
- **Verified live end-to-end** (Playwright against the real dev backend): all 4 screens' view mode screenshot-matched the design exactly; direct fields (name, DOB, gender, blood group, nationality, PAN, guardian name/relation, academic fields including branch and expected exit) saved immediately with no reason prompt; changing email correctly required a reason, showed the pending-approval banner, and the change only took effect after the dev owner account approved it via `/owner/profile-requests`.
- **Known follow-up, documented not silently shipped**: Emergency Contact's Phone/Alternate phone fields inherit a pre-existing (unrelated to this session's governance work) OTP-verification requirement when changing an already-set guardian/emergency number — no OTP UI exists in this form yet, so such a save fails with a clear error toast rather than an OTP prompt. See [[Bugs]].
- See [[Business-Rules]], [[Bugs]], [[Database]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant Food tab: dropped meal rating, added a real empty state
- Removed the "How was today's lunch?" mood-rating card from `TenantFoodPage.tsx` (design-source content that wasn't wanted on this screen; it was toast-only/non-persisted regardless).
- Added an explicit empty state ("No menu published yet") for hostels that have never had a food schedule/voting-period/poll published at all — previously this case silently rendered a near-blank page (just header + footer). The already-correct "keep showing the last *published* month until the owner publishes a new one" fallback (`useTenantFoodSchedule`'s `currentMonthKey` = latest published month, not literally the calendar month) needed no change — confirmed via code read, not rebuilt.
- See [[Features#Tenant dashboard — pixel-fidelity rebuild (2026-08-14)|Features]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant Profile: editable forms + a new owner-approval workflow for governed fields
- Follow-up to the same-day pixel-fidelity rebuild below, driven by user feedback comparing the rebuilt Profile tab against the design screenshots.
- **"Your details" screens are now real editable forms**, not read-only rows (`ProfileEditScreen.tsx` + `profileEditConfigs.ts`, replacing the read-only `DetailScreen` path for these 4 keys — `profileDetailConfigs.ts` is now `@deprecated`). Personal Information gained Blood group, Nationality, and a "Government ID" (PAN number) section — new nullable columns on `tenants` (migration `20260814120000_add_tenant_identity_fields`; a same-day follow-up migration dropped a mistakenly-reintroduced `aadhaar_number` column — Aadhaar stays document-tracked only, per an existing, deliberate removal documented in `TenantProfileUpdateSchema`'s own code comment).
- **New governed-field business rule, specified directly by the user**: primary phone, personal email, permanent address, and date of birth now require owner approval before they take effect; every other field still saves immediately. Enforced in `updateTenantSelfProfile()` (400s if a governed field is present in a direct `PATCH`), not just at the frontend.
- **New backend routes**, deliberately *not* built on the existing `ChangeManagementFacade` (which is hardwired the opposite direction — owner proposes, tenant approves, and powers a live owner-side "Request Change" feature; extending it risked that feature): `POST/GET /api/tenants/me/profile-requests` (tenant submits/lists), `GET /api/owner/profile-requests` + `[id]/{approve,reject}` (owner reviews). Same `change_requests`/`change_request_events` tables, tagged `change_type: 'tenant_self_service_update'` to stay distinguishable from the facade's own rows.
- **New owner-side review screen**, `features/owner-profile-requests` → `/owner/profile-requests` (no owner UI existed for this direction before), linked from the owner's Alerts → Requests tab when any are pending.
- **Documents: "Upload" no longer navigates away.** The missing-document rows now have a real inline `<input type="file">` (upload-icon pill, per explicit request) instead of routing to the frozen, differently-themed `TenantProfilePortalPage` — that navigation was the user-reported "this is [a different-looking page] and it should not show like that."
- **Verified live end-to-end** (not just build): submitted a real governed-field change as the dev test tenant → confirmed the pending banner → approved as the dev test owner via the new review screen → confirmed via direct DB read that `tenants.permanent_address` was actually updated and the `change_requests` row reached `APPLIED`.
- Also fixed the same `findUnique({ where: { profile_id } })` bug (see below) in `lib/auth/resolve-operational-scope.ts`'s `resolveTenantScope()`, needed by the new tenant-facing routes.
- See [[Features#Tenant Profile — editable forms + owner-approval for governed fields (2026-08-14, same day as the pixel-fidelity rebuild above)|Features]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Decisions]] ADR-069, [[Database]], [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-14 — Tenant dashboard rebuilt to match `Stayo Tenant.dc.html` exactly
- All 5 tenant tabs (Home/Money/Room/Food/Profile) — already real and backend-wired from an earlier undocumented pass (2026-07-26/27) — rebuilt screen-by-screen against the design source, by explicit user choice (rebuild over audit-and-patch; existing feature-hook data layer kept since it already called the correct endpoints).
- **New shared overlay system** (`platforms/tenant/components/overlays/`): `DetailScreen`/`FormPanel`/`SuccessPanel` + a `useOverlayStack` push/pop hook, config-driven (matching the design source's own `DETAIL`/`FORM` map architecture) — replaces one-off screens for the ~20 Room/Profile drill-ins and 8 request-form flows. `TenantRoomPage`'s service requests now go through `FormPanel` instead of the old bottom-sheet `ServiceRequestSheet.tsx` (now `@deprecated`, zero remaining consumers).
- **New `useTenantProfile()` hook** (`features/tenant-profile/`) wraps `tenantPortalApi.getMyProfile()` — same query key as the frozen `TenantProfilePortalPage`, so the two share cache. Powers the rebuilt Profile tab's hero/detail cards/documents, and Home/Room/Money's hostel-name header/footer text.
- **Complaints has no dedicated tab** (explicit user decision, matching the design) — folded into Home's quick-action/status card, Room's Maintenance section + 6 service tiles, and Profile's Tickets list, all backed by `tenant_service_requests`.
- **Payment-standing tier copy rewritten** to match the real score semantics (starts at `EXCELLENT`, only degrades) rather than the design mockup's specific "new tenant builds up" demo framing, which would have been actively misleading for a real tenant. See [[Decisions]].
- **Design tokens added**: `--success-bg`/`--warning-bg`/`--info-bg`/`--destructive-bg`/`--research*` in `product.css`, registered as Tailwind utilities in `stayo-theme.css`'s `@theme inline` block; new `stayo-panel-slide-in`/`stayo-hero-up-in`/`stayo-ring-pulse`/`stayo-accordion-reveal` keyframe utility classes.
- **`TenantAppShell`** gained the same `sm:max-w-[480px]` desktop-centering treatment `OwnerAppShell` already had.
- **`TenantHelpPage`**'s dead `fetch('/api/tenant/requests')` form (hit a nonexistent endpoint, used `alert()`) removed — replaced with a deep link into Room's real, working service-request flow.
- **Fixed a real, pre-existing bug across 6 backend files** (not introduced by this pass, found while verifying live): `prisma.tenants.findUnique({ where: { profile_id } })` is invalid — `profile_id` isn't a unique column — and 500'd every request. Fixed the 8 occurrences reachable from the tenant dashboard (`tenants/me/{room,billing-frequency,payments/history,documents,photo,complete-profile,onboarding-settings}`, `payments/{create-intent,verify}`) to use `liveTenancyWhere()` + `findFirst`. Several more occurrences remain elsewhere in the payments/tenancy surface, not reachable from this dashboard — see [[Bugs]].
- **Fixed a real positioning bug found live**: the overlay panels used `absolute` positioning relative to the scrolling page (blank/misaligned whenever the page had scrolled before opening one) instead of `fixed` to the viewport. Changed all four overlay components.
- **`PaySheet.tsx`**'s "Secured by PhonePe" text/doc-comment corrected to Razorpay (the only real payment provider per CLAUDE.md — stale copy-paste from an earlier state of the codebase).
- **Verified live** — a dev-DB test tenant (`tenant@teststayo.dev`) was repaired via `apps/backend/scripts/create-test-tenant.ts` (its auth/tenancy/allocation rows were incomplete) and walked through all 5 tabs plus drill-in/form/success overlays with Playwright against the real dev backend, not just `vite build`.
- See [[Features#Tenant dashboard — pixel-fidelity rebuild (2026-08-14)|Features]], [[Bugs]], [[Decisions]].

### 2026-08-13 — Step 2 (Agreement) rebuilt to match the design source exactly
- Per explicit direction after the chrome rebuild (below): pixel-exact colors/fonts/layout going forward, not semantic-token approximations. `steps/AgreementStep.tsx` fully rewritten reading colors, copy, and layout directly from `Stayo Onboarding.dc.html`'s Step 2 markup rather than the semantic-token styling ported from the legacy page.
- **Structural change, not just styling:** the design has Step 2 as *one* screen (agreement box + two signature rows), not the two sequential screens (a rules-acknowledgement screen, then a separate agreement screen) the previous version built. Merged into one screen — the 5-item acknowledgement checklist the backend requires (not shown in the design at all) now sits as a compact block between the agreement box and the signature rows, styled in the same cream/Inter language instead of a separate step. Submitting now signs both backend steps (`RULES` then `AGREEMENT`) in sequence behind one "Submit & sign contract" tap.
- **Signature rows rebuilt** from a 2-column grid of centered cards to the design's actual horizontal rows (icon left, title+badge+status middle, Sign/Add/Edit action right), with exact icon paths, colors, and badge copy ("Required"/"Optional") read from the source.
- Removed the "Full preview" button/modal trigger added in the first pass — not present in the design, dropped per the exact-matching direction.
- Cleaned up: `RuleIcon` (used by the old rule-category accordion, no longer rendered) removed from `steps/shared.tsx` as dead code.
- **Not verified in a live browser this pass** — reaching Step 2 requires completing Step 1's real phone OTP first (no dev-database write permission to fast-forward a test tenant's state was available this session — that action was correctly blocked and not worked around). Verified via `vite build` (clean, no regressions on the intro/Step 1 screens already confirmed live) and direct comparison against the design source's exact markup/CSS values.
- See [[Features]].

### 2026-08-12 — Tenant activation wizard redesigned to match `Stayo Onboarding.dc.html`
- **`/activate`, `/activate/:token`, `/invite/:token` now mount a new `platforms/tenant/onboarding/ActivationPage.tsx`** instead of the frozen `src/portal/pages/ActivateAccountPage.tsx` — the second (and final) slice of the `portal → platforms/tenant` extraction `ActivationLayout.tsx`'s own doc comment had flagged as pending.
- **Backend unchanged.** The existing `ACCOUNT → RULES → AGREEMENT → PROFILE → ACTIVATE` state machine and its `/api/tenants/activate*` routes were not touched — this is a frontend presentation/file-layout change, not a logic rewrite.
- **New Step 0 intro splash**, ported from the design source: time-of-day sky (day/dusk/night, tap-to-cycle), a pixel-art Stayo building with a swinging door, three looping actor sprites, and a sound toggle — with real room/rent/move-in data instead of the design's hardcoded placeholders.
- **New Step 5 celebration/summary screen** — the legacy page navigated straight into the tenant portal on activation success; it now shows a move-in summary first, and "Enter Stayo" performs the same session hand-off the legacy page already did.
- **Design ↔ backend step mapping**: `RULES`+`AGREEMENT` collapse into one visual "Review & Sign Agreement" screen (matching `ActivationProgress.tsx`'s pre-existing collapse); password + billing-cycle selection (the design's Step 4) fold into the real `ACTIVATE` step's payload, same as the legacy page.
- Almost all business logic — OTP send/verify, phone/duplicate validation, signature and photo upload, the profile-draft autosave, the activation-progress simulation, the post-activate Supabase session hand-off — carried over unchanged from `ActivateAccountPage.tsx`, which is now `@deprecated` (kept, not deleted, per the standing policy).
- **Not verified in a live browser this session** (no dev-server/database access available) — verified via `vite build` (clean) and `check:architecture` (one pre-existing, unrelated failure in `TenantHelpPage.tsx`).
- See [[Features]].

### 2026-08-13 — Fixed: new tenant activation wizard rendered in the wrong theme
- **User-reported, on first browser use: "it's not matching at all."** The `checking`/`invalid`/intro/post-activate summary screens in the new `ActivationPage.tsx` (above) rendered outside `<ThemeProvider theme="product">` — same bug class as `PendingActivationsPage`/`LandingPage` earlier in this changelog — so they fell back to the legacy unscoped `theme.css`/`fonts.css` instead of StayO tokens.
- **Fix 1:** wrapped all four in `<ThemeProvider theme="product">`.
- **Fix 2, found while verifying fix 1:** every new step file (`steps/*.tsx`, `ActivationIntroScreen.tsx`) had copied `bg-accent`/`text-accent`/`border-accent`/`ring-accent` from the legacy page, where `--accent` happened to be the old orange (`#F07B1D`) — close enough to look plausible while unthemed, but wrong once themed: the `product` theme's `--accent` is a muted tan, and `--primary` (`#b46a55`) is the real rust brand color. All 42 occurrences changed `accent` → `primary`.
- **Verified live** this time — Playwright against the real dev DB confirms the intro screen and Step 1 now render with correct StayO colors/fonts throughout, zero console errors.
- **Follow-up same day, second user-reported mismatch:** the theme/color fixes above were right, but the intro screen's proportions still didn't match — the building/actor scene sat too close to the room-ready card instead of the design's spacious open-sky gap. Root cause: the container was sized `min-h-[720px]`, but every absolute-positioned element in the scene (`bottom: 198px`, etc., copied verbatim from the design source) was authored against the design's actual `874px`-tall device frame. A shorter container compresses the same absolute offsets, eating the gap. Fixed by matching the container height to `874px`. Confirmed by rendering the actual design source file locally (not just reading its code) and comparing screenshots directly, side by side.
- **Follow-up same day, third user-reported issue:** a stray white rectangle appeared near the building's door mid-animation. Root cause: the door swings via a 3D `rotateY` transform past 90°, which exposes the element's back face — never given `backface-visibility: hidden`, so the browser rendered its unstyled back as a blank white patch during part of the swing. Fixed by adding `backface-visibility: hidden` to `.ob-door`. Verified by sampling multiple points across the full 14s animation loop with Playwright (door closed, mid-open, fully open, closing) — clean at every sampled frame.
- **Follow-up same day, fourth and largest user-reported mismatch: the wizard chrome itself didn't match.** User compared a real screenshot of the design source's Step 1 against the app and flagged it directly. `ActivationLayout.tsx`/`ActivationProgress.tsx` were reused as-is from an earlier pass on the assumption their doc comments ("already StayO-themed") meant they matched the design — they didn't, once actually compared: no Stayo "S" logo badge, no time-of-day gradient carrying from the intro into the wizard, and a 4-pill text tracker instead of the design's 5-icon journey track (Welcome/Agreement/Identity/Account/Move In, each with its own SVG icon and a bobbing avatar above the active node). Also confirmed from the same screenshot: the design collects **Billing Cycle in Step 1**, not Step 4 where it had been placed based on the legacy page's submit-timing rather than the design's actual field layout.
  - Rebuilt `ActivationProgress.tsx` to the design's exact 5-icon journey track (colors/icon paths read directly from the source's `journey` array), added `ActivationProgress.css` for the avatar bob.
  - Rebuilt `ActivationLayout.tsx`: time-of-day gradient (`flowGradient`, previously only used by the intro), Stayo logo badge, sun/moon toggle, glass card wrapping the journey track — and removed the enclosing white body card, since the design has step content floating directly on the gradient (only specific elements — room-summary tiles, form fields, the sticky action bar — are their own cards).
  - Extracted the shared color logic into a new `skyTheme.ts` (both the intro screen and the wizard chrome now derive from one `skyEnv()` instead of two independent copies).
  - Moved Billing Cycle from `PasswordActivateStep` to `AccountStep`, matching the design; still only submitted at the real `ACTIVATE` step.
  - Routed the post-activate summary screen (`WelcomeSummaryStep`) through the same `ActivationLayout` chrome instead of a standalone page, with a new virtual `MOVE_IN` journey-track step.
  - **Verified live** — Step 1 now matches the user's design-source screenshot closely: gradient, logo, journey track, room card, field order, and billing-cycle placement all line up. Steps 2–5 still unverified live (need a real OTP to progress past Step 1 in the browser).
- See [[Features]].

### 2026-08-12 — Admin can finally say no, and assign a plan without leaving the owner
- **The console could approve a hostel but never reject one.** `HostelVerificationStatus` had only `PENDING | VERIFIED`, so a hostel the admin had reviewed and declined was **indistinguishable from one nobody had opened** — it sat in the pending queue forever and the owner was never told anything. Added `REJECTED` plus `hostels.verification_note`, and `POST /api/platform-admin/hostels/[id]/reject-listing`.
- **A reason is required** (min 4 chars, enforced server-side), matching the rule owner-document review already follows: a rejection with no reason just makes the owner resubmit the same thing into the same queue. The note is surfaced back on the hostel row.
- **Rejection leaves the listing at `DRAFT`, not `SUSPENDED`.** Suspension means something was live and got pulled; using it for a hostel that never listed would misreport its history.
- **Plan assignment is now reachable.** `POST /platform-admin/hostels/[id]/subscription` already existed and was simply never surfaced — the owner profile now assigns or changes a plan per hostel (14-day trial on first assignment). Plans are fetched only when the picker is opened. Billing stays per-hostel until the owner-level migration.
- **Migration:** `20260812120000_hostel_rejection` appended to `SUPABASE_APPLY_ALL_PENDING.sql`, idempotent — **not yet applied to any database**.
- See [[Database]], [[APIs]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-12 — Admin: hostels move *inside* the owner, and approval happens there
- **Making Owners the primary list wasn't enough — the logic underneath was still hostel-first.** Hostels are now loaded as a child of the owner (`GET /api/platform-admin/owners/[id]`, returning that owner's hostels with full per-hostel figures, documents and recent activity) instead of being filtered out of a platform-wide roster. That matches the admin's actual model: a property only means something in the context of whose it is.
- **Approval moved to where the property is.** A hostel awaiting verification is approved from inside the owner profile, so the admin sees whose it is and what else that account needs, instead of being sent to a separate list to find it again.
- **Fixed two broken links introduced the same day.** The owner card and the approvals queue navigated to `/admin/hostels?owner=<id>` and `?verification=PENDING`, but neither the page nor its endpoint reads those params — both silently showed *every* hostel on the platform while looking correct. Serving hostels from the owner removes the need for either link rather than patching them, and a test now asserts no attention item routes into `/admin/hostels` at all.
- **Progressive disclosure:** the admin meets the owner first; hostels are introduced underneath them.
- See [[Features]], [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-12 — Admin becomes owner-first: Owners replaces Hostels, and the dashboard becomes a queue router
- **The admin app was built around the wrong entity.** Stayo's hierarchy is Stayo → Owner → Hostels, but the admin listed *properties* — so an owner running three hostels appeared as three unrelated rows with no way to see the business behind them, and there was no owner screen at all.
- **Owners is now the primary list** and the nav destination. Each row leads with the *reason* it needs attention rather than a wall of metrics, and the list is ordered worst-first, because with hundreds of owners the admin should never scroll to find the problem.
- **A new owner profile** — the screen that did not exist. Needs-you reasons each paired with the action that clears them, business figures, and a health panel. Deliberately observability and intervention only: it does not reproduce the owner's own app.
- **The dashboard's nine static counters become a router.** A "Needs you" list of queues (documents to verify, hostels awaiting approval, owners needing attention, new leads, unpaid invoices), each routing to the screen that clears it *pre-filtered*, above a four-figure platform snapshot. A queue at zero is not rendered at all — a permanent "0 pending" row trains people to skip the section that exists to be read.
- **Tenant counts left the dashboard.** "791 tenants" is not the admin's number to act on — tenants belong to owners — and it crowded out the figures that describe Stayo's own business (owners, live hostels, MRR, collected).
- **Health is derived only from signals that exist.** Activation, verification, listing and collections are real. **Engagement is shown as `untracked`, never as healthy**, because there is no login or session tracking anywhere; `activity_logs` records a last *action* written by only a handful of services, so treating its absence as inactivity would flag every owner who simply hasn't hit one of those paths. Support is likewise untracked — there is no ticketing backend.
- Backend adds `GET /api/platform-admin/owners` (raw per-owner signals, composed from the existing per-hostel aggregations) plus `documents_awaiting_review` and `owners_total` on the dashboard KPIs. Hostels stays routable so owner cards can deep-link into it.
- **Verified:** 673 frontend tests pass (29 new, pure), `vite build` succeeds, no new type errors. **Not verified in a browser.**
- See [[Features]], [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-12 — Action Center row is always complete: 1-3 without agreements, 1-3-1 with
- **Today's Revenue now fills whatever space the Renewal Agreements card leaves.** With agreements on it stays a full-width card below the row of three (1-3-1); with agreements off it becomes the third card *in* the row (1-3). Previously it always spanned the row, so switching agreements off left a visible gap beside Activate Tenants and Fill Vacant Beds — a 1-2-1.
- Purely presentational: no change to what any card reads or where it links. Agreement visibility is still the existing `showRenewalAgreements` derived from the hostel's `agreement_required` setting ([[Decisions#ADR-063|ADR-063]], [[Decisions#ADR-059|ADR-059]]).

### 2026-08-12 — New owners get a walkthrough: a self-ticking checklist plus a one-time spotlight
- **A "Getting started" card leads the dashboard for a new owner** ([[Decisions#ADR-067|ADR-067]]): set up your hostel → invite your first tenant → record your first payment. Every step is **derived from real account data**, so it ticks itself off no matter where the work happened, needs no stored step counter, and cannot disagree with the account it describes. It removes itself for good once all three are done.
- **A brand-new dashboard is all zeros**, which is why this is a checklist rather than a conventional tour — a spotlight over ₹0 cards points at things that do nothing yet.
- **Owner KYC is reported, not asked for.** `owner_documents.status` is admin-set and "can only be changed by an admin", so it appears as a status line — in review / verified / needs attention, with the reviewer's reason on a rejection — explicitly labelled as blocking nothing. Making it a checklist step would have created an item the owner could never tick.
- **A 3-stop spotlight runs once** on a genuinely empty account: the checklist, the Action Center, then search. Stops are anchored **by ref, not CSS selector**, so a rename cannot leave it dimming the screen while highlighting nothing. Esc skips, the page behind is inert, and it honours `prefers-reduced-motion`. No tour library was added.
- **A monthly-reset trap avoided:** the payment signal is *this month's* collection, so completion is latched one-way. Without it the card would reappear every 1st to tell a long-running hostel it had never taken rent.
- **Fixed during implementation:** anchor refs populate after the overlay's first render, and assigning a ref does not re-render — so the tour filtered every stop out and would have silently never appeared. It now arms a frame later.
- `FirstHostelCard` is deleted; its content is step one. `useOwnerDashboard` exposes `gettingStartedSignals` as raw numbers, since the formatted strings beside them can't be compared to zero.
- **Verified:** 644 frontend tests pass (19 new, pure), `vite build` succeeds, no new type errors. **Not verified in a browser.**
- See [[Decisions#ADR-067|ADR-067]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-12 — Hostel creation leaves onboarding: Add Hostel builds the property floor by floor
- **Onboarding drops from 12 screens to 4** ([[Decisions#ADR-066|ADR-066]]) — welcome, account, KYC, done. It no longer creates a hostel; a new owner lands on their real dashboard with one invitation to build. The root problem wasn't the number of questions: `OwnerOnboardingData` modelled an entire property as four scalars (`floors`, `roomsPerFloor`, `bedsPerRoom`, one `monthlyRent`) which were multiplied into a uniform grid, so a ground floor of three 4-sharing rooms and one 2-sharing room **could not be described at all**. The answers didn't matter, because the building was wrong on arrival and got fixed room-by-room afterwards anyway.
- **New builder at `/owner/hostels/new`** (resumable at `:id/build`): name it → raise the floors, with editable names → fill each floor from a room count plus a floor default, tapping any room that differs. "Same as this" clones a floor onto the next, and rent is remembered per sharing size, so a mixed 40-room building is a handful of decisions rather than 80.
- **The animation now tracks the real thing.** `OnboardingScene` read `step >= 7` to draw windows and `step >= 8` to light them, welding the artwork to one screen's step numbering. It moves to `shared/ui/brand/HostelScene` driven by hostel facts, so the builder reuses it: storeys rise as floors are added, windows appear as rooms do, and light up once those rooms are configured.
- **Rent is stated as a default, and no revenue is projected.** Verified in code: `rooms.base_rent` prefills the invite wizard via `auto_fill_room_rent`, the owner overrides it per tenant, and `tenants.monthly_rent` is the binding figure — two tenants in one room routinely pay different rents. The builder says so on screen and shows rooms and beds only, because a monthly total built on `base_rent` would be fiction.
- **Writes are incremental**, so "finish later" needs no local draft: the hostel row exists once named, each floor's rooms are saved on leaving it through the new `POST /api/floors/[id]/rooms` (one transaction, duplicate room numbers named before anything is written), and progress is **derived** — a floor with no rooms is one still to do; `total_capacity: 0` marks a hostel still being built.
- **The first hostel no longer asks for the password.** `POST /api/owner/hostels` gated every creation behind `CREATE_HOSTEL` step-up; it now applies only once the owner already has a hostel. The gate protects an account with data, and it sat directly in the path of the new flow on the emptiest possible account.
- **Lead-funnel regression caught and fixed in the same change:** `markLive` was invoked by the wizard's trailing `completeInvitation(token)`, so deleting the publish step would have stalled every lead-originated owner at `HOSTEL_CREATED` permanently. A new owner-scoped `markLiveForOwner` fires from the bulk-rooms route instead — triggered by the **first rooms landing**, not the hostel row, since under an incremental builder a named hostel with no rooms is not a live property.
- **Deleted:** 8 onboarding steps (`create`, `location`, `details`, `floors`, `rooms`, `beds`, `review`, `publish`), `generationGate`, `OnboardingScene`, the wizard's structure/pricing fields and `submitPublish`. `POST /api/owner/hostels/provision` loses its only caller and is marked deprecated in [[APIs]] rather than removed here.
- **Verified:** 587 frontend tests pass (44 new, pure), 331 backend pure tests pass, 7 new DB-backed tests pass against real Postgres, and `tsc --noEmit` introduces **no** new errors in either app. `check:architecture` still fails only on the pre-existing, unrelated `TenantHelpPage.tsx` raw-`fetch` violation. **Not verified in a browser.** Note: the local Prisma client was stale (missing `rooms.sort_order` from ADR-062) and needed `npm run prisma:generate` — worth doing after pulling this.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-066|ADR-066]], [[Features]], [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-12 — Fixed Activate Tenants queue rendering in the legacy pre-StayO theme
- **`/owner/tenants/activations` was rendering unbranded** — navy primary button, serif display font — instead of StayO's terracotta/Manrope look. Root cause: `PendingActivationsPage` is a sibling route of `<OwnerAppShell>` (not nested inside it), so it never inherited the shell's `<ThemeProvider theme="product">`, and fell back to `theme.css`'s unscoped legacy tokens — the pre-rebrand "Shri Adithya" theme, which intentionally still exists for not-yet-migrated screens.
- **Fix:** `PendingActivationsPage` now wraps itself in `<ThemeProvider theme="product">`, matching the pattern its sibling routes (`PendingVerificationsPage`, `TenantDetailPage`) already used for the same reason.
- Also fixed in the Action Center layout: Today's Revenue now always renders as a full-width card below the row (previously only when Renewal Agreements was also showing), and the Renewal Agreements card's visibility no longer causes a layout shift after page load (its settings fetch is now part of the same loading gate as the rest of the dashboard).
- **Follow-up same day:** the shared `WorkQueue` component (behind all four owner queues — Collection, Agreements, Vacancy, Activation) was missing the graph-paper background pattern used elsewhere in the StayO product theme (e.g. `PendingVerificationsPage`). Added once at the `WorkQueue` level so all four queues stay visually consistent rather than fixing it per-page.
- See [[Bugs]], [[Decisions#ADR-063|ADR-063]].

### 2026-08-11 — Rooms tab: vacant rooms open their sheet, and floors + rooms gain an explicit-Save "Reorder" mode
- **Bug fix: a vacant room's card now opens, like every other room.** Previously only occupied/reserved rooms responded to a tap — a vacant room's card did nothing except its separate "Assign" button, so there was no way to view a vacant room's sheet at all. `RoomRow` is now always tappable; `RoomSheetModal` shows an explicit "No active tenants" row when a room has none, instead of a silently empty list.
- **Floors can now be reordered.** New "Reorder" button at the top of the Rooms tab enters a dedicated mode (`RoomsReorderPanel`, new) where floors and, within each floor, its rooms, both drag into place — handle-only drag, same touch-safe pattern as the Home property list. Nothing saves while dragging; an explicit "Save" (or "Cancel") commits or discards the whole batch.
- **This replaces, not adds to, the previous per-floor drag.** The "expand a floor → drag a room → auto-saves on release" behavior from 2026-08-10 (below) is gone — reordering now only happens inside the new Reorder mode. `useHostelRooms` gains `reorderFloors` (writes each floor's `sort_order` via `PATCH /floors/:id` — no bulk endpoint exists) alongside the existing `reorderRooms`, which now returns its mutation's promise so both can be awaited together on Save.
- **Not verified in a live browser** — this session had no working `tsc`/dev-server toolchain; only `vite build` (esbuild, no type-check) and `check:architecture` were run, both clean for every touched file.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-064|ADR-064]], [[Features]].

### 2026-08-11 — Home dashboard: Action Center absorbs Snapshot, Renewal Agreements becomes conditional
- **One Action Center instead of two sections.** The read-only "Snapshot" section (Beds, Outstanding, Today's Revenue) is gone. Beds was dropped as redundant with the existing Fill Vacant Beds action card (same figure); Outstanding was dropped outright, redundant with Collect Rent's overdue amount; Today's Revenue moved into Action Center and is now clickable for the first time, opening `/owner/money`.
- **Renewal Agreements is now conditional** ([[Decisions#ADR-063|ADR-063]]) — the card only renders when the owner's primary hostel has `agreement_required` on ([[Decisions#ADR-059|ADR-059]]), reusing that existing setting rather than adding a second, dashboard-only toggle. Hidden until the settings query resolves, rather than flashing present-then-removed.
- **Unchanged:** the monthly "{Month} Collection" progress bar, the "View all" Action Center sheet, and every other card's existing click destination (Collect Rent, Activate Tenants, Fill Vacant Beds).
- **No backend change** — reuses `GET /hostels/:id/preferences`, already consumed elsewhere by the Agreement Requirement settings page.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-063|ADR-063]], [[Features]], [[Business-Rules]].
### 2026-08-11 — Pre-activation workspace rebuilt: real invitation status, one-field edits, and no owner-side activation
- **The owner can no longer activate a tenant** ([[Decisions#ADR-065|ADR-065]], per explicit owner direction). The "Activate tenant" button called `reactivate(status:'ACTIVE')`, flipping a tenant live without them ever registering — while the card directly above it said "waiting for tenant to create account". Activation happens only when the tenant finishes their own registration.
- **The screen now shows where the invitation actually is.** A four-step timeline — sent → opened → creating account → active — reading `opened_at`, `activation_started_at` and the real per-invitation `expires_at`. All of that was already in `tenant_invitations` and none of it was displayed; the old card said "Waiting for tenant to create account" and "Expires in 3 days" as fixed text, regardless of what had happened. "Not opened in over a day" now promotes the WhatsApp nudge to the primary action.
- **Editing is one field at a time, and sends once.** Tapping a row opens a sheet for that row only. Changes collect locally, a bar appears showing how many are unsent, and `Review & send` names every before → after value first. That batching is load-bearing: `updateInvitation` delegates to the resend path, so **each save rotates the token and re-issues the tenant's link** — the confirmation now says so.
- **Five defects fixed, all verified against live code** (details in [[Bugs]]): (1) the "Configure" form opened **completely blank** because the overview endpoint never returned a `hostel_id`, so the modal's prefill query was permanently disabled; (2) every invited tenant read "⚠ Assign room" — the room was read from `room_allocations`, which don't exist until activation, while the bed is really held by a reservation — which pinned the primary button to "Complete setup" for every tenant forever; (3) Private Notes were two hardcoded example strings in component state, though `tenant_notes` and its full CRUD API had existed unused all along; (4) "Copy link" copied `/activate?token=<tenant uuid>` — wrong token, wrong route, a dead link; (5) the cancel dialog promised a 30-day retention no code implements.
- **Also cut:** the Agreement card ("Preview agreement" was a toast stub, "Send link" was a duplicate resend), the Account Status card (four items only the tenant can supply), and the "Manage invitation" sheet (it duplicated buttons already on the page, and two of its three items called the same handler). `EditTenantModal` is deleted — it PUT `{name, phone}` to an endpoint whose schema requires `room_id`, so it had **never** succeeded. Seven cards became four sections; rent was previously restated three times and the room four.
- **One move-in total.** `computeMoveInTotal` is now the only formula — the profile summed rent + deposit while the edit modal summed rent + deposit + one-time maintenance, so the same labelled figure disagreed with itself between two screens of one flow.
- **Cancelling an invitation no longer 500s with "Transaction not found"** (owner-reported). `debitInTx` — which runs inside the caller's transaction — checked tenant ownership through the **global** Prisma client, opening a second connection while the interactive transaction held one. On a pooled/remote database that nested read waits for a free connection, the transaction exceeds its 5 s default timeout and closes, and the following `tenant_financial_ledger.create()` fails. Reached via `cancelInvitation → bulkWaiveInTx → applyCorrection → debitInTx`, since cancelling waives the tenant's pending obligations. The check now runs on `tx`, reusing a lookup that was already happening. Guarded by a test that debits a tenant created inside the same uncommitted transaction — a global-client read cannot see it — **confirmed to fail against the old code**. See [[Bugs]].
- **Phone numbers no longer print the country code twice** (owner-reported: "+91 +918008046952"). Stored phones are E.164, and the UI wrapped them in a `+91 {phone}` template — so re-saving without the prefix appeared to change nothing, since the value was normalized back and re-prefixed on render. New `shared/lib/phone.ts` gives one idempotent display format, a 10-digit edit field that stores back E.164, and digit-based comparison so a notation difference isn't mistaken for an edit. See [[Bugs]] for the one **frozen-tree** call site left unfixed.
- **Verified:** 586 frontend tests pass (38 of them new, pure), 331 backend pure tests pass, and 6 new **DB-backed** backend tests (`tenant-overview-invitation.test.ts`) pass against a real Postgres — those also empirically confirm that an invited tenant has no `current_room` and that the reserved room is the correct source. `tsc --noEmit` introduces no new errors (3 pre-existing in this area, unchanged). **Not verified in a browser.** `check:architecture` still fails on one **pre-existing, unrelated** violation (`TenantHelpPage.tsx` uses raw `fetch`) which fails `npm run build` independently of this change.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-065|ADR-065]], [[Bugs]], [[Features]], [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-10 — Rooms tab: floors collapse to an accordion, rooms drag-reorder within a floor
- **Floors on the owner Rooms tab now collapse, like the Food Library accordion** — previously every floor's rooms rendered flat, all at once. Tap a floor to reveal its rooms; collapsed, it shows a room/vacant-count summary. A search match force-expands the matching floor.
- **Drag-to-reorder rooms within a floor now actually works** ([[Decisions#ADR-062|ADR-062]]) — it didn't exist before. The old "layout" mode only moved a room *between* floors, and did so via native HTML5 `draggable`, which `DragHandle`'s own doc comment already flagged as non-functional (doesn't fire on touch — the same class of bug [[Decisions#ADR-042|ADR-042]] fixed for the Home Property list). Replaced with `motion/react`'s `Reorder`, handle-only drag, same pattern as the Property list. Order persists to a new nullable `rooms.sort_order` column (`PATCH /api/rooms/reorder`, `RoomOrderService`) — same shape as `hostels.display_order`.
- **Moving a room to a different floor** is now an explicit "Floor" select in the Room Sheet's edit form (the old floor-to-floor drag is removed — two floors can't usefully both be drop targets when only one is expanded at a time).
- **Verified live**, not just by build/typecheck: ran both dev servers, logged in as the seeded test owner, and drove the real UI with Playwright — confirmed the collapse/expand, a real drag reorder round-tripping through the new endpoint, and the new order surviving a full page reload, with no console errors.
- **Incidental fix:** the room deep-link effect (`?room=<id>` from Universal Search) called `Object.values()` on a `Map`, which always returns `[]` — the deep link had silently done nothing since it was written. Fixed while rewriting the page.
- See [[Decisions#ADR-062|ADR-062]], [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Database]].

### 2026-08-09 — One Stayo loading screen everywhere, no circular spinners left, and error screens redesigned onto the same stage
- **The app now has a designed loading state** ([[Decisions#ADR-061|ADR-061]]): the Stayo mark's four windows lighting clockwise over a warm sky gradient, with drifting embers and a linear progress track. New `shared/ui/brand/` exports `StayoLoader` (buttons/rows), `StayoLoadingBlock` (section bodies), `StayoLoadingScreen` (whole surfaces), `StayoErrorScreen`, and `StayoMark`/`StayoWordmark` — which are now the source of truth for the brand vector's geometry.
- **Every circular spinner is gone.** 67 `animate-spin` rings across 42 files, in four different colour treatments, replaced; `Loader2` removed from every import that had it. The only remaining rotation is none — the "Check again" button on the payment-return page now shows the window loader instead of a spinning refresh icon.
- **A cold load is no longer a white page.** `index.html` gains `#stayo-boot`, a pre-React boot splash that paints on the HTML parser's first pass, before any JS or CSS has arrived. `main.tsx` fades it out after React's first commit into a `StayoLoadingScreen` drawing the same screen, so the hand-off is invisible. It duplicates ~40 lines of CSS and the mark's path on purpose — it cannot import anything; **both copies must be edited together.**
- **Route fallbacks were rationalised, not blanket-replaced.** Cold boundaries (owner/tenant/admin platform entry, auth gates, public and journey routes) show the brand screen — including the public tree's old bare `bg-slate-50` rectangle, which was off-palette entirely. Transitions inside an already-mounted shell keep their layout skeletons, which read as faster.
- **Error screens are now the loading screen with the lights out** — same sky, same mark, windows unlit with one guttering, severity carried by a badge icon and the copy instead of a red alarm. Stack traces are dev-only and collapsed behind a disclosure. `PageError` and `ErrorCard` kept their exact prop signatures, so every existing caller works unchanged; `ErrorCard` moved from raw `red-*` utilities to the theme's warm `destructive` tokens.
- **Pane sequencing is CSS `animation-delay`, not `requestAnimationFrame`** — visually identical to the design source, no main-thread cost, and it keeps animating while the bundle parses, which is when it matters.
- **Verified:** 536 frontend tests pass, `tsc --noEmit` introduces no new errors (38 pre-existing, unchanged), `check:architecture` and `check:branding` pass, and `vite build` succeeds. **Not verified in a browser.**

### 2026-08-09 — Deposit gets a calculation mode with a live preview, Save only appears when you've changed something, and the tenant agreement becomes optional
- **The security deposit can now be a fixed amount *or* a multiple of rent, and the screen shows what that works out to** ([[Decisions#ADR-060|ADR-060]]). `deposit_calculation_mode` has existed in the backend all along, but no UI ever wrote it — the Deposit screen saved `deposit_months` alone, so an owner setting "2 months" changed **nothing**: the stored mode stayed `FLAT` and invites kept charging `default_amount`. Now the mode is written explicitly, and the screen states the outcome before saving — "2 months × ₹8,000 rent = ₹16,000", a range when rooms charge different rents, and no invented figure when no room has a rent yet. Rents come from the grouped-rooms payload the hub already fetches, so the preview costs no extra request.
- **Switching the deposit off now actually collects nothing.** `resolveTenantInviteDefaults` never reads `deposit.enabled` — it resolves from the mode regardless — so "off" is stored as a flat ₹0, the one shape that resolves to zero. Left as-is, turning the deposit off would have kept charging months × rent.
- **The screen warns about a trap instead of hiding it:** rent is `auto_fill_room_rent ? base_rent : 0`, so months-of-rent with auto-fill off silently resolves to ₹0. It now says so rather than showing a confident ₹16,000 nobody will be charged.
- **`refundable` became editable** — stored, read by move-out settlement, and previously unreachable in the UI. The Finance row also describes the deposit by mode, and a flat ₹0 deposit that is switched on reads as needing attention rather than the "configured" it used to claim.
- **Save now appears only once you've actually changed something**, across every configuration screen with a Save button (all five billing screens, receipt footer, hostel identity, profile). Every screen previously showed a permanent Save bar, so an untouched screen looked identical to an edited one, and saving a screen you had only read still issued a PATCH — bumping the policy version and writing a Recent Changes entry for a change that never happened. The comparison is against a baseline captured from the loaded policy, so retyping the same value or toggling something off and back on correctly leaves the screen clean. A **Discard** action sits next to Save, since an appearing bar implies a pending edit.
- **A hostel can now waive the tenant agreement** ([[Decisions#ADR-059|ADR-059]]). New `tenant_rules.agreement_required` (default **true**) skips the rules and signing steps during onboarding. It deliberately governs the **ceremony only** — `Agreement` rows are still created, because that record is the financial contract rent changes, obligations, renewals and settlement all key to; suppressing it would detach rent from its contract rather than skip paperwork. Skipped steps are reported as skipped rather than faked complete, so progress reads 2-of-3 instead of 2-of-5 and the audit trail stays honest.
- **Both activation gates were updated, not just the obvious one** — the finalise path re-checks `rules_accepted` independently of `assertTransition`, so changing only the state machine would have left agreement-free hostels unable to activate at all. The tenant flow needed no change (it follows `current_step`), but the progress bar's hardcoded "Agreement" pip is now dropped when the requirement is off.
- **Verified:** 541 frontend tests and 331 backend pure tests pass, the frontend builds, and `check:architecture`, `check:invariants` and `check:activation-invariants` all pass. **Not verified in a browser**, and no tenant has been onboarded end-to-end through an agreement-free hostel.
### 2026-08-09 — Hostel Drill-down: real header data, and fetch errors no longer masquerade as empty tabs
- **The hostel drill-down header (`/owner/hostels/:hostelId/*`) now shows the real hostel name/city/status** instead of a hardcoded mock lookup that silently fell back to generic "Hostel" for any hostel not in the mock list — found while investigating a report of a tenant missing from a specific hostel's Tenants tab. Reuses the already-warm `portfolio/summary` query, no extra request.
- **`useHostelTenants`/`useHostelRooms` now surface `isError`/`error`/`refetch`**, and their pages render the existing `ErrorCard` component with a retry action on a real fetch failure, instead of silently rendering the same empty state as a hostel that genuinely has none. See [[Bugs]] for the full investigation and the still-open question this was meant to help diagnose.

### 2026-08-08 — Fixed a Prisma `relationJoins` bug that 500'd every tenant's authenticated request post-login
- **Every route a logged-in tenant hit after `/api/auth/login` was crashing** with `TypeError: (tenancies || []).filter is not a function` — found while building a test tenant end-to-end to check the Food feature, but reproduces for any tenant with exactly one live tenancy, so this affected the tenant portal generally, not just the test fixture. See [[Bugs]] for the full root-cause writeup.
- **Root cause:** `resolveSupabaseSession()`, `user-service.ts#getProfile`, and the `ACTIVATE`-step auto-login in `activation-workflow-service.ts` all fetched a profile's live tenancy via a `where`-filtered to-many Prisma `include` (`liveTenancyInclude`); with the `relationJoins` preview feature enabled, Prisma generated SQL that returned a single JSON object instead of an array whenever exactly one tenancy matched.
- **Fix:** all three call sites now fetch the profile plain and resolve the live tenancy via a separate `getActiveTenancy(profileId)` query instead of the filtered `include`. Verified live (login + `/api/auth/me` + both `/api/food/tenant/*` routes now 200) — not run through `vitest`, since this environment has no test database configured.

### 2026-08-08 — Food Polls: a real, ad-hoc polling feature (owner create, tenant vote)
- **New feature, built end-to-end**: owners can now create a one-off poll (title, poll type, options, meal category, date, closing time) at `/owner/food/polls`, publish it immediately, and tenants vote on it from `/tenant/food`. New tables `food_polls`/`food_poll_options`/`food_poll_votes`; new routes under `/api/food/polls/*` and `/api/food/tenant/polls/*`. Auto-closes daily via the existing `food-carry-forward` cron, now reporting `pollsClosed`. See [[Decisions#ADR-057|ADR-057]], [[Food]] §16.
- **Deliberately independent** of both the dormant monthly voting window (`food_voting_periods`/`food_votes`, decoupled from schedule generation the same day — see below) and the deleted mock "Food Polls" ([[Decisions#ADR-048|ADR-048]]) — this is real, from scratch, with a working tenant-vote path neither of those ever had together in one place.
- "Use Winning Option →" on a closed poll's results adds the winning label straight to the Food Library, via the same creation path the schedule picker already uses.
- Discovered and worked around two **pre-existing, unrelated** dev-database issues while applying the migration: a previously-failed migration (`20260501000000_add_late_fee`) blocks `prisma migrate deploy` outright, and `schema.prisma` no longer declares two `tenants` columns that still hold 390 real rows in the dev DB, so `prisma db push` would have silently dropped them. Neither touched; the new tables were applied via a hand-written, purely-additive migration executed directly. Flagged in [[Food]] §16 for whoever hits this next.

### 2026-08-08 — Food schedule generation decoupled from voting
- **The owner Food tab no longer gates "Generate Schedule" on a voting period**, and the Voting card is hidden. Generation now always builds straight from the Food Library (all active items per meal type, alphabetically) — deliberate simplification, not a bugfix. See [[Decisions#ADR-056|ADR-056]].
- **`POST /api/food/schedules/generate` no longer auto-attaches an existing voting period** for the hostel+month when `votingPeriodId` is omitted — it previously did, so hiding the frontend card alone would not have fully decoupled generation. Voting-ranked generation is now reachable only when a caller explicitly passes `votingPeriodId`.
- **The tenant Food tab's "Vote on this month's menu" section is also hidden**, avoiding a permanent "Voting hasn't started" dead-end now that the owner side never opens voting.
- **Nothing voting-related was deleted.** The DB models, API routes, `VotingPanel`, and both `useFoodVoting`/`useTenantFoodVoting` hooks stay in place, unused — reversible groundwork for a future, separate "polling" feature. See [[Food]] §10.
- Removed the now-dead `votes` publish-check line and `hasVotesApplied` helper (`publishChecks.ts`); `publishChecks.test.ts` drops from 17 to 12 tests accordingly.

### 2026-08-08 — "+ Add hostel" actually opens the popup now, and its password step-up is actually enforced
- **The dashboard's "+ Add hostel" now opens `AddHostelModal`** (Hostel Name/Address/City/State/Pincode/Contact Number bottom sheet) instead of navigating to the 12-step `/onboarding` wizard. The modal itself was built in the 2026-08-02 dead-button pass below but its one call site (`OwnerDashboardPreviewPage.tsx`'s `onAddHostel`) was never updated to use it — see [[Bugs]] for the full root-cause writeup.
- **`POST /api/owner/hostels` now actually verifies the `CREATE_HOSTEL` step-up token.** Previously the frontend fetched an identity token via `confirmIdentity` but never sent it, and the backend never checked for one — the password prompt was cosmetic. The route now calls `verifyIdentityConfirmation` and atomically consumes the token inside the same `prisma.$transaction` as the hostel-name-uniqueness check and `hostels.create` (mirroring the guard `payments/record-offline` already uses). See [[APIs]].

### 2026-08-08 — Configuration hub redesign (slices 1–3: Hub, Hostel, Finance, Automation, Agreements)
- **The hub and its Hostel and Finance screens now match the supplied mockups**, with every count derived from live policy rather than the design's example numbers. Rows whose subsystem does not exist render "Not available yet", are inert, and are excluded from every count — so the progress ring never reports on features nobody can use. See [[Features]].
- **Recent Changes became real.** Nothing recorded configuration changes before this: `recordPolicyChanges()` now writes one `activity_logs` row per changed policy leaf through the existing `ActivityService` (no migration), hooked into the one `PATCH /api/hostels/:id/preferences` chokepoint every domain is written through, and `GET /api/owner/config-changes` reads it back. Labels come from a pure `describeConfigChange()` — "Late fee raised to ₹50", direction-aware, and never interpolating a value it has no phrasing for.
- **Three bits of fiction removed from production:** the hub header rendered `mockOwnerProfile` from `@shared/mocks`; Advanced's six rows were tappable-looking and inert; and the Finance screen printed `${auto_rent_day}st`, rendering "2st" for rent generated on the 2nd.
- **Deleted the duplicate `/owner/config/*` scaffold tree** — twelve placeholder routes registered in `AppRouter` but unlinked from navigation, the reconciliation [[Features]] had flagged.
- **Audit corrections:** `AgreementTemplate` and `hostels.gst_number` both exist, contrary to the earlier note; and `billing.deposit` *is* the legacy `advance_*` preference, so "Advance payments" is not a separate setting.
- **Not verified in a browser, and Recent Changes has never shown a real row** — no backfill is possible, so it fills as changes are made.
- **Slice 2: the Automation module.** Nine live workflow toggles (rent generation, late-fee processing, reminder engine, overdue escalation, WhatsApp/email/in-app channels, auto receipts, nightly reconciliation, daily snapshots), each gating a real cron job. The audit undercounted the available flags — seven automation toggles exist, not four. Overdue escalation writes a **day count**, not a boolean, because the backend validates it as 0–365. SMS stays a placeholder: the flag is stored but nothing sends an SMS. Toggling anything here now shows up in Recent Changes, since both came from the same work.
- **Slice 3: the Agreements module.** Four screens — hub, templates list, template preview with every `{{VARIABLE}}` highlighted, and a per-template clause manager whose toggles save as a draft. No owner-facing agreement UI existed at all before this. Departures from the design, each forced by the data: the real **eight** substitution variables rather than eighteen; **Signatures** rather than "Aadhaar OTP" (no Aadhaar integration exists); stored `severity` badges rather than invented Core/Policy/Optional; and a per-template clause manager, since a cross-template library needs a new table plus a backfill.
- **Billing policy split into focused screens** ([[Decisions#ADR-058|ADR-058]]).
