Every Finance row used to deep-link to the same 352-line, five-section form, so tapping "Security deposit" landed you in a six-section wall. Now each row opens the screen that owns its fields — rent schedule, part payments, deposit, late fees, and agreement duration under Hostel. ADR-043's guarantee is kept by construction: `buildBillingPatch` writes only the visible sections' fields, the backend deep-merges, and the late fee is always written whole — the partial write that once reset the charge type. **"Part payments" also becomes a real row**; the setting was always stored but only reachable by scrolling the combined form.
- **Slice 4: Notifications + Account & Security.** Both restructure the design rather than copy it. Notification channels are stored **globally**, so they appear once at the top instead of as per-event chips that would all write the same three flags; **SMS and push are omitted** (no provider, and no push infrastructure at all). "Account & Team" becomes **Account & Security** — `Role` has no manager or staff, and 2FA has no schema, so inventing them on a security screen would be the worst kind of fiction. In place of the mockup's dead "3 devices" row, it ships the real **Sign out of all devices** action, which now actually revokes via the Redis deny-list.

### 2026-08-08 — Owner sign-out actually signs you out
- **Fixed a high-severity auth bypass:** the owner's "Sign out" control reset *mock onboarding state* and redirected to the landing page without touching authentication — no server revocation, no Supabase sign-out. The session survived, so returning to `/login` re-hydrated the user and dropped the "signed-out" owner back into `/owner/home`. Reported from a real session; tenant, admin and the legacy portal were unaffected. See [[Bugs]].
- **Guarded against recurrence:** `logoutIntegrity.test.ts` asserts every sign-out control in the app reaches `AuthContext.logout()`, following each file's own imports, so a cosmetic sign-out cannot ship again. It failed on exactly the two affected owner pages before the fix.

### 2026-08-08 — Phone reset checks the account before sending a code
- **`POST /api/auth/forgot-password/phone` now tells you when no account uses that number** (404 `NO_ACCOUNT_FOR_PHONE`) instead of a generic "if an account exists…" success. Reported by the user: a mistyped digit left you on the code-entry screen for five minutes waiting for a code that was never sent.
- **This reverses the anti-enumeration stance in [[Decisions#ADR-055|ADR-055]], on purpose and with a reason:** `selfSignUpOwner`/`selfSignUpTenant` already reject a duplicate with `ALREADY_EXISTS: Phone number already registered` on public unauthenticated routes, so the fact was one signup form away. The generic reply was guarding a secret the product already gave away, at a real usability cost.
- **What still holds:** per-phone and per-IP limits still run *before* the lookup, so the disclosure is per-probe rather than a bulk oracle; a deactivated account reports as absent, so account status stays undisclosed; and the **email** channel remains fully generic, since no equivalent signup-side leak exists for email addresses. Restoring non-disclosure here would be theatre unless the signup routes are fixed first.
- **A failed send is no longer reported as success** — the OTP service's own error code (or `502 OTP_SEND_FAILED`) surfaces, so the UI keeps you on the number step. A failed *resend* stays on the code screen rather than discarding a code you may have already typed.

### 2026-08-08 — Auth recovery: why Google and login were dead, and password reset by WhatsApp
- **Root-caused a production auth outage to a Supabase project mismatch.** Google sign-in died with `Invalid session` and password login with "Unable to connect", from one fault: the backend verified access tokens against a different Supabase project than the frontend minted them with. **Fixing it is an operator action** — set `SUPABASE_URL`/`SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` on the backend Vercel project to the canonical project (`xhoqkhwsnqfwhjsffybs`, the one `DATABASE_URL` already points at). See [[Bugs]].
- **Made that class of fault visible.** `/api/health` now reports `auth.supabase.{project_ref, expected_issuer, jwks_reachable}` — one `curl` instead of downloading the deployed frontend bundle to compare refs. `SUPABASE_URL` is normalized, so a trailing slash can no longer reject every session in the system silently.
- **Two error messages stopped lying.** A `setSession` failure reported "check your internet" (it is a server config fault); `/api/auth/me` collapsed every Supabase rejection to a flat `Unauthorized` despite `lib/auth/supabase-session.ts` computing specific codes and claiming in its own header comment that `/auth/me` consumed them — that wiring never existed. It now answers 403 with the real reason, and `AuthCallbackPage` distinguishes "your account can't sign in this way" from "this deployment is misconfigured".
- **Google sign-in opens to tenants** ([[Decisions#ADR-054|ADR-054]]), keeping the never-auto-provision invariant and mirroring login's `INVITED`-tenancy activation gate, which the old role block had been enforcing only by accident.
- **Password reset by WhatsApp OTP** ([[Decisions#ADR-055|ADR-055]]): `POST /api/auth/forgot-password/phone` and `POST /api/auth/verify-reset-otp`, the latter exchanging a verified code for a 5-minute reset token consumed by the existing `/api/auth/reset-password`. `PASSWORD_RESET` is deliberately not a skippable OTP purpose, so it fails closed when WhatsApp is down — guarded by a test verified to fail under mutation.
- **Password reset emails stopped silently failing.** Provider failures were caught and reported as success, so a total delivery outage looked identical to a working one; they are now logged and event-logged (`PASSWORD_RESET_EMAIL_FAILED`), and the response carries `delivery_degraded` derived from provider *configuration* only, so it cannot enumerate accounts. The email was also still branded "Sri Adithya Boys Hostel" and now uses the Stayo shell. **Operator action:** verify `yourstayo.com` at resend.com/domains.
- **`/forgot-password` rebuilt** as choose-method → identify → set-password, rendering under `ThemeProvider theme="marketing"` like LandingPage. Without it the route resolved theme.css's legacy navy/orange `:root` tokens while the login popup resolved marketing clay — the reason it looked like a different product. No hardcoded hex values remain.
- **Not verified in production:** the environment fault above blocks any login there, so this flow has not been exercised end-to-end and no real WhatsApp reset code has been received yet. See [[Features]] for the verification status and the three outstanding operator actions.

### 2026-08-07 — Tenants join without paying, and can move between hostels
- **The onboarding payment gate is gone.** A tenant's room is assigned when they join, not when their deposit clears. `reservation-status-service.ts` and `activation-financial-enforcement-service.ts` are deleted, along with the `PAYMENT_PENDING`/`RESERVED`/`MOVE_IN_READY` vocabulary; deposit and maintenance are ordinary dues payable after move-in. See [[Decisions]] ADR-052.
- **Occupancy stopped depending on money.** `room-capacity-service` counted an unpaid-but-moved-in tenant as *not* occupying their bed, leaving it invitable to someone else. It now counts active allocations of `ACTIVE` tenants — which also removes a per-tenant query inside a loop and a duplicated inline copy of the deposit arithmetic.
- **A `tenants` row is now one tenancy, not one person.** `profile_id` loses its global unique; a partial unique index (`tenants_one_live_tenancy_per_profile`) allows exactly one live tenancy per profile while preserving every past stay as its own row. A settled former tenant can finally join a different hostel — previously impossible at the database level. See [[Decisions]] ADR-053, [[Database]].
- **Inviting someone who already lives somewhere now explains itself.** Replaces `ALREADY_EXISTS: User with this email already exists` with a 409 carrying an ownership-scoped disclosure: the asking owner sees their *own* hostel and room named, but never another owner's. Rendered as a dialog in the invite modal.
- **Accepting one invitation withdraws the others.** Competing invitations are voided, their room reservations released back to capacity (`release_reason: 'JOINED_ELSEWHERE'`), and the losing owners notified — previously those rooms stayed reserved until expiry and the invitee's other links died on a raw constraint error.
- **Removed:** partial/token-deposit reservations (`reservation_policy`, `minimum_reservation_deposit`, `minimum_deposit_threshold`), migration 062. The admissions funnel's `reserved`/`payment_pending` stages. Two dead tenant-portal components (`TenantReservationCard`, `OnboardingProgressTracker`) that had zero importers.

### 2026-08-07 — Admin dashboard: Recent Activity accuracy, Owner Leads card bug fixes
- **Owner Leads dashboard card removed.** Per explicit user direction, once the leads badge (below) existed, the Dashboard's own "Owner Leads" preview card (Approve/Reject/Details inline) became a second, redundant lead-facing surface. Removed it along with `leads_preview` from `GET /api/platform-admin/dashboard`'s response — no remaining consumer. The Dashboard's left column is now just Overview (KPIs), with Revenue Summary + Hostel Health as the right column. See [[Features]], [[APIs]].
- **Leads get their own header badge.** New `UserPlus` icon in the admin top bar next to the bell, showing `+N` actionable leads (`NEW`/`UNDER_REVIEW`/`APPROVED`); clicking it navigates straight to `/admin/leads` rather than opening a dropdown, since leads already have one dedicated place to work them. See [[Features]].
- **Recent Activity moved behind the notification bell.** The dashboard's inline "Recent Activity" card duplicated the header bell (`GET /api/platform-admin/notifications`) with a different interaction model; removed the card and the `activity` field from `GET /api/platform-admin/dashboard` entirely — the bell is now the only place this feed shows. `composeRecentActivity()` also now excludes leads (previously mixed lead status-changes in alongside hostel/invoice events) — leads already have a dedicated place (the Owner Leads card, `/admin/leads`), so a lead update showing up as a generic notification too was a second, inconsistent path to the same information.
- **Recent Activity feed labels.** `composeRecentActivity()` labeled every hostel-row creation "Onboarded" unconditionally; now reads `verification_status`/`listing_status` and reports `Onboarded`/`Suspended`/`Verified — pending listing`/`Pending verification` accordingly, matching the vocabulary already used by `AdminHostelsPage.tsx`'s chips. See [[Bugs]].
- **Owner Leads dashboard card.** A single shared `useMutation` instance was disabling every lead's Approve button whenever any one lead's mutation was in flight — scoped per-row via `mutation.variables`, and the card now shows a status badge, reusing `canApprove`/`canReject`/`STATUS_LABEL`/`STATUS_TONE` from the `leadQueue.ts` module introduced in `8f77ff9` (which independently fixed the companion bug: `leads_preview` no longer includes already-actioned leads). See [[Bugs]].
- **Dev DB cleanup.** Purged ~700 stray test-fixture rows (135 `Test Hostel *` hostels and everything hanging off them, plus a leftover test lead) that had accumulated because `apps/backend/.env.test` and the dev root `.env` resolve to the same Supabase project — flagged as an open item, not fixed. See [[Bugs]].

### 2026-08-07 — Owner onboarding: persistence, guidance, verification, location
- **Draft persistence.** The wizard held every answer in `useState`, so closing the tab across eight steps threw it all away. It now auto-saves to localStorage and restores with a banner and a Start over escape. Passwords are excluded by an explicit field allowlist, asserted by a test. See [[Features]].
- **Password guidance.** Replaces a single length check with a live criteria checklist and strength meter. A 20+ character passphrase counts as strong on length alone.
- **Deposit.** Asks whether one is taken, then months-of-rent or a flat amount, echoing the resolved rupee figure. Monthly rent moved above it, since months has nothing to multiply otherwise.
- **Room generation.** Floors/rooms/beds now confirm before advancing without generating — previously Continue walked straight past, so an owner could finish having laid out nothing.
- **Location.** Address autocomplete behind a swappable `PlacesProvider`, stubbed until a Google Maps key exists. The stub reports `isReal:false` and returns no coordinates. See [[Decisions]].
- **Owner documents.** Aadhaar and PAN are both required to upload; approval gates going live, not wizard progress. Adds the admin review queue at `/admin/documents` and `owner_documents.review_note` — uploads had been landing since ADR-038 with no surface to review them, so nothing could ever leave PENDING. See [[APIs]], [[Database]].
- **Mobile.** Onboarding tiles stack below `sm`; at 390px a long label collided with its input.


### 2026-08-07 — Lead capture: qualify first, Google last
- Landing-page owner CTA no longer opens Google. It opens a three-question flow (hostel name, owner name, phone + OTP) that **saves the lead at the phone step**, then offers Google purely as optional email enrichment via the new `POST /api/leads/track/[token]/link-email`. Previously Google gated everything, so anyone unwilling to hand over an identity on a first visit was lost entirely. See [[Features]], [[APIs]].
- Scroll-depth enquiry prompt: removed the 7-day localStorage cooldown outright (an invisible stored timestamp made the feature look broken to everyone testing it), restyled to the marketing theme, and fixed two bugs that meant it **could never appear at all** — see [[Bugs]].
- Fixed the landing nav's `scrolled` treatment, which had never fired in production for the same underlying reason.
- Added `platform_leads.pain_point` / `current_tooling` (currently unwritten — see [[Database]]).

### Added
- **2026-08-06**: **Owner lead funnel notifications + public enquiry tracking** (`feat/owner-lead-funnel-notifications`, module: [[Features]] "StayO owner-acquisition funnel"; [[Decisions#ADR-050|ADR-050]], [[Decisions#ADR-051|ADR-051]]). The owner-acquisition funnel (landing → Google + phone-OTP lead → admin review → activation) ran end to end but never talked back to the applicant — no acknowledgement on submission, no status-tracking surface at all, a silent rejection path, and `markLive()` telling nobody their hostel had gone live. Five WhatsApp templates now cover the whole funnel — ① `stayo_owner_lead_received`, ② `stayo_owner_invitation`, ③ `stayo_owner_account_activated` (supersedes the now-**orphaned** `stayo_owner_welcome`, left in place and still Meta-approved but no longer called by anything), ④ `stayo_owner_onboarding_complete`, ⑤ `stayo_owner_lead_rejected` — declared in one new registry module, `platform-lead-template-contracts.ts`, a deliberate departure from the established one-file-per-template pattern (see [[Decisions#ADR-050|ADR-050]] for why). **Schema:** `platform_leads` gained three columns — `tracking_token` (unique, 32-byte hex, minted at row creation, never expiring), `applicant_message` (owner-visible), `rejection_reason` (set only by the new reject action); `notes` stays admin-private, unchanged (see [[Database]]). New public route `GET /api/leads/track/[token]` (see [[APIs]]) backs a new `/enquiry/:token` page, gated only by possession of the token — a deliberate bearer-secret trade-off over OTP/Google re-auth ([[Decisions#ADR-050|ADR-050]]). New admin route `POST /api/platform-admin/leads/[id]/reject` requires a reason and is the *only* path that notifies a decline — a plain PATCH to `LOST` stays silent, since `LOST` also means "went cold" ([[Decisions#ADR-051|ADR-051]], [[Business-Rules]]); `PATCH /api/platform-admin/leads/[id]` also now accepts `applicant_message`. Frontend: a scroll-depth (40%) "Are you a hostel owner?" landing prompt reusing the existing `GoogleSignInModal`, and an admin reject-with-reason UI with the applicant-message field visibly separated from private notes. **Honestly incomplete:** only ① `stayo_owner_lead_received` is approved in Meta as of this writing — sends ②③④⑤ will fail until Meta approval clears (code is complete and correct; the templates are the blocker). The migration (`20260806120000_platform_lead_tracking`) is committed but **not applied to any database** in this environment, and the dev-server/curl and browser end-to-end verifications were not performed — no reachable, migrated database was available. See [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Decisions#ADR-050|ADR-050]], [[Decisions#ADR-051|ADR-051]].
- **2026-08-06**: **Food UX pass — a week you can see, a meal you can move, and voting that tells anyone** (`feat/food-ux-pass`, seven commits; module page: [[Food]]; feature entry: [[Features]]). **No new ADR** — the one policy question this touched was already answered by [[Decisions#ADR-042|ADR-042]], and the rest restates rules the module already had. **What was broken.** The hostel picker was a native `<select>`, which sizes itself to its **longest option** — a real hostel name pushed the control past the viewport and it rendered clipped, on the header of the very screen that control exists to disambiguate. The weekly editor was **28 cards over roughly seven screens** of scrolling, so seeing the week meant remembering it, and moving Tuesday's dinner to Thursday meant two picker trips with a duplicated dish in between. Voting had three holes: **opening it notified nobody** — there was no notification call in that route at all, while publishing a schedule fanned out to every active tenant — the owner could not change an open window's dates once opened, and turnout was invisible, the panel showing only a count of vote *rows*. **What live data said.** The same production snapshot as the Phase 0–1 audit: **1 voting period and 2 votes in the product's lifetime**. That makes the missing notification the most plausible cause of an inert feature rather than a tidiness fix — an inference, not a proof, since `notifications` carries no read-tracking. **What shipped.** The picker is a `max-w-[46vw]` trigger plus the app's own `BottomSheet`, which also shows names in full instead of truncating them (`min-w-0` on the neighbouring title block is what lets truncation engage at all). The editor is seven rows of four chips — `DayRow.tsx`, the shape `MonthHistoryList` already used for a published month — and the whole week now fits about one screen, which is what makes dragging a chip to another day practical on a phone. Dragging a meal onto another day swaps them through **one new endpoint**, `POST /api/food/schedules/[id]/meals/swap`: both cells exchange items in a single `$transaction`, because two sequential PATCHes are not equivalent — a failure between them leaves one meal duplicated and the other lost, on a row tenants may already be reading. Opening voting now notifies every active, profiled tenant and returns `notified`; the results route gained **`voterCount`** (DISTINCT tenants) and `eligibleCount`, with `totalVotes` retained and unchanged because it counts rows and a tenant may pick several items per meal type — the two numbers mean different things and both are now on screen; and the owner can edit an open window. **What was reused rather than rebuilt:** the drag is `dragListener={false}` + `useDragControls` from `motion/react`, the handle-only rule `PropertyList.tsx` established and ADR-042 recorded — adopted, not re-decided, so the page keeps its scroll and the chip keeps its tap-to-edit everywhere except the grip; the **same-meal-type constraint is the rule the cell PATCH already applied**, now stated in three places for three different reasons (the PATCH guards the write, the pure `canSwap` guards the transaction, `isValidDrop` refuses the gesture so an invalid drag dies at the drop instead of surfacing as a 400 mid-drag); editing an open voting window needed **no new endpoint**, since the upsert already supported it and only the UI was missing — one more built-but-unreachable capability in a module that has had several; and the notification fan-out copies publish's shape exactly, `Promise.allSettled` plus an `isNewRound` guard mirroring `wasAlreadyPublished`, because the route is *also* the edit-the-window path and re-announcing on every date tweak trains tenants to ignore it. **No new dependency, no schema change, no migration.** **One finding worth keeping:** motion's `PanInfo.point` is `pageX`/`pageY`, **not** viewport coordinates — verified in `framer-motion/dist/es/events/event-info.mjs`, the docs saying only "relative to the device or page" — so rects are stored in page space via `window.scrollX/Y`; getting this wrong makes every drop on a scrolled page land on the wrong cell, silently. Hit-testing uses measured rects rather than `elementFromPoint`, since the dragged chip sits under the pointer and would always hit-test itself. **Explicitly not built, and the honest gaps:** no reopening a closed voting round and no second round in a month; **the drag gesture has no automated test** — the frontend suite is node-only with no jsdom, so only the two pure decisions are covered and the interaction has never been exercised in a browser; **there is no keyboard or assistive-technology path to swap** two meals (the capability survives by editing each cell, but the direct swap is pointer-only, and unlike `PropertyList` there is no Move up/down equivalent); a swap carries the same blast radius as any cell edit, moving both meals for **every** such weekday in the month rather than one date; and one defect was **caught by writing these docs and fixed before merge** (`9f7cb8c`) — the new notification's title read *"Vote on next month's menu"* while the owner page opens voting for the **current** month, reintroducing in a second place the exact copy this module deliberately corrected the day before. It was found by checking the string against which month the route actually operates on, rather than against the plan that specified it. Tests rose backend **176 → 183** pure / 11 files and frontend **225 → 233** / 11 files; 9/9 invariants pass. See [[Food]], [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Decisions#ADR-042|ADR-042]].

### Fixed
- **2026-08-07**: **Dev/testing frontend deployment blocked by a hardcoded CSP.** Stood up `dev` as the standing integration branch (`origin/dev`, tracked by the Vercel `stayo-testing` backend project's Production environment, domain `stayo-testing.vercel.app`) alongside the split dev Supabase project from [[project_stayo_rebrand_scope|the earlier env-split work]]. Backend build failed first: `apps/backend/lib/db.ts:97-100` constructs its Supabase client **eagerly at module load**, so a missing `SUPABASE_URL` in the Vercel project's env vars crashed the entire build during Next.js's page-data-collection step (`Error: supabaseUrl is required`, surfaced from an unrelated route, `/api/agreements/[id]/renewal-offer`) rather than failing only the route that needed it — fixed by setting the project's env vars, not a code change (the eager-init pattern itself is a latent fragility worth revisiting, not addressed here). Then the frontend: `apps/frontend/src/lib/api-client.ts` already builds its API base URL correctly from `VITE_API_URL` alone (no hardcoded host), so a dev frontend project only needed that var pointed at the dev backend — but `apps/frontend/vercel.json`'s CSP `connect-src` allowlisted only the production backend origin, so the browser silently blocked every request regardless of what `VITE_API_URL` said. Fixed by adding the dev backend origin to `connect-src` as a static second entry — `vercel.json` can't read env vars at parse time, so true per-environment values aren't possible without adopting Vercel's Build Output API, which was considered and deferred as disproportionate for now. `/pay/:token` WhatsApp payment-link rewrites were deliberately left pointed at prod only. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-06**: **Owner lead funnel notifications branch — seven findings from the final whole-branch review, fixed in one pass** (`feat/owner-lead-funnel-notifications`; module: [[Features]] "StayO owner-acquisition funnel"; full write-up: [[Bugs]]). Three Important, four Minor, no Critical. **Data-loss finding.** `AdminLeadsPage.tsx`'s message-editor `useEffect` seeded the textarea from `leadDetailQuery.data?.applicant_message` while the drawer itself rendered from a list-row fallback the moment it opened — so an admin opening a lead that already had a message saw an empty box for the instant before the detail query landed, and a "Save message" click in that window PATCHed `applicant_message: ''`, destroying what the applicant was reading. Now seeds from `openLead?.applicant_message` (the same fallback the drawer renders from) and the Save button is disabled while `leadDetailQuery.isLoading`. **Important, runtime-only (compiler blind).** `platform_leads.create` at both `app/api/platform-admin/leads/route.ts` and `scripts/seed-uat-demo-chain.ts` omitted the now-required `tracking_token` — `prisma` is typed `any` and `next.config.js` sets `ignoreBuildErrors: true`, so this only surfaces as a runtime `PrismaClientValidationError`. Both now mint one the same way `leads/self-serve/route.ts` already does (`crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex")`). **Important, migration safety.** `prisma/migrations/20260806120000_platform_lead_tracking/migration.sql` called `gen_random_bytes()` (pgcrypto-only) with no `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;` — on a Supabase-realistic DB where the extension is absent, the three `ADD COLUMN`s would commit and the backfill `UPDATE` would then throw, leaving a half-applied migration blocking every later one. Fixed by prepending the same `CREATE EXTENSION` line `20260514134500_whatsapp_logs_id_default` already uses. See [[Database]]. **Important, recovery path.** Since `stayo_owner_invitation` is unapproved in Meta and admin-created leads never collect a `google_email`, a lead approved with both channels failing left the activation link nowhere an admin could find it short of a manual DB query. `approveLead()` now returns `activationLink` alongside the existing delivery flags; the approve route response and the admin API wrapper's type were updated to thread it through; the lead drawer now shows it as a copyable field, but **only** when `!whatsapp_sent && !email_sent` on that approval — the `APPROVED`-vs-`INVITE_SENT` status logic is unchanged. See [[APIs]]. **Minor.** `rejection_reason` was written by the reject endpoint but never readable anywhere — if template ⑤ failed to send (unapproved), the applicant's tracking page said "Not proceeding" forever with no reason. `GET /api/leads/track/[token]` now selects and returns it, but only when `status === 'LOST'` (`null` otherwise); the response stays an explicit allowlist, `notes` still never appears. `/enquiry/:token` renders it in the same style as the existing "Message from our team" block. See [[APIs]]. **Minor.** `sendAccountActivated`'s event-log types were lowercase (`owner_account_activated_notified`/`_failed`) while its four siblings log SCREAMING_SNAKE — renamed to `OWNER_ACCOUNT_ACTIVATED_NOTIFIED`/`_NOTIFY_FAILED` for consistency; no other code referenced the old strings. `AdminLeadsPage.tsx`'s `TIMELINE_LABEL` map gained the seven event types it was missing (`LEAD_RECEIVED_NOTIFIED/_NOTIFY_FAILED`, `LEAD_REJECTED`/`_NOTIFIED`/`_NOTIFY_FAILED`, `LEAD_LIVE_NOTIFIED`/`_NOTIFY_FAILED`), which previously rendered as raw SCREAMING_SNAKE in the drawer. **Minor.** `EnquiryStatusPage.tsx`'s `isError || !data` collapsed a 500 into 404 copy ("we couldn't find that enquiry") for a real applicant when the database merely blipped; now checks the error's `response?.status === 404` (duck-typed, not an `axios` import — `check-architecture.mjs` forbids importing `from 'axios'` even for types in UI code) and shows a distinct "something went wrong, try again" state for anything else. **No schema change beyond the pre-existing three-column migration (now fixed, see above); no new dependency.** Verification: backend `test:pure` 13 files/209 tests pass; frontend `test` 13 files/250 tests pass; frontend `build` (architecture + branding checks) passes; backend `tsc` shows zero new errors in any file this wave touched. See [[Bugs]], [[APIs]], [[Database]].
- **2026-08-06**: **The Food UX branch's edges, in one wave** — three Important and eight Minor findings from the final whole-branch review of `feat/food-ux-pass`, no Critical (module page: [[Food]] §7.2; full write-up: [[Bugs]]). The review verified the two things the branch rested on and found them **right** — motion's `PanInfo.point` really is page-space and the shell really does scroll the window, so the coordinate reconciliation holds; and the three-way same-meal-type enforcement is coherent rather than redundant, because the client only ever compares client values and the server only server values, so the lowercase/uppercase split cannot desync them. Every finding was at an edge. **The one that could lose data.** *Edit window* prefilled its `datetime-local` inputs with a **UTC** wall clock while the browser reads such a value back as **local** time, so an open-and-save that changed nothing subtracted the owner's UTC offset — **−5h30m per save in IST, −11h after two** — and a window ending within that margin landed in the past, answering `409 VOTING_CLOSED` to every tenant while the panel still showed OPEN. The edit path was simply the first place the value made a **round trip**; `defaultStart`/`defaultEnd` had carried the same bug since they were written, harmlessly, because nothing round-tripped them. Both directions now go through the pure `votingWindow.ts`, whose tests pin **no** timezone — mutating `process.env.TZ` would leak across vitest workers, so the drift is asserted as `getTimezoneOffset()` and holds in every zone. **The two that were asymmetries.** A drag on a PUBLISHED week was live, irreversible and unannounced, while the *smaller* tap-to-edit path — one weekday, not two — already had an undo, a toast and a blast-radius warning; a swap now fires `stayoToast.undo` naming both weekdays, undone by re-issuing the same call, because **a swap is its own inverse**. And the week is roughly **766px against ~590px** of usable phone height with no auto-scroll, so any two days not on screen together could not be joined by a drag at all — while a release over nothing returned in silence, indistinguishable from a broken feature. Misses now speak, and the picker sheet gained **"Move to another day"**, which fixes the reach, supplies the non-pointer equivalent [[Decisions#ADR-042|ADR-042]] point 6 requires (this branch had adopted points 1–5 and declined 6 while the hint line advertised dragging first), and lets that hint line lead with tapping. **Backend robustness.** Both swap writes are now conditional on the cell still holding the item the transaction read — atomicity does not *order* two overlapping swaps, and under READ COMMITTED A(c1↔c2) and B(c2↔c3) could both read before either committed and leave one item duplicated and the other lost. `voting-periods` stopped deciding "is this a new round?" with a read before the write (two concurrent first-opens notified everyone twice) and **claims** the round with a conditional update instead — a transaction would not have helped, since both readers still see no row under READ COMMITTED — and `notified` counts fulfilled results rather than attempts. The swap route also maps `FORBIDDEN:` to 403; the role gate admits ADMIN, who got a 500 quoting an internal string. **Four small ones:** the grip is disabled rather than unmounted while a swap is in flight (it was shifting all 28 chips sideways and back), is `aria-hidden` rather than carrying an `aria-label` on a role-less `<span>` where conforming AT drops it, no longer lets a cancelled **mouse** drag open the picker (the synthesised `click` lands on the common ancestor of grip and label, where `stopPropagation` on the grip can never reach it), and the hostel trigger's `max-w-[46vw]` became a px cap — at 1440px it resolved to 662px, wider than the whole 480px shell, so it stopped binding and a long name could push the header out again, which is the exact bug that class was added to fix. **No schema change, no migration, no new dependency.** Tests rose backend **183 → 187** pure / 11 files and frontend **233 → 242** / 12 files; 9/9 invariants pass, `tsc` unchanged at 500 and 40. **One gap stands:** the gesture wiring still has no automated test, because the frontend suite is node-only with no jsdom — which is also why both new fixes were written as pure, exported functions. See [[Food]], [[Bugs]], [[APIs]].
- **2026-08-05**: **The Food branch's edges, in one wave** — six Important and seven Minor findings from the final whole-branch review of `feat/food-phase-0-1`, no Critical (module page: [[Food]]; full write-up: [[Bugs]]). They shared one shape, and it is the shape a pure-function-only suite structurally cannot catch: **the tested cores were right and the wiring around them was not.** `toWeekGrid` handles a null grid; the component consuming that empty grid shipped **four dead *Fix* buttons** at the top of the module's busiest screen for any hostel with no schedule row — a new hostel, or *any* hostel on the 1st before the carry-forward cron runs — and again on every first paint while loading. `buildPublishChecks` was thoroughly tested and **two of its three inputs were wrong at the call site**: `votesConsidered={Boolean(voting.period)}` answers *"does a voting period exist"*, not *"was this week built from votes"*, so the pre-flight readout announced **"5 student votes used"** above Publish for a carried-forward week assembled from zero; and `voterCount` summed `food_votes` **rows**, which one tenant may hold several of. `decideRebuild` was exhaustively tested and **its call site's TOCTOU was not** — the status read sat 5–9 generator round-trips ahead of the `$transaction` acting on its decision, so a concurrent publish could have its month deleted, i.e. the Phase 0 data-loss path narrowed to a race window rather than removed. `createAndReturn` had **no test at all** and swallowed every error: a duplicate item name returns 409, the hook ate it, and the sheet cleared the input *before* checking the result — the seven-interaction dead-end this feature existed to remove, replaced by a two-interaction one saying less than the message it deleted. Worst in blast radius: the **Kitchen sheet ignored the selected hostel**, so a two-property owner reading Sri Lakshmi's week and tapping *Send to kitchen* got Sri Adithya's menu, under Sri Adithya's name, pre-filled into a `wa.me` share — wrong-property data leaving the product to a third party, with no picker on the screen and no route to the other hostel's sheet at all. And `FILL_GAPS` **rewrote provenance**: a purely additive fill flipped `source` to `GENERATED` and set `generated_from_voting_period_id`, making a carried-forward month claim it was built from student votes — which would have corrupted the very signal the votes check was being fixed to read. **What shipped:** `rewritesProvenance` on `RebuildDecision`, so only a full replace may claim authorship — expressed on the **policy object rather than inline in the route**, because the policy is the surface with tests; the published-status guard **re-asserted inside the transaction**, aborting with the same `409 SCHEDULE_PUBLISHED` (the early check stays, for the cheap common-case rejection); the cron's voting-expiry sweep pushed into SQL (`voting_ends_at: { lte: now }`) instead of scanning every OPEN period in the system, with `shouldAutoClose` deliberately **retained** as the pure tested unit. On the frontend the votes predicate became **`hasVotesApplied(schedule)` — pure and tested** over `generated_from_voting_period_id` rather than a boolean in JSX, the point being that next time this is a test failure and not a review finding; `voterCount` → `voteCount`; the consecutive-days scan **wraps Sunday→Monday** (one row per `(day, meal)` means the week repeats all month, so Sunday's lunch really is followed by Monday's, four times over); and variety now names **every** dominated meal type rather than only the worst — the motivating live menu was Dosa ×7 breakfast *and* Sambar Rice ×7 lunch, and reporting one silently endorsed the other. Plus: Today card loading and no-schedule states, the kitchen sheet's `?hostelId=` thread-through **with a switcher on the screen**, a spoken add-item failure that keeps the typed name unless it succeeded, the hostel name on the Home food row once there is more than one, and an undo toast that no longer fires on no-op edits nor lets a failed revert pass for a success. Finally the **`WeekGrid` contract** (design §8.1, binding) was closed in its own largest consumer: the week **editor** still read a second, raw-row `grid` projection built beside `weekGrid` in the same hook — two projections of one thing, which is what made the bypass invisible at the call site — and open-coded a filled-ness test that disagreed with `isFilled`. That projection is deleted, day order has one definition, and the three spellings of the empty cell ("Empty", "Not set", "not set") collapsed onto one `EMPTY_CELL_LABEL`. **No schema change, no migration, no new endpoint.** Tests rose backend **173 → 176** pure and frontend **219 → 225**; 9/9 invariants pass; `tsc` at baseline both apps (backend 500, frontend 40); build and architecture check clean. See [[Food]], [[Bugs]], [[Decisions#ADR-048|ADR-048]].

### Added
- **2026-08-05**: **Food module — Phases 0 and 1 of the redesign** ([[Decisions#ADR-048|ADR-048]]; module page: [[Food]]). Stop destroying, stop pretending, then win the ten seconds an owner actually spends here. **What was broken.** *Regenerate* set `status: "DRAFT"` and `deleteMany`'d all 28 meal rows unconditionally, so one tap next to the green PUBLISHED badge — no confirmation, and unlike first-time Generate, no `canGenerate` gate — emptied every tenant's Food tab, because the tenant read filters on `status: "PUBLISHED"`. The route's own comment claimed regeneration was *"always safe."* `food-carry-forward` was 95 lines of correct, transactional, idempotent, `CRON_SECRET`-protected code that `vercel.json` **never scheduled** — so the 1st of every month started with no schedule at all. Voting periods had no way to close themselves, which dead-ends the owner permanently, since Generate is gated on a CLOSED period. And the entire **Food Polls** tab was `useState(mockFoodPolls)` — writing nothing, `totalTenants: 180` hardcoded in two places, date fields rendered as `<div>`s, "Edit" a toast, "Smart Insights" four hardcoded strings — while being the **Home screen's Quick Action**, so the most discoverable food feature was the one that did nothing and the real voting system sat unnamed on the other tab. **Verified against live production data, and it changed three conclusions:** `CARRIED_FORWARD` count **0** proved the cron had never run rather than merely gone unused; **1 voting period ever and 2 votes total**, with August generated at `generated_from_voting_period_id: null`, made voting inert in practice rather than merely thin; and **7 of 84 meal rows were `"Not set"`** — all snacks, published to tenants — making the bad generated menu live, not theoretical. Also 2 active hostels both with libraries, only one reachable. **What shipped.** `decideRebuild` (pure) makes the destructive path unreachable rather than merely discouraged: `generate` gained `mode: BUILD | FILL_GAPS | START_OVER` and answers `409 SCHEDULE_PUBLISHED`, with **`FILL_GAPS` the only mode allowed against a published month** because it is purely additive; a test enumerates every `(mode × status)` pair and asserts none can write `PUBLISHED`. The cron is registered at `0 1 * * *` and its daily loop now also closes expired voting. Food Polls is **deleted** (~842 lines net) and the Quick Action repointed and relabelled — **reversing [[Decisions#ADR-029|ADR-029]] point (3)'s "keep both", authorised explicitly by the user after the audit; no capability was lost, because there was none.** Then Phase 1: a **Today card** leading the tab (current meal as the hero, chosen by `mealSlotAt()`, with an unset slot offering *Fix* straight into that cell), the same line on owner **Home** (rendering nothing when there is no answer — a food gap belongs in the Action Center, Phase 2), **inline add-item** in the picker (seven interactions → two), **publish pre-flight checks**, an **undo toast** on live edits naming the weekday blast radius, a **hostel picker** closing an `hostels[0]` violation ([[Decisions#ADR-003|ADR-003]]), and a **Kitchen sheet** at `/owner/food/kitchen` — print plus a `wa.me` share, serving two personas who had nothing, with no backend and no Meta template. **What was reused rather than rebuilt:** `assignWeekForMealType` (kept verbatim; this branch gave it its first tests), publish atomicity and idempotency, `item_name` denormalization, library soft-delete, tenant read isolation, the vote-toggle model, `stayoToast`, and the `wa.me` pattern from `searchActions.ts`. The publish checks are **arithmetic over the week already in hand** — no endpoint, no service, no model — and return only `PASS | WARN`, asserted by test, so **checks inform and can never block**. The **`WeekGrid`** contract (design §8.1, binding) is the one shape every week-reader consumes, so a week's *producer* can change without consumers changing — which is what makes saved templates (Exam Week / Festival / Holiday) additive later rather than a redesign; all four new grid readers consume it. Three doc/copy corrections rode along, each of which had been asserting the opposite of the code: the `food_votes` schema comment said single-choice (the constraint includes `menu_item_id` precisely to allow multi-select), the meals-PATCH comment never said that editing one cell changes that weekday for the **whole month**, and the tenant app said *"Vote for next month"* while the endpoint votes into the **current** one — the label was made true rather than the behaviour changed, deliberately. **Food went from 0 tests to 63** (22 backend pure, 41 frontend); suites now 173 backend / 10 files and 219 frontend / 10 files, all passing. Every new test file was added to `vitest.pure.config.ts`'s explicit include list — it is a glob-free array, so a file left out is silently skipped while the run still reports success. **Explicitly not built (Phases 2–3):** `serve_date` and per-date overrides, so festival and exam-week menus stay unrepresentable and *"what did we serve on the 14th"* remains unanswerable; menu templates; `is_manual` cell locks; the `food-memory-service` read model and everything that composes it; Action Center food cards; Universal Search food source; cost per meal; and the one-tap "Ask students" move of voting to the month being planned. See [[Food]], [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-05**: **Food module audit — no code changed** (`docs/audits/food-module-audit.md`). Full lifecycle audit (library → planning → publish → tenant → voting → kitchen → history → analytics) against live code and live data, before any redesign. Recorded here because the vault had **no dedicated [[Food]] module page and no [[Business-Rules]] entry** for it. *(An earlier version of this entry claimed the vault had "no Food coverage at all" — that was **wrong**, based on a truncated grep: [[APIs]] already documented every `/api/food/*` endpoint and [[Database]] all five `food_*` models. Corrected here and in the audit's §8 item 8.)* Headlines: there is **no monthly menu** — `food_schedule_meals` is keyed `[schedule_id, day_of_week, meal_type]`, so a month is one Mon–Sun pattern with no date column, making festival/exam/one-off menus unrepresentable and blocking cost-per-day, kitchen sheets and real history; **Regenerate destroys the live menu** — the generate upsert sets `status: "DRAFT"` and `deleteMany`s all 28 cells, so one untriggered-by-confirmation tap next to the PUBLISHED badge empties every tenant's Food tab (the route's own comment claims it is "always safe"); and the **entire Food Polls tab is `useState(mockFoodPolls)`** — ~600 lines writing nothing, promoted by a Home Quick Action, while the real voting system sits unnamed on the other tab. Also: `food-carry-forward` is a correct, transactional, idempotent cron that **`vercel.json` never schedules** (live `CARRIED_FORWARD` count: 0); the Food tab passes `session.primaryHostelId` (`hostels[0]`) with no picker, violating the "never fall back to first hostel" invariant the backend check enforces but cannot see; voting is real but **inert** — 1 period and 2 votes ever, and August's menu generated with `generated_from_voting_period_id: null`; and there are **zero food tests**, including for `assignWeekForMealType`, a pure function doing largest-remainder maths. Verified correct and explicitly **not** to be rebuilt: publish atomicity/idempotency, the generator heuristic, `item_name` denormalization, library soft-delete, tenant read isolation. See [[Bugs]], [[TODO]].
- **2026-08-05**: **Expense memory — Phase 1 of the Expenses redesign** ([[Decisions#ADR-047|ADR-047]]). The Add Expense wizard opened on an empty form whose helper text read *"Suggestions come from your past entries"* — a promise nothing kept. The audit found why, and it was the **tenth built-but-never-wired feature in this codebase**: `getFrequentExpenses` and `getExpenseTitleSummary` were fully implemented, exposed via `mode=suggestions`/`mode=title_summary`, **and had frontend wrappers** — with no caller anywhere. The `expenses` table already carried vendor, payment method, receipt, recurrence and notes. So Phase 1 was mostly wiring plus one extension: `getExpenseMemory` on the **existing** service, as another `mode` on the **existing** route — no new service, no new endpoint. It adds average/highest amount, notes, recurrence, receipt rate and day-of-month history, plus a **vendor-keyed** view so typing a supplier works as well as typing what was bought. The first wizard step now opens on the owner's most-recorded expenses, narrows as they type, and one tap reuses everything they supplied last time before jumping to the amount — **reuse → edit → save**. **Nothing is invented**: fields the owner never filled stay empty, and reuse prefills the *last* amount rather than the average, which is a number that never actually occurred. Prediction is historical only — `typicalDayOfMonth` uses the median so one mistimed entry can't drag it, and returns null below three occurrences or when dates are scattered, because a wrong nudge is worse than none. A test caught the due-now ranking bonus quietly losing to frequency, contradicting its own comment. 29 new pure tests (144 backend, 178 frontend); invariants pass; `tsc` at baseline both apps. **Verified live** against 11 real expenses: typing "rice" returns the vendor *Sri Rice traders* with category, payment method, average and last purchase — the spec's own example — and correctly claims no monthly pattern from two occurrences. **Phases 2–4 not built.** See [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-05**: **Every Action Center card now leads to a work queue — Phase 3** ([[Decisions#ADR-046|ADR-046]]). After Phase 2 only Collect Rent went anywhere; Review Agreements, Activate Tenants and Fill Vacant Beds were **structurally incapable** of being tapped, because `StatCard` had no `onClick` prop at all. Dead affordances six, seven and eight, sitting beside a hero card that did navigate. Per explicit direction — *"do not invent different interaction models for different cards"* — the model is now enforced by **one component** rather than by discipline: `WorkQueue` owns the header, the four view states, sections, numbered rows, the reason line and the action bar, and a queue supplies only data and actions. **The Collect Rent screen was refactored onto it too** rather than left as the odd one out, shrinking 9.08 kB → 4.94 kB. **No new backend for any of the three**: agreements reuse `renewalDecisionService.getOwnerRenewalQueue` (which already ranks by decision state and days overdue), activations reuse `usePendingActivations` and the backend activation state machine, and vacancy composes the existing hostel-scoped `GET /api/rooms` fanned out across hostels. `PendingActivationsPage` was rebuilt on the shared component — it previously had no prioritisation and no inline actions, so it could show who was stuck but not let the owner act without opening each profile; it now sorts longest-waiting-first with Call/WhatsApp. **No progress bar or daily target**, per explicit product direction — completion is the row leaving the list. Two bugs caught during implementation: the renewal payload exposes `hostel_id` but no hostel name (the subtitle would have rendered empty), and agreements needed first-match-wins section assignment because the backend tags one agreement with several states at once. **Cross-checked live**: the vacancy queue independently computes 2 rooms / 2 free beds, matching the Home card exactly, so a card and the queue it opens cannot disagree. 178 frontend tests pass; build clean; `tsc` at baseline. **Phase 4 (one merged daily task list) not built.** See [[Features]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-05**: **Intelligent Collection Queue — Phase 2 of the owner navigation model** ([[Decisions#ADR-045|ADR-045]]). The Action Center's "Collect Rent" card showed ₹1,08,600 owed and a `›` chevron — with **no `onClick`**. Fifth dead affordance of that class. It now opens a real work queue at `/owner/money/collect`. **Audit first:** `/api/owner/finance/collections` turned out to be a 410 tombstone, `getDuesReport` is per-obligation and hostel-scoped where a queue is per-tenant and portfolio-wide, and `getTenantDues` is per-tenant async (N+1). The right primitive already existed — `financialService.getTenantPaymentSummary`, sync and documented as taking pre-fetched rows for exactly this batch case — so **no financial figure is computed twice**: outstanding and last payment come from it, overdue-ness from the settlement planner's `isOverdue()`, last reminder from `reminder_logs`. Fixed **4-query budget** regardless of tenant count. Tenants are grouped into **Needs immediate attention → Due today → Waiting after reminder → Due soon** and ordered within each by a score where **every point is attributed** (`factors[]` in owner language: "12 days overdue (+24)", "₹8,800 outstanding (+8)", "Has never paid (+15)"); the card shows the top two, a bottom sheet shows all, and a test asserts `score === sum(factors)`. **Live data caught a real design flaw before ship:** the reminder-cooldown rule put 7 of 10 tenants into "waiting" and ranked an 11-day-overdue tenant below a 12-day one, reporting almost no work while ₹59,000 sat uncollected — a severity cap now stops deferring once reminders have visibly failed. The architectural invariant also caught an optional hostel-id contract and was right to; the service now takes an explicit `hostelFilter: string | null`. `recommendation: null` ships on the contract so the future engine is a value change, not a redesign. Cards not tables, thumb-sized Collect/Call/WhatsApp on every row, lucide icons rather than emoji. 62 new tests (178 frontend, 115 pure backend); all 9 invariants pass; build clean; `tsc` at baseline both apps. **Verified against live data before and after the fix**: 3/7 across "needs attention"/"waiting" before, **4/6** after, with the 11-day-overdue tenant correctly promoted and the ₹84,500 total unchanged. **Phases 3–4 not built.** See [[Business-Rules]], [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-05**: **Universal Search — Phase 1 of the new navigation model** ([[Decisions#ADR-044|ADR-044]]). The Home search bar was a `<div>` wrapping a `<span>`: it looked like a field and did nothing. Behind it sat **two overlapping tenant-search endpoints** — `/api/owner/search` (labelled "for the global navbar", tenants only, no ranking, bare array outside the envelope, and **orphaned**: its wrapper `ownerService.searchTenants` existed but nothing called it) and `/api/payments/quick-collect/search` (good ranking, but ACTIVE-only and issuing a per-tenant obligations *and* ledger query per hit — 40 round-trips for 20 results). The orphaned path was rebuilt as the real thing; the payment one was deliberately left alone, since its cost profile suits a considered payment flow and not a typeahead. Search now covers **tenants** (name, four phone fields, room, hostel, roll number, email, and the word "invited"), **hostels** (name, city) and **rooms** (number), grouped and ranked on one shared scale — exact name → phone → room → hostel → prefix → contains → fuzzy. **The route and the client never name a result type**: sources are `SearchProvider`s in a single registry and every result carries its own `href`, so payments/complaints/expenses/staff can be added by writing a provider alone. Providers run under `Promise.allSettled`, so one failing source degrades its own group instead of blanking the search mid-keystroke. **A result is a workspace, not a link** — tenant rows carry inline Call · WhatsApp · Copy number · Collect · Profile, with Collect deep-linking into the existing QuickCollect flow with the tenant preselected, and each action shown only when it can actually work. Reuse over reimplementation: the hostel provider composes `portfolioService.getPortfolioSummary` so search occupancy matches the Home cards exactly, and the tenant provider composes `financialService.getTenantPaymentSummary` over one batched query so outstanding is never independently derived. A unit test caught a real bug pre-ship: a bare 10-digit query against a `+91`-stored number scored only as a weak *contains*; phone matching now compares the national 10-digit tail, verified live (`9845013001` → exact match on `+919845013001`). 45 new tests (151 frontend, 78 pure backend); build clean; invariants pass; frontend `tsc` at **499**, two *below* its baseline. **Phases 2–4 (Collect Rent queue, agreement/activation/vacancy queues, unified daily task list) are not built** — the Action Center still shows counts only. See [[Features]], [[APIs]], [[Bugs]].

### Changed
- **2026-08-05**: **Billing behaviour now has exactly one home, and collecting a payment no longer ambushes the owner.** Reported as "marking a payment says partial payments aren't allowed and I can't find any setting for it" — which turned out to be four problems. (1) `partial_payments.enabled` was fully enforced in `settlement-planner.ts` and defaulted to `false`, but appeared in **no UI at all** — the enforcement shipped years before any control did. (2) The review sheet showed the refusal *and* a complete success preview simultaneously ("1 installment left part-paid… a receipt is generated"), describing an outcome that couldn't happen. (3) **Three screens** each owned part of billing, and `MoreBillingPage` wrote `type: 'FLAT'` unconditionally while omitting `max_amount` — so opening it after setting a PERCENTAGE late fee with a cap **silently destroyed both**. (4) Three Configure screens pinned their save bar at `bottom-0`, putting the button **under the bottom nav**. Per explicit user direction the fix is structural, not a patch: a single canonical **Billing policy** screen now owns rent collection, security deposit, agreement duration, rent schedule and late fees; `MoreBillingPage`, `MoreConfigLateFeesPage` and `MoreConfigTenantDefaultsPage` are **deleted** with their routes redirected. A new **`minimum_percentage`** floor (0-100 of total outstanding) joins `minimum_amount` — both are floors so the larger wins, clamped to the outstanding balance so a big absolute floor can't strand a small final payment. The collect flow now states the policy **before** the amount is typed and, at review, **either** explains the block with a route to fix it **or** shows the outcome — never both. No screen or message names a backend flag; tests assert that. Also added **`npm run test:pure`** — the main vitest config imports `lib/db` and TRUNCATEs the `test` schema, so with no `DATABASE_URL_TEST` the whole suite is unrunnable *including pure functions*; `test:pure` runs 56 settlement tests with no database reachable, which is how the new percentage logic was actually verified. 17 new backend tests + 21 new frontend tests (128 frontend pass); invariants pass; `tsc` at its 501-error baseline. Three old assertions pinning the previous rejection wording were updated — deliberate, not a regression. See [[Decisions#ADR-043|ADR-043]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Features]], [[Bugs]].

### Added
- **2026-08-04**: **The hostel cards on Home can actually be reordered now, and sorted.** The `⠿` handle on each Property card had never done anything — `react-dnd`/`react-dnd-html5-backend` were in `package.json` but **imported nowhere**, no `DndProvider` was mounted anywhere in `src/`, and `DragHandle` was a decorative `aria-hidden` `<span>`; `hostels` had no ordering column either, so nothing could have been persisted even if it had worked. (And `react-dnd-html5-backend` would not have worked on a phone regardless — the HTML5 drag API doesn't fire on touch.) Per explicit owner-persona direction the fix is **both** manual control and metric sorting, not one or the other: a five-mode sort control (**My order · Most dues · Most vacant · Top revenue · Name**), with drag live **only** in "My order" — the handle is hidden in metric modes, so there is never again a dead affordance. Drag starts from the handle only (`dragListener={false}` + `useDragControls` + `touch-none`), which is what preserves both the card's tap-to-drill and the page's vertical scroll on touch. Manual order persists **server-side** in the new nullable `hostels.display_order` via `PATCH /api/owner/hostels/reorder` (full-list, one transaction, `403` on a foreign id + `HOSTEL_SCOPE_VIOLATION` log, `409 STALE_ORDER` on a partial list), not `localStorage` — an order that differed between the owner's phone and desktop would read as broken. **No new dependency**: `motion` was already installed and used, and `react-dnd`/`react-dnd-html5-backend` were removed as dead weight. **Move up / Move down** in the ⋮ sheet is the keyboard/screen-reader path, since dragging is inherently pointer-only. Two card-polish fixes rode along: the occupancy pill now tones by threshold (it was hardcoded `success`, so 75% and 88% rendered identically green — a status-shaped decoration carrying no status), and dues are emphasised red only when non-zero. 22 new frontend tests (107 pass); backend invariants pass; `tsc` back at its 501-error baseline, i.e. this change adds none. **Backend tests written but never executed** — `DATABASE_URL_TEST` is defined nowhere and `.env.test` doesn't exist, so the whole backend suite is unrunnable here (pre-existing; an untouched existing test fails identically); behaviour was verified live against the real API instead. See [[Decisions#ADR-042|ADR-042]], [[Database]], [[APIs]], [[Features]], [[Bugs]].

### Changed
- **2026-08-04**: **Backend latency — serverless functions pinned to `bom1`, and Prisma `relationJoins` enabled.** The owner Tenants screen was taking ~10.9s. The waterfall looked like slow queries, but measurement said otherwise: `GET /api/tenants` unauthenticated (401, no DB) took **117ms** while `GET /api/health` (same path + one `SELECT 1`) took **1388ms** — so a single trivial query cost **~1271ms**, and function invocation was never the problem. `x-vercel-id: bom1::iad1::…` gave the cause: `apps/backend/vercel.json` declared no `regions`, so functions ran in `iad1` (Washington DC) against a database in `ap-south-1` (Mumbai). Two changes shipped separately, each deployed and re-measured before the next: (1) `"regions": ["bom1"]` — config only, DB round-trip **~1271ms → ~28ms**; (2) `previewFeatures = ["relationJoins"]` — schema flag only, no service code touched, since `join` becomes the default relation load strategy on PostgreSQL once enabled (confirmed by measuring that an unspecified strategy emits the same query count as an explicit `join`), taking the `getAllTenants` `findMany` from **14 SQL queries to 4**. `query` vs `join` result equivalence was verified across every hostel before deploying. **Service-code optimisation was deliberately not done** — `tenant-service.ts` is unchanged, and whether it is still justified will be decided from measurements rather than assumed. Full measured baselines, plus an explicit list of what remains *unmeasured* (notably `GET /api/tenants` end-to-end HTTP latency, which needs an authenticated session), are in the new [[Performance]] page. One measurement round was nearly reported as an 80× regression before `curl`'s `time_namelookup` showed the ~15.1s was a local DNS resolver timing out, identically on unrelated hosts — the page documents the DNS-bypass method so this is not re-encountered. See [[Decisions#ADR-041|ADR-041]], [[Performance]].

### Fixed
- **2026-08-04**: Admin Console's Hostels screen now shows multi-hostel ownership, per explicit user request ("we need to know how many hostels some person is running right now"). `GET /api/platform-admin/hostels` already queried `owner_id` per row but never returned it — now returns `owner_id` + a platform-wide (unfiltered) `owner_hostel_count`, so every hostel card shows a "N hostels" badge next to the owner's name when they run more than one. The detail panel (`GET /api/platform-admin/hostels/[id]`) gained the same fields plus `sibling_hostels` — a clickable list of every other hostel that owner runs, letting the admin jump between a person's hostels from any one of them. No schema changes; `hostels.owner_id` already existed. See [[Features]], [[APIs]].
- **2026-08-02**: Owner app dead-button pass — see [[Bugs]] for the full writeup. "+ Add hostel" now opens a new lightweight `AddHostelModal` bottom sheet (Hostel Name/Address/City/State/Pincode/Contact Number, per explicit follow-up direction — floors/rooms/rent are added afterward via the existing Rooms tab, not up front) instead of the full 12-step onboarding wizard; submits to `POST /owner/hostels`, a real endpoint with no prior frontend caller, gated by a password-confirmation step (new `CREATE_HOSTEL` purpose on `/api/auth/confirm-identity`, one-line backend addition — the only backend change in this whole pass). The property card's kebab menu opens a new `HostelOptionsSheet` (real "Edit hostel details" scoped to that specific hostel via new `/owner/more/hostel/:hostelId` route, real "Archive hostel" via a new `useArchiveHostel` hook calling the pre-existing `DELETE /hostels/:id`); `TenantActionsSheet`'s Share Payment Link/Create Charge/View Receipts/Request Change/Change Billing Frequency now call real, pre-existing flows (`ChangeFrequencyModal`, `CreateObligationModal`, `useTenantActions().sharePaymentLink`, the Activity tab, `ChangeRequestDrawer`) instead of a toast; Room Sheet's "Edit room details" now calls the real `PATCH /rooms/:id` via a new `updateRoom` mutation on `useHostelRooms`; Settings' Tenant Defaults row, About's Privacy Policy/Terms of Service (real `/legal/*` pages) and Licenses (new static OSS-attribution sheet), Help's Email us (`mailto:`), and the dashboard's "View Leads" (now "Invite Tenant", opens the real invite wizard) are all wired for real. See [[Features]], [[Bugs]], [[APIs]].

### Added
- **2026-08-01**: Owner app's "Configure" bottom-nav tab now opens a real Configuration hub (`/owner/more`) instead of the old flat More-tab list — closes the gap the 2026-07-27 UI-fidelity pass explicitly deferred ("Workspace Configuration" stayed a "Coming soon" toast since its `Stayo Config.dc.html` deep-link target didn't exist as a route). Phase 1 ships two real modules, **Hostel** and **Finance** — search overlay, a completion ring/needs-attention feed computed from genuine data gaps (never a deliberately-off toggle), hostel identity/refund-policy/tenant-defaults, and a real 3-charge-type (FLAT/PERCENTAGE/PER_DAY) late-fees screen superseding `MoreBillingPage.tsx`'s FLAT-only toggle (kept, unlinked, not deleted). A static, honest Payment Gateway screen replaces the mockup's fake Razorpay/Cashfree "Connect" flow, since no self-serve gateway-connect capability exists. Agreements/Automation/Notifications/Account & Team are deliberately not in this phase's module grid — see [[Features]] for the full real/not-real breakdown per module. All reads/writes go through the existing `useHostelPolicy`/`useUpdateHostelPolicy` → `PATCH /hostels/:id/preferences`, so this phase needed zero new backend endpoints. `MorePage.tsx` (the old list, including two fake "Coming soon" Agreements/Properties stub tiles) is deleted; Settings/Help & Support/About StayO/Sign out are preserved in a new section at the bottom of the hub. Live-verified against the real dev database via authenticated API calls, which caught and fixed one real bug before shipping: the Tenant Defaults deposit-months stepper allowed 0, but the backend requires 1–12 (confirmed live via a rejected PATCH). See [[Features]], [[Backend]].
- **2026-07-30**: Admin console header bell is now real instead of a static "No notifications yet." placeholder. `GET /api/platform-admin/notifications` (new, admin-only) returns the 20 most recent lead/hostel/paid-invoice events via a new shared `composeRecentActivity()` (`lib/services/platform-admin-activity-service.ts`) — extracted from the Dashboard's existing inline Recent Activity computation so both surfaces read from one function, not two independently-drifting queries; the Dashboard route was refactored to call it too, with no change to its own output (verified — same 7 items live before/after). `AdminAppShell.tsx`'s bell now polls every 60s (`staleTime: 30s`), shows a red dot when unread items exist, and renders the real list (title/subtitle/relative time) in place of the placeholder. See [[APIs]], [[Features]].
- **2026-07-29**: Owner-acquisition funnel, phase 2 — the Admin Console's "Approve Lead" now does something real instead of only changing a status label. `POST /api/platform-admin/leads/[id]/approve` (new, replaces a bare status PATCH) generates a single-use activation token (`platform_lead_invitations`, mirroring `tenant_invitations`' proven shape — new table, not shared code, since a lead has no tenant/hostel/room context yet), sends it via WhatsApp with an email fallback (`MetaWhatsAppProvider.sendOwnerActivation`/`EmailService.sendOwnerActivation`, new), and logs every step. `PlatformLeadStatus` replaced outright with a real business-progress lifecycle (`NEW → UNDER_REVIEW → APPROVED → INVITE_SENT → OWNER_ACTIVATED → HOSTEL_CREATED → LIVE`, or `LOST`) — `APPROVED` onward is enforced server-side as system-managed, not just a UI convention (the admin PATCH endpoint now rejects manual writes to those values). New public pages/routes: `/owner-invite/:token` (`OwnerLeadInvitePage.tsx`, distinct invalid/expired/already-used states) → `/onboarding`, now prefillable via router state (`useOwnerOnboardingState` gained an optional `initialData` param and a new `city` field it didn't have before). Three real auto-progression choke points, no admin clicks or polling: owner-signup (`OWNER_ACTIVATED`, plus `platform_leads.converted_owner_id` — present in the schema since phase 1 but never actually written until now, see [[Bugs]]), hostel creation (`HOSTEL_CREATED`), and a small trailing call after the onboarding wizard's publish step finishes (`LIVE` — the one status with no natural server-side hook, since floor/room creation is a frontend-orchestrated loop). New Timeline section in the Admin Lead drawer, reading `systemEventLog` (`LEAD_CREATED`/`LEAD_APPROVED`/`LEAD_INVITE_SENT`/`LEAD_INVITE_OPENED`/`LEAD_OWNER_ACTIVATED`/`LEAD_HOSTEL_CREATED`/`LEAD_LIVE`) filtered by a `lead_id` key inside its JSON `metadata` field (that table has no dedicated `lead_id` column — a deliberate reuse-over-schema-change tradeoff, see [[Decisions#ADR-032|ADR-032]]). Full lifecycle verified live end-to-end against the real dev database (lead → approve → activation-context fetch → real owner-signup with the token → hostel creation → publish-complete → all 6 timeline events present in order); a normal non-lead-originated `/onboarding` signup was confirmed unaffected. **Known environment gaps flagged, not fixed** (pre-existing, outside this change's scope): WhatsApp needs a new Meta-approved template that doesn't exist yet; the dev environment's `RESEND_API_KEY` is invalid, so email delivery currently fails too — both correctly leave the lead at `APPROVED` with the real error surfaced rather than lying about a successful send. See [[Decisions#ADR-032|ADR-032]], [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Frontend]], [[Backend]], [[Features]].
- **2026-07-28**: Owner-acquisition funnel, phase 1 — the landing page's "Manage My Hostel" CTA now runs a real flow instead of a fully mocked one. `shared/ui-patterns/GoogleSignInModal.tsx` swapped its hardcoded fake account for a real `supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({provider:'google'})` redirect to a new page, `app/pages/public/LeadSignupCallbackPage.tsx`, which reads the Google identity client-side and never resolves it into a StayO session (by design — `resolveSupabaseSession()` is built to reject exactly that "no account yet" case, correct for real login, wrong for a brand-new lead) — the Supabase session is signed back out once the flow completes. `features/owner-onboarding/components/HostelLeadModal.tsx` similarly swapped its fake `setTimeout` OTP step for real calls: `POST /auth/send-phone-otp`/`verify-phone-otp` (`purpose: LEAD_CAPTURE`) then a new public `POST /api/leads/self-serve`, which requires a fresh verified OTP for that phone (the actual anti-spam gate, same pattern `/api/auth/owner-signup` already uses) and creates a real `platform_leads` row, immediately visible in the Admin Console's existing Leads page. New `google_email` column on `platform_leads`. New `features/hostel-leads/api/index.ts` wrapper. Verified live end-to-end via the real OTP demo-mode bypass (logs the code to console/file since WhatsApp isn't configured) through to a real row appearing in the admin API.

### Changed
- **2026-08-05**: **The public landing page is now owner/staff-only** — per explicit user direction, V1 does not ship a tenant-facing product, and most site visitors are tenants/students, so showing marketplace UI (even the existing static/demo one) misleads that majority. See [[Decisions#ADR-049|ADR-049]] for full reasoning. Removed from `app/pages/public/LandingPage.tsx`: the hero's "Find Your Stay"/"Manage Your Stay" role tab, the "Tenant Journey" steps card, the student testimonials column, and the "Tenant rating" trust stat (replaced with owner-relevant stats: hostels onboarded, rent collected monthly, owner rating). The live hostel search/enquire demo section is replaced by the existing "Get Verified" panel standalone; `HostelDiscoveryDemo.tsx` and `FilterSelect.tsx` are deleted outright (no longer referenced anywhere). `HeroShowcase.tsx` lost its `active` prop/tenant-listings branch and always renders the owner dashboard mock. The nav/hero "Login" flow now opens `LoginModal` in `mode="owner"` (login-only) instead of `mode="tenant"` — role routing (`handleAuthSuccess`) is unaffected, since it already dispatched on the API's returned role rather than the modal's mode. `FEATURES` grew from 3 owner + 3 tenant cards to 6 owner-only cards (added WhatsApp Rent Bot, Multi-Hostel Dashboard, Automated Late Fees — all pre-existing real capabilities, not new copy for unbuilt features). `MarketingFooter.tsx`'s tagline no longer references a marketplace. No backend/API/schema changes; the authenticated tenant app (`platforms/tenant`) and tenant login itself are untouched. See [[Decisions#ADR-049|ADR-049]], [[Frontend]].
- **2026-08-01**: **The post-activation "Admission Confirmed" WhatsApp message now delivers too.** Same bug class as the invitation template, one flow over: `ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_TEMPLATE_NAME` was `tenant_onboarding_completed_v1`, **a template absent from this WABA**, and `whatsapp-onboarding-handler.ts` hardcoded `languageCode: "en_IN"` against a template published as `en` — so every tenant who completed onboarding got nothing. Audited before changing anything: the body mapper `buildTenantOnboardingTemplatePayload` was **already correct**, producing six parameters in exactly the template's `{{1}}`–`{{6}}` order, so it was reused unchanged rather than reimplemented — only the two wrong values moved. New `onboarding-template-contract.ts` follows the invitation module's pattern with one deliberate inversion: this template's "Open Dashboard" button is a **static** URL (`https://yourstayo.com/`, no placeholder), and Meta rejects a button component supplied for a URL with no variable — so the contract declares zero button parameters and the drift check treats a button that *gains* a placeholder as the error, where the invitation template treats a *missing* button as the error. `npm run check:whatsapp-template` now gates all three templates (OTP, invitation, onboarding-complete) and passes live for each. **Verified with a real send** to a real handset: params `['Shiva','Delux Hostel','G4','21/06/2025','8,500','7']`, delivered first attempt. 16 new tests; four existing assertions in `whatsapp-onboarding-notification.test.ts` were updated — including one named *"should be tenant_onboarding_completed_v1"* — because they had been pinning the broken name and language. See `docs/audits/whatsapp-template-mapping.md`.
- **2026-08-01**: **Tenant invitations actually deliver on WhatsApp now.** Every invitation had been failing at Meta, invisibly until the invite wizard stopped claiming false success. Two causes, both confirmed by reading the live Graph API rather than the WhatsApp Manager UI: the payload builder sent **four** body parameters (tenant, owner, room, rent) to `stayo_tenant_invitation`, which declares **two** (`{{1}}` tenant, `{{2}}` hostel) — Meta `#132000`; and it defaulted to language `en_IN` against a template published as `en`, which Meta treats as a different template entirely — `#132001`. The default template name was also `tenant_account_activation_v2`, which does not exist in this WABA at all. New `invitation-template-contract.ts` declares the parameter *meanings* once and builds the payload from them, replacing an inline `components` array that branched on the template *name* — precisely the drift trap that caused the earlier OTP incident, and which was about to get worse with a third template in play. It reuses `otp-template-contract.ts`'s `countPlaceholders`/`describeTemplateShape`/`fetchTemplate` rather than re-deriving them. `npm run check:whatsapp-template` now gates **both** templates and passes live for each. Retired names (`hms_tenant_invite_v2`, `tenant_account_activation_v1/v2`) are actively ignored even if still set in a deployed environment, rather than forwarded to Meta as a nonexistent template. **Verified with a real send** to a real handset through `MetaWhatsAppProvider.sendInvitation` (not a raw curl), delivered on the first attempt: `wamid.HBgMOTE4MDA4MDQ2OTUyFQIAERg…`. 18 new tests; one existing provider test was updated — it had pinned the nonexistent `tenant_account_activation_v2` in `en_IN` with four body parameters, so it had been asserting the bug. **Still broken and not fixed here:** `ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_TEMPLATE_NAME` is `tenant_onboarding_completed_v1`, also absent from this WABA; the approved `stayo_tenant_onboarding_complete` (`en`, 6 body params, no button) needs wiring before the post-activation message works. See `docs/audits/whatsapp-template-mapping.md`, [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-01**: **The owner can now see where every invited tenant is stuck** (audit Task 4; activation business logic unchanged). Tenant Detail showed only an "Invited" pill, and Home's "Activate Tenants" card — which counted real invited tenants — opened the **Invite** wizard, asking the owner to invite somebody else. The activation state machine already existed and is the source of truth, so nothing here re-derives it. **The reuse had to be one level below `getContext()`**: that method is keyed by the tenant's secret activation token *and mutates* — it resolves with `markOpened: true` and **auto-accepts the hostel rules on the tenant's behalf** when they haven't been accepted, so an owner opening a progress screen would have silently completed a step for the tenant and corrupted the state they were looking at. New `activationWorkflowService.getOwnerActivationState(tenantId, ownerId)` loads the same records owner-scoped and calls the same private `computeState()`, with no writes on any path; `GET /api/tenants/:id/activation-state` exposes it. Tenant Detail now shows the real current step ("Waiting for rules acceptance", "Ready to activate", "Activated"), completed steps struck through, blocked steps marked, the backend's own `progress_percent` (read, never recomputed — if the backend reweights, the UI follows), and the specific missing profile fields. New `/owner/tenants/activations` queue shows tenant, current step, waiting-since and KYC per row; a tenant drops out the moment `activation_completed` is true, without waiting for the list's status string to catch up. **KYC stays an independent state machine** — rendered as its own `KYC Pending`/`KYC Verified` badge, never part of any step calculation, and explicitly tested to confirm unverified documents don't block reaching `ACTIVATE`. 13 new backend tests (state reuse, owner isolation, and three asserting the read performs no writes) + 25 new frontend tests. See [[Features]], [[APIs]].
- **2026-08-01**: **The owner can now approve or reject a tenant's KYC — the step onboarding used to die on** (audit item P0-4; **frontend only, zero backend changes**). Tenants could upload documents that nobody could ever act on. The cause was not a missing backend: `PATCH …/documents/:docId/verify`, `…/reject`, `GET …/download` and `GET /api/tenants/pending-documents` all existed, owner-scoped and correct, and `tenantService.verifyDocument/rejectDocument` already wrapped them with the right verb. What was missing was any UI reaching them — `VerificationPanel.tsx` had been orphaned by the 2026-07-26 salvage pass, so the Documents tab could render a status pill and nothing else. The requirement was to audit before building endpoints; the audit said build none. Tenant Detail → Documents is now a real review surface (View · Download · Approve · Reject, plus the previous rejection reason shown back to the owner), backed by a new pure `kycDocuments.ts` whose rules deliberately mirror the route guards — an archived doc answers 409 and an approved one has nothing left to do, so neither offers a button, while a REJECTED doc stays actionable because the tenant may have re-uploaded against the same row. Rejection **requires a reason**: the route already answered 400 without one, and since the reason is the only thing the tenant is shown, the sheet offers five one-tap common reasons plus free text and keeps Submit disabled until valid. New `/owner/tenants/verifications` queue grouped **by tenant** rather than by document, **oldest wait first** — that tenant's move-in is the one blocked — with a genuine success state ("All caught up") rather than a blank list at zero.
