Home's "Verify Pending KYC" card, which already counted real documents and did nothing on tap, now navigates there; its `actionCenter` prop was made **required** so it can no longer silently fall back to `mockActionCenter` counts. Approving invalidates **four** query keys together (pending documents, tenants list, portfolio, that tenant's detail) because approving the last required document flips `tenants.document_verified`, which four surfaces derive from independently — refreshing three would leave a visibly stale number. **Deliberately not built:** upload-on-behalf-of-tenant — it is in the problem statement but not the numbered requirements, and `/api/tenants/:id/documents` is GET-only, so it needs a new route plus ImageKit handling; flagged rather than smuggled in. **Already working, verified rather than rebuilt:** the tenant sees the rejection reason at `/tenant/profile/details`, which already parses the same JSON thread the reject route writes. 30 new frontend tests (pure logic) + 17 new backend tests — the latter are **characterisation tests** that passed on first run, pinning already-correct behaviour on routes that had none: cross-owner 403, tenant-role 401, archived 409, missing/whitespace/over-length reason 400, thread append not overwrite, `document_verified` flipped only when every required type is approved, and `file_url` never leaked to the client. See [[Features]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-01**: **The invite wizard stops telling owners an invitation was delivered when it wasn't** (audit item P0-1; frontend only, no backend change). `useInviteWizard.onSuccess` discarded the entire response and rendered *"Invitation sent! {name} will get a text to complete KYC."* on every 2xx — while the backend was already reporting `whatsapp_sent`, `whatsapp_error`, `email_sent`, `needs_email` and handing back the `activation_link`. The wizard also collected **no email address**, so the backend's email fallback could never fire. In production that reads: invite 20 tenants, see 20 success screens, nobody receives anything. **The trap:** both endpoints report delivery *failure* with a **2xx** — invite answers `202` when neither channel sent, and resend answers `202` with an *error-shaped* body (`{error:{code:'EMAIL_FALLBACK_REQUIRED'}}`). Axios resolves on 2xx, so neither rejects and a `try`/`catch` reads both as success; delivery state can only come from the body. New pure `inviteDelivery.ts` resolves it, treating anything unrecognised as **undelivered** rather than delivered (a string `"false"` doesn't count as sent). Three real result states — WhatsApp delivered · WhatsApp failed but email delivered (naming the address) · neither, which shows the reason, the activation link, and **Copy link / Share link** (a `wa.me` deep link) so the owner always has a way to onboard the tenant by hand. `needs_email` prompts for an address inline on the result screen and re-dispatches through the existing `POST /api/tenants/resend-invitation` — whose `overrides.email` was already honoured — so nobody restarts the wizard; the resend's error body carries no `activation_link`, so the original is carried forward rather than dropped. An optional Email field was added to step 1 (phone stays mandatory) and is echoed on the review step as "None — no fallback" when blank, so the risk is visible *before* sending. **`apps/frontend` had no test suite at all** — added `vitest` (node environment only; no jsdom, no testing-library, judged a larger footprint than the fix) plus `npm test`, and kept the decision logic pure so all of it is genuinely covered: 30 tests across the three delivery cases, `needs_email`, the 202 error-body reading, activation-link preservation, clipboard success/denial/absence, and share-URL building. Components are thin renderers over that tested state. **Not verified against live providers this session** — the WhatsApp-fails → email-succeeds path needs a real run. Manual check: invite with WhatsApp env unset and no email → expect the failure screen with a working Copy link, then add an email inline → expect "Invitation sent by email". See [[Features]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-08-01**: **Onboarding publish is now one atomic request instead of ~45, and stops discarding what it collects** ([[Decisions#ADR-040|ADR-040]]; audit item P0-3). The wizard's Publish step issued `1 + F + (F × R)` sequential calls against `/api/owner/hostels`, `/api/floors` and `/api/rooms` — 45 for the default 4-floor/10-room property — with no transaction. A failure at floor 3 committed a partially built hostel, and pressing Publish again hit the owner-scoped duplicate-name guard and returned `400 A hostel with this name already exists` **forever**, stranding the owner on step 11 of 12 with no way forward. New `POST /api/owner/hostels/provision` → `hostelProvisioningService.provision()` does the whole thing inside one `prisma.$transaction` (20s timeout; one batched `rooms.createMany` per floor, so 160 rooms is 8 statements rather than 160): every row lands or none does, so a failed publish leaves nothing behind and retrying just works. The duplicate check runs *before* the transaction and its `409` now carries `details.hostel_id`, so a client can offer to open the existing hostel — which also unsticks owners already holding a shell hostel from the old flow. Room numbering (`101…110`, `201…`) and floor naming (`Floor 1`) are byte-identical to the loop replaced, so nothing reading `room_no` shifts. **Four discarded fields now persist**: hostel type and "food available?" as new `hostels` columns, the security deposit into `preferences_config.billing_defaults` (reusing the preferences API's own `sanitizeBillingDefaultsPayload` rather than re-deriving the deposit/advance coupling), and the publish-vs-draft choice as `hostels.publish_requested`. That last one is deliberately **not** `listing_status` — that value is admin-controlled and paired with `verification_status = VERIFIED`, so writing it from onboarding would let any owner self-approve their public listing past platform verification. Rooms now carry a real `base_rent`: per explicit product direction the Details step gained a required **Starting monthly rent** field, and the schema rejects `null` (notably *not* via `z.coerce.number()`, which silently turns `null` into `0` — i.e. a free room; a test caught this). Two regressions caught by test rather than review: `null` base rent slipping through coercion, and the dropped `leadInvitationService.markHostelCreated` side effect — without it the wizard's trailing `/leads/invitation/:token/complete` is a no-op, since `markLive` only promotes a lead already at `HOSTEL_CREATED`, so every lead-originated owner would have silently stalled at `OWNER_ACTIVATED`. 30 new tests; all 9 architectural invariants pass; frontend build clean. See [[APIs]], [[Database]], [[Features]].
- **2026-07-28**: Authentication architecture migrated from a custom JWT+refresh-cookie system to **Supabase Auth as the single authentication provider**, per an explicit long-term architectural decision from the user (real Google login was the trigger — the previous raw Google OAuth2 implementation was found to have no working credentials — but the migration was scoped deliberately wider, not a point fix). Full reasoning, alternatives considered, and consequences in [[Decisions#ADR-031|ADR-031]]; architecture/flow detail in [[Architecture#Auth flow|Architecture]], [[Backend#Auth/session model|Backend]], [[Frontend#Auth|Frontend]]; endpoint-level changes in [[APIs#Auth|APIs]]; schema changes in [[Database]]. Business logic, roles (OWNER/TENANT/ADMIN), authorization rules, and all 243 `getSession()`-consuming routes were explicitly **not** touched — only the identity/session layer underneath them changed. Headline points:
  - `profiles.auth_user_id`/`auth_linked_at` link app profiles to `auth.users`, populated **just-in-time** on first successful login after this migration (no forced mass password reset) via a new `ensureSupabaseIdentity()`; a profile that never logs in again stays unlinked indefinitely — accepted, made measurable via `scripts/reconcile-supabase-identities.ts`.
  - `middleware.ts` verifies Supabase JWTs via JWKS (ES256) first, falling back to legacy HS256 verification during a dual-accept transition window — no forced logout on deploy, legacy tokens simply expire naturally (~12h).
  - Google login is now `supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth()` (full-page PKCE redirect) instead of a hand-rolled OAuth2 code exchange; `/api/auth/google-callback` and `/api/auth/refresh` are removed entirely (refresh is now client-SDK-driven).
  - Session revocation and the 30-minute idle-timeout business rule (both things Supabase doesn't natively provide) are deliberately preserved via the existing Redis layer, not dropped — logout now also calls `supabase.auth.admin.signOut()`, making revocation stronger than before, not weaker.
  - Found and fixed several real pre-existing bugs while touching this code (see [[Bugs]] for each): `changePassword` passed the wrong id to Supabase for any profile not 1:1-linked; `completePasswordReset` used unpaginated `listUsers()`; `/api/auth/onboarding-login` was missing from `middleware.ts`'s public-route allowlist; `middleware.ts` never cleared identity headers on paths where they shouldn't be set; two `createSessionAndTokens()` call sites (owner-signup, tenant-activation auto-login) would have thrown once the function started requiring a plaintext password.
  - Trade-off accepted and documented, not hidden: the refresh token moves from an httpOnly cookie to Supabase's client-side session storage — more XSS-exposed, an inherent consequence of a cross-origin Vite SPA using the client-side Supabase SDK (mitigated via a short access-token TTL and Supabase's built-in refresh rotation/reuse-detection).
  - Live-verified against the real Supabase project (not just code review): password login, JIT linking (including no-duplicate-user-on-second-login), `/auth/me`, and the reconciliation script, for all 4 UAT demo accounts (owner, admin, 2 tenants).

- **2026-07-27**: Tenant Food tab's menu display, per explicit user request — was a month-picker (pill tabs, one month's Monday–Sunday grid visible at a time, days stacked as full-width cards down the page); now every published month is shown at once, stacked oldest → newest as its own labeled section (the most recent tagged "Current"), with each month's 7 days laid out side by side in a horizontally-scrollable row instead of stacked cards, and no calendar/date grid (the schedule is a repeating weekly pattern, not calendar-dated — see the Monthly Food Planning entry below). `useTenantFoodSchedule` (`features/food/hooks/useTenantFoodSchedule.ts`) now fans the existing per-month `GET /food/tenant/schedule?month=` endpoint out across every month in `GET /food/tenant/schedule/history` via parallel `useQueries`, rather than fetching one selected month — no backend/API change. Still exposes a single current-month `grid` for `useTenantHome.ts`'s unrelated "today's meals" lookup, unchanged. `platforms/tenant/pages/TenantFoodPage.tsx` updated to match. See [[Features]].

### Fixed
- **2026-07-30**: Owner-activation emails (from [[Decisions#ADR-032|ADR-032]]) were building links against the old `sriadithyahostels.in` production domain instead of the local dev frontend — `getFrontendUrl()` falls back to that hardcoded constant whenever neither `NEXT_PUBLIC_FRONTEND_URL` nor `FRONTEND_URL` is set, and neither was set in the working `.env`. Fixed by setting `FRONTEND_URL="http://localhost:5174"`; no code change. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-30**: Admin Leads page's status/approve mutations only invalidated the `['admin','leads']` list query, not the open drawer's separate `['admin','lead-detail', id]` query — an approved/updated lead kept showing its pre-mutation status in the drawer until manually reopened, and a second click on the now-stale button 409'd. Both mutations now invalidate both keys. Also: `APPROVED` (a lead whose send failed) is now retryable via the same "Approve Lead" button (relabeled "Retry Send"), instead of getting stuck since only `NEW`/`UNDER_REVIEW` were approvable. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-28**: Admin login now actually lands on the Admin console instead of the Owner dashboard — fixes the bug diagnosed (but left unfixed) earlier in this session. Two compounding causes: `AuthContext.tsx`'s already-authenticated-on-`/login` redirect lumped `owner`/`admin` together into `/owner/home`; `LoginPage.tsx`'s fresh-login handler sent `owner` to a `/dashboard` route that was never registered in the router at all (worked by accident via a fallback chain through the catch-all route and the public landing page's own unrelated redirect). Both now branch correctly, and `OwnerProviderShell.tsx` no longer allow-lists `admin` (it never needed to — `/admin/*` was already independently guarded). See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-27**: Dev-mode CORS (`lib/config/domains.ts`'s `getCorsAllowOrigin()`) was blocking login/every API call whenever the frontend's Vite dev server came up on any port other than the two hardcoded in `.env`'s `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (5173/5174) — e.g. 5175, which happens any time an earlier dev-server instance is still holding 5174. Now reflects back any `localhost`/`127.0.0.1` origin verbatim outside production, instead of falling back to the production origin and getting blocked by the browser. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-27**: Owner portfolio/dashboard revenue and dues KPIs (`GET /api/owner/portfolio/summary`, both the per-hostel cards and the aggregate) fixed after showing ₹0 for any hostel without a same-day `hostel_daily_snapshots` row — three compounding bugs in `lib/services/hostel-daily-snapshot-service.ts`: the live-fallback path (`previewLive()`) hardcoded every money field to 0 instead of querying real data; the real snapshot-write path (`createSnapshot()`) never supplied the required `id` column so a snapshot row had in fact never once persisted successfully (silently swallowed by `Promise.allSettled`); and once both were fixed, `portfolio-service.ts` turned out to read a `capacity` field off the snapshot row that doesn't exist in that table's schema, zeroing `total_capacity`/`occupancy_rate`. All three fixed; `createSnapshot()` is now also idempotent (upsert on `(hostel_id, snapshot_date)` instead of `.create()`). Found via manual UAT click-through, not a data-seeding artifact — this affects every hostel on its first day. See [[Bugs]] for full root-cause detail.
- **2026-07-27**: Owner Tenants tab — the "All Hostels" combined view had no way to tell which hostel a given tenant belonged to (`TenantRow.tsx` never rendered the already-available `hostelName` field). Added an optional `showHostel` label, shown only when viewing "All Hostels" (`TenantsPage.tsx` → `TenantList` → `TenantRow`). See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-27**: Full UI fidelity migration — Admin, Owner, and Tenant apps brought to literal pixel/color/spacing parity with their `.dc.html` design mockups (`Stayo Admin Dashboard/Stayo Admin.dc.html`, `Stayo SaaS redesign1/Stayo App.dc.html`+`BottomNav.dc.html`+`Stayo Config.dc.html`, `Stayo SaaS redesign1/Stayo Tenant.dc.html`+`TenantNav.dc.html`), per an explicit user directive treating every difference from the design as a bug. No business logic, API, routing, or state-management changes — presentation only, with two intentionally-scoped functional exceptions noted below. Root-cause fixes applied globally first: `--background` token corrected from `#efe9e2` (mockups' unused outer-canvas color) to the real on-screen `#f7f3ef`; the invented header/logo bar previously added to `OwnerAppShell`/`TenantAppShell` (no basis in either mockup) was removed per explicit user confirmation; bottom-nav icon size/label/gap/inactive-color brought to spec on both shells. Per-screen literal-value passes then covered: Admin (Dashboard/Hostels/Leads/Revenue/More/Settings — cards, search bars, filter chips, the Hostels detail view converted to a slide-in drawer matching Leads' pattern per explicit user request), Owner (Home/Tenants/Money/Food/More/Configure — `StatCard` gained a `variant` prop for its two distinct mockup styles rather than a duplicate component), and Tenant (Home — added the missing dark hero header band; Money — added a previously-missing "Current installment" breakdown card and a "reversed payments" history section, both using real backend fields (`financial-read-model-service.ts`'s `rent_due`/`late_fees_due`/`maintenance_due`, and payment history's `is_reversal`), not fabricated; Room — hero card, Living Status tile tinting, Roommates avatar size, Room Services grid, House Rules wrapper all brought to spec; `PaySheet` gained the mockup's "Pay with" method selector (`BottomSheet` gained a `hideHeader` prop for this) — the selector is visual-only since PhonePe's hosted checkout, not this API, actually resolves payment method; `ServiceRequestSheet` gained the mockup's structured-input field type (Visitor Pass's name/phone/date/time), composed into the existing single `description` string since the real `tenant_service_requests` schema has no per-field columns for it). Several data-less UI elements found in the mockups were deliberately **not** built for lack of any real backing field (Tenant Home's hostel-name label, a "now serving" meal pill, Money's "new tenant" pill, Room's "Room facilities" section) — flagged as known gaps rather than faked. Explicitly deferred, not built this pass: the Tenant Food tab's 5 additional mockup sections (per-day special picker, personalization badges, mood/feedback picker, a separate research-poll card, tap-to-expand meal detail — large enough for its own pass), Owner More's "Workspace Configuration" destination screen (a functionality gap, not styling — the mockup's `Stayo Config.dc.html` deep-link target doesn't exist as a route yet), custom hand-drawn fill/outline bottom-nav icon SVGs (asset-authoring task, Lucide substitutes kept), `ServiceRequestSheet`'s bottom-drawer-vs-full-screen-panel container pattern (real interaction-pattern difference from the mockup, but a larger structural rework kept separate from the field-type fixes), and photo-attachment upload on maintenance requests (the API/DB do have a real `photoUrl` column, but no existing generic-image-upload endpoint — the one precedent, `/api/tenants/me/documents`, is scoped to identity-document types and reusing it as-is would misuse that validation, so this needs a small backend addition to do safely, out of scope for a presentation-only pass). See [[Frontend]] for the new `StatCard`/`BottomSheet` props.
- **2026-07-27**: Admin console deep audit vs. `Stayo Admin.dc.html` — all 6 screens (Dashboard/Hostels/Leads/Revenue/More/Settings) compared line-by-line against the design source; every gap found was either wired to real, already-available backend data that just wasn't surfaced, or (for the Dashboard's expanded KPI set, hostel-health stats, and new Revenue Summary card) composed from a small backend extension reusing existing calculation logic rather than duplicating it. Highlights: Hostels/Revenue gained real status-filter chips the backend already supported but the UI never exposed; Leads cards gained inline Call/WhatsApp actions and submitted timestamps; the Dashboard was rebuilt from a full-width-KPI-banner layout into the mockup's asymmetric 2-column grid with all 8 KPIs (was 5, none matching the mockup's labels) and a new Revenue Summary card; a real data-correctness bug was caught mid-build and fixed before shipping (Revenue's "Active Hostels" metric was counting *all* hostels regardless of listing status, and an early Dashboard KPI draft mistakenly sourced "Pending Dues" from tenant-owed rent instead of unpaid platform-subscription invoices — two different revenue domains). Two sub-scopes were explicitly deferred at the user's request, not built: hostel-detail Photos/Documents (no backing schema exists at all) and Revenue's Analytics stats block + date-range filter (need new backend time-windowed calculations). Full per-page breakdown, root causes, and what was deferred in [[Bugs]]; API response shape changes in [[APIs]].
- **2026-07-27**: Admin console mobile layout — `AdminAppShell.tsx` had no responsive handling at all (found by the user testing in Chrome mobile emulation: full desktop sidebar squeezed into a phone viewport, no way to navigate). The design source (`Stayo Admin.dc.html`) turned out to already specify a full mobile layout via `@media (max-width:900px)` — sidebar hidden, a floating pill bottom-nav shown instead — that was simply never built, despite the shell's own doc comment incorrectly claiming the omission was deliberate. Added the sidebar-hide + floating bottom-nav (reusing the same `ADMIN_TABS` list and the active-state visual pattern already used by `OwnerAppShell`/`TenantAppShell`'s bottom navs) at the mockup's exact 900px breakpoint. See [[Bugs]] for full root-cause detail; corrected the same stale "desktop-first, no bottom nav" claim in [[Features]] and [[Frontend]].
- **2026-07-27**: Stabilization pass over the Tenant App + Platform Admin Console work (below) — no new product features, cleanup/production-readiness only. Deleted 11 fully-dead legacy files from the frozen `src/portal` tree (superseded once Home/Money/Room were rebuilt for real) and updated `scripts/check-architecture.mjs`'s allowlist + `src/portal/README.md` to match. Wired the real, previously-orphaned `TenantPaymentReturnPage.tsx` at `/payment-return` (was a non-functional stub, even though the real PhonePe redirect-checkout flow needs it). Fixed a broken `["OWNER","ADMIN"]` auth-guard pattern copied into 8 new owner-scoped routes (the `ADMIN` branch could never actually succeed — `resolveOwnerScope()` throws for non-`OWNER` roles). Fixed a dead-value ternary in the Revenue "assign subscription" route that made the client-supplied `billingCycle` param have no effect on the stored `amount`; now derives cycle/amount directly from the selected plan. Added missing `onError` toasts to 9 previously-silent-failure mutations across `AdminHostelsPage`, `AdminSettingsPage`, `MoreServiceRequestsPage`, `MoreNoticesPage`. Fixed 4 stale ADR-018→ADR-030 cross-references left over from the earlier ADR-renumbering fix. Full details, root causes, and verification in [[Bugs]]. Also flagged two out-of-scope findings for a future pass (not fixed here): the same broken `OWNER+ADMIN` guard pattern exists pre-existing across ~100 other owner routes repo-wide (harmless/fail-closed, now technically "live" instead of theoretically dead since `ADMIN` became a real role), and the Revenue routes' unbounded cross-hostel `findMany` queries (correct for true platform-wide MRR/ARR today, worth moving to DB-side aggregation at scale).

### Added
- **2026-07-26**: StayO Platform Admin Console — a full multi-tenant SaaS platform-admin persona, built per `Stayo Admin Dashboard/Stayo Admin.dc.html` and per the user's explicit, informed choice to reverse ADR-006 (see [[Decisions]] ADR-030 for the full reasoning and the alternatives that were declined). `Role` gained a real, assignable `ADMIN` value for the first time. New schema: `platform_admins`, `platform_leads` (prospective hostel owners — distinct from the pre-existing tenant-admissions `leads` table), `subscription_plans`/`hostel_subscriptions`/`platform_invoices` (marketplace billing), `notification_templates`, `platform_settings`, plus `hostels.verification_status`/`listing_status`. ~18 new routes under `/api/platform-admin/*` (see [[APIs]]), all requiring a strict `ADMIN`-role session with no public self-serve signup (`scripts/bootstrap-platform-admin.ts` creates the first admin). New desktop-sidebar frontend shell (`AdminAppShell`, this persona's first departure from the owner/tenant apps' mobile-first bottom-nav chrome) with all 6 screens real: Dashboard (KPIs + Leads/Hostels previews + a derived Recent Activity feed), Hostels (list/detail, approve/suspend/reactivate listing, assign/change subscription plan), Leads (grid + detail drawer, status lifecycle, call/WhatsApp), Revenue (MRR/ARR/collected/pending/lifetime KPIs, per-hostel subscription cards, a real "record payment" action, CSV/GST exports), More, and Settings (General/Plans/Admin Users/Notification Templates). Seeded 3 real subscription plans (Starter ₹499/mo, Growth ₹1,499/mo, Premium ₹2,999/mo) via a one-off script. Found and fixed three real, previously-undiscovered bugs while building this (see [[Bugs]] for each): `notifications.id` had no schema default, so `NotificationService.createNotification()` had *always* silently failed everywhere in the codebase it was called, not just this new feature (fixed — added the default, matching house style); `GET /api/auth/me`'s `is_admin` field was hardcoded `false`; `LoginPage.tsx`'s post-login redirect sent `role === 'admin'` to the owner dashboard instead of `/admin`. Verified end-to-end against the live database via a **real password login** (not a minted token) for the bootstrapped admin account: approved/suspended/reactivated a real hostel listing, created and progressed a real lead through NEW→CONTACTED, assigned a real Growth subscription and recorded a real ₹1,499 payment (confirmed correct in MRR/ARR/collected KPIs and a GST export showing the correct 18% figure), invited a second admin (SALES title), created and toggled a notification template, saved general settings, and sent a real broadcast notification that both seeded owner accounts actually received (post-fix). See [[Features]], [[Database]], [[Decisions]] ADR-030.
- **2026-07-26**: StayO tenant app — all 5 tabs (Home, Money, Room, Profile; Food was already real) now real, per `Stayo SaaS redesign1/Stayo Tenant.dc.html`. New minimal content models `hostel_announcements`/`hostel_events` (owner-authored, Home tab), `tenant_service_requests`/`tenant_service_request_events` (unified model for 6 request types: maintenance/room-change/cleaning/lost-key/visitor-pass/extra-mattress, Room tab), `hostel_utility_status` (living-status grid), `hostels.house_rules` (new `Json?` column, house-rules accordion). ~15 new routes (see [[APIs]] — Tenant App section). Salvage-first: real logic from the frozen `src/portal` tree (`TenantFinancialsPage`, `TenantProfilePortalPage`, `TenantMoveOutPage`) and the previously-orphaned `TenantRenewalPage.tsx` was mounted into the live route tree rather than rebuilt from scratch, matching the owner-app convergence playbook below. New owner-side surfaces: `/owner/more/notices` (announcements/events authoring) and `/owner/more/service-requests` (assign/progress/resolve/reject queue) — without these the tenant-facing features would have nothing to read or be write-only. Found and fixed two real bugs along the way (see [[Bugs]]): every tenant login/activation redirected to a dead `/tenant/dashboard` route (now `/tenant/home`), and `payment-service.ts`'s module-level Razorpay env validation 500'd every route importing it locally despite PhonePe being the real provider (dev-placeholder env vars added). Verified end-to-end against the live database, including activating the two previously-`INVITED` seed tenants to get a genuine tenant session for the first time this project. See [[Features]], [[Database]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-26**: Monthly Food Planning & Voting (V1) — full replacement of the mock Week1-4 food-schedule model with a real, voted, backend-driven system, per an explicit user product spec. New Prisma models: `food_menu_items` (per-hostel, per-meal-type item library), `food_voting_periods` (one per hostel per month, DRAFT/OPEN/CLOSED), `food_votes` (single-choice per tenant per meal type, upsert-on-change), `food_schedules` (one mutable row per hostel per month — modeled as a **repeating weekly pattern**, not calendar-dated, see [[Decisions]] ADR-017), `food_schedule_meals` (28 cells: 7 days × 4 meal types). ~17 new backend routes under `app/api/food/*` (menu-items CRUD, voting-period lifecycle + live results, tenant voting, schedule generate/fetch/edit-cell/publish/history, tenant schedule + history — see [[APIs]]) plus a new daily cron `app/api/cron/food-carry-forward` (`0 4 * * *` in `vercel.json`, idempotent via `food_schedules`'s `@@unique([hostel_id, month])`). New `lib/services/food-schedule-generator.ts` (`assignWeekForMealType`) implements the generation algorithm: tally votes per meal type (ties broken by name; falls back to all active library items if a meal type has zero votes so no slot is ever empty), rank by vote count, compute per-item slot counts via largest-remainder proportional rounding, then deal items across the 7 weekdays one full pass at a time so repeats spread out rather than cluster. `app/api/food` was added to `scripts/architectural-invariants-check.ts`'s scanned roots (no optional `hostelId`, no first-hostel fallback).
  - **Owner frontend** (`features/owner-food/`): Library CRUD, a new `VotingPanel` (open/close voting, live per-meal-type tally), a new `WeeklyScheduleGrid` (one real week, tap-to-edit cells via `ScheduleMealPickerSheet`, Publish button on DRAFT / "Live — edits update immediately" note on PUBLISHED) and `MonthHistoryList` (past published months, lazy detail fetch) replace the deleted `useMealSchedule.ts`/`buildSchedule.ts`/`MonthlyMenuCard`/`WeekGroup`/`MealCard`/`MealActionSheet`/`MealPickerSheet`. `FoodPage.tsx`'s Monthly Schedule sub-tab now composes Library → Voting → Weekly grid → History; the sibling **Food Polls sub-tab is completely unchanged** (`CreatePollModal`/`PollResultsSheet`/`useFoodPolls`/`mockFoodPolls` untouched), per explicit user decision to keep both features rather than merge/replace — see [[Decisions]] ADR-029.
  - **Tenant frontend** (new): `features/tenant-session/useTenantSession.ts` (tenant-side analog of `useOwnerSession()`), `features/food/hooks/{useTenantFoodVoting,useTenantFoodSchedule}.ts`, `platforms/tenant/pages/TenantFoodPage.tsx` (published weekly menu + month picker, plus a single-choice-per-meal-type vote section) — replaces the `RouteScaffold` previously at `/tenant/food`. First real (non-scaffold) tenant-facing surface built for the Food tab.
  - Built and verified in 5 slices against the live Supabase-backed dev server via curl (menu items → voting → generate/review → publish/tenant-view/history → carry-forward cron), each slice checked before moving to the next. One slice's live verification was interrupted by a genuine, sustained Supabase infrastructure outage (not a code issue — confirmed via raw TCP reachability while Postgres-protocol queries timed out); resumed and completed once the database recovered.
  - **Known gaps, not fixed this pass**: the only seeded tenant (Arjun Mehta) remains `INVITED`, not `ACTIVE`, so a genuine tenant-session vote-cast and published-schedule view were never exercised with a live 200 response (tenant-route auth/shape was verified via curl, but not a real tenant login). The carry-forward cron's "clone previous month" branch was not exercised live (no prior published month existed yet to carry from) — only its idempotent no-op branch was verified; assessed low-risk since it reuses the exact `createMany` pattern already verified correct in the generate-schedule route.
  - See [[Database]], [[APIs]], [[Features]], [[Decisions]] ADR-029.

### Changed
- **2026-07-26**: Food voting granularity reversed from single-choice to **multi-select per meal type**, per explicit user direction shortly after the Monthly Food Planning & Voting feature above shipped — see [[Decisions]] ADR-029 (revision note). `food_votes`'s unique constraint widened to `(voting_period_id, tenant_id, meal_type, menu_item_id)` (`prisma db push`, dev DB); `POST /api/food/tenant/vote` changed from upsert-overwrite to toggle (add the vote if absent, remove if present); `useTenantFoodVoting`'s `myVoteBySlot`/`castVote` became `myVotesBySlot` (a `Set` per meal type) / `toggleVote`; `TenantFoodPage.tsx`'s per-item selector changed from a radio-style circle to a checkbox-style square. The vote-tally logic in `voting-periods/[id]/results` and `schedules/generate` needed no change — both already `groupBy` on `menu_item_id`, so the generation algorithm's balanced-schedule behavior is unaffected. Verified: a direct-DB script confirmed the new constraint allows one tenant to hold multiple `food_votes` rows within the same meal type (previously impossible), that re-voting the same item now correctly hits a duplicate-key rejection (the case the route's toggle logic branches on), and that the tally counts each item independently; `tsc --noEmit` clean on both the changed backend routes and the changed frontend hook/page. Not exercised: a real tenant HTTP session casting the vote (same pre-existing gap — no `ACTIVE` seeded tenant available).

- **2026-07-26**: Phase 1 continuation — Money, Tenant mutations, and core Settings on real data, closing out the "Owner Product" phase of the roadmap. Followed a full read-only readiness audit (published separately) whose single biggest finding was a second, fully real, API-wired owner app sitting completely unrouted in `apps/frontend/src/app/components/*` (reachable only from `app/App.tsx`, which nothing imports). Rather than rebuild these flows from scratch against mocks, salvaged the real logic/wiring pattern from that dead code into the live `/owner/*` tree:
  - **Money** (`features/owner-money/`): new `hooks/useRealMoney.ts` composes `portfolioService.getSummary()` (shared cache with the Home dashboard), a per-hostel `dashboardService.getCashflow()` fan-out for the 7-day forecast (never assumes "first hostel"), `useRealTenantList()` reused for overdue tenants, and `expenseService.getAll()` for the Expenses tab — `StatusBanner`, `CollectionRateCard`, `ActionQueueCard`, `CashflowForecastCard`, `CollectionsFilters`, `BusinessHealthStrip`, `InsightTilesGrid`, `MonthlyTrendCard`, `CategoryChipsRow`, `ExpenseBreakdownCard`, `TopVendorsCard` all converted from self-importing mocks to accepting real data via props. The mock's fabricated "Unusual spending" tile was replaced with the real backend's own anomaly-detection field (`category_breakdown[].anomaly`) rather than kept as a lie.
  - **Expenses CRUD**: `AddExpenseModal`/`useAddExpenseWizard` now submit to real `POST /expenses` (create) or `PUT /expenses/:id` (edit, via a new `editingId` mode also used by "Duplicate"); `ExpenseDetailModal`'s Mark Pending/Delete call real `expenseService.update`/`delete`; `ExportExpensesModal` streams a real file from `GET /expenses/export`.
  - **Quick Collect** (`features/owner-tenants/quick-collect/QuickCollectModal.tsx`): fully real — `GET /payments/quick-collect/search` (select), `GET /payments/settlement-preview` (preview, the real FIFO settlement engine — surfaces `payment_accepted:false`/`rejection_reason` when the backend's collection policy rejects the amount, verified live), `POST /auth/confirm-identity` + `POST /payments/record-offline` (password → confirm). Accepts either an already-loaded `initialTenant` (from Tenant Detail, `obligations` included, no re-fetch) or starts on the search step (from Money's tenant rows).
  - **Change Rent** (new `features/owner-tenants/actions/ChangeRentModal.tsx`): identity-confirmed (`CHANGE_RENT` purpose) then `POST /tenants/:id/change-rent`. Wired into `TenantActionsSheet`'s "Change Rent" row (previously a "Coming soon" toast) via a new `onChangeRent` prop, same branching pattern as the existing `collect` row.
  - **Move-out / Checkout** (new `features/owner-tenants/actions/MoveOutSheet.tsx`): drives the real multi-stage `moveOutService` state machine (`REQUESTED → inspect → SETTLEMENT_PENDING → settle → SETTLEMENT_APPROVED → vacate → PHYSICALLY_VACATED → complete → COMPLETED`) one stage at a time, reading current state via `GET /move-out/requests`. Wired into `TenantActionsSheet`'s "Check-out / Exit" row via a new `onCheckout` prop. Open disputes are flagged but not resolved here — a separate, rarer flow, explicitly deferred.
  - **Settings core**: `MoreBillingPage` now reads/writes real billing policy via `useHostelPolicy`/`useUpdateHostelPolicy` (`GET`/`PATCH /hostels/:id/preferences`, deep-merged server-side — verified live). Two new screens, `MoreProfilePage` (`GET`/`PATCH /owner/me/profile` — name/address editable, phone read-only since changing it requires a separate OTP-verification step the backend enforces) and `MoreHostelIdentityPage` (`PATCH /hostels/:id` + logo upload/remove), replace two "Coming soon" rows in `MoreSettingsPage`. **Deferred, not built this pass**: Tenant Defaults, Automation/WhatsApp linking, Notifications, Access/Docs, and the Agreement-template editor — these sections' real UI still exists only in the orphaned tree.
  - `useTenantDetail.ts` gained a `hostelId` field on `RealTenantDetail` (derived internally already, just not previously exposed) so downstream mutation modals don't need to re-derive it.
  - Verified against the live dev server with the real seeded test data: created/updated/exported/deleted a real expense end-to-end; confirmed `settlement-preview`'s real rejection path (`payment_accepted:false`) renders correctly; confirmed `change-rent`'s real "no active agreement" error and move-out's real "PAYMENT_PENDING reservation" business-rule error both surface through the UI's error banner correctly (via the existing invited-but-not-activated test tenant, which structurally couldn't complete a full successful payment/move-out — that last leg needs an activated tenant, not yet available in the seed data); confirmed the hostel-policy PATCH's deep-merge preserves untouched sibling fields; confirmed `npm run check:invariants` still passes.
  - **Orphaned-tree deletion deferred**: the plan called for deleting `app/components/*` after salvage, but the deferred Settings sections above still have real, unported reference UI sitting only in that tree — deleting it now would destroy that reference before it's used. Left in place pending either the Settings follow-up slice or an explicit decision to accept the loss.
  - See [[Frontend]], [[Decisions]].

### Fixed
- **2026-07-26**: `GET /api/tenants/:id/score` 500'd for any tenant without a `profile_id` yet — i.e. every freshly-invited, not-yet-activated tenant, a very common real-world state — because the route passed `null` straight into a Prisma `findUnique({ where: { profile_id } })`, which Prisma rejects. Found via real end-to-end testing while wiring the Invite flow (see below). Fixed in `app/api/tenants/[id]/score/route.ts`: short-circuits with a neutral default score (`100`/`EXCELLENT`/`STABLE`, matching the schema's own default) instead of querying, since a tenant with no profile genuinely has no behavior history to score yet.

### Changed
- **2026-07-26**: Phase 3, slice 2 (Tenants: Invite wizard → real). The Invite Tenant wizard now submits to the real `POST /owners/invitations` (`tenantService.invite`, already existed as a wrapper). The room picker (`StayStep`) was hardcoded fake room strings — now a real dropdown of vacant rooms via `GET /api/rooms?grouped=true` (reusing Phase 2's room service), required since the backend's `InvitationSchema` requires a real `room_id` UUID. Hostel picker now uses `useOwnerSession().hostels` instead of mock properties. On success, invalidates the Tenants list query so the new tenant shows up immediately. Verified fully end-to-end against the live database: sent a real invite, confirmed the tenant appears in the real list (status `INVITED`, real `SECURITY_DEPOSIT`+`RENT` obligations auto-generated), and confirmed the Detail page's 4 composed endpoints all return sensible real data for it — this is also what surfaced the `/score` bug above. Quick Collect, Change Rent, and the rest of the Actions sheet remain mock/toast — still a separate follow-up slice. See [[Frontend]], [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-26**: Phase 3, slice 1 (Tenants: List + Detail → real data). New `features/owner-tenants/hooks/useRealTenantList.ts` replaces `useTenantFilters` — `GET /api/tenants` is hostel-scoped with no "all hostels" option, so the "All Hostels" filter fans out in parallel across every real hostel (`useOwnerSession().hostels`) and merges client-side, same discipline as Phases 1-2. New `features/owner-tenants/hooks/useTenantDetail.ts` composes 4 real endpoints for Tenant Detail: `owner/tenants/:id/overview` (header/guardian/stay/activity — real `guardian_name/phone/relation` fields exist, previously assumed mock-only), `:id/score` (a real tenant behavior score I hadn't known existed — `{score, grade, trend, insights}`, backed by `tenant_behavior_scores` — replaces the mock's fabricated `riskScore`/`riskLabel`; the mock's "Payment Rate %" tile had no real per-tenant equivalent anywhere in the backend, so it's swapped for the real `trend` field instead of faking a number), `:id/documents` (real `PENDING|APPROVED|REJECTED` status — "missing" is computed by diffing against `required_documents`, since an absent required doc has no row at all), and `:id/full` for `rent_obligations` (the only endpoint that has them, capped at 5 — Charges tab now says so honestly rather than implying completeness). `TenantRow`/`TenantList` needed zero changes — real data is mapped into the exact shape they already render, same trick used for Rooms in Phase 2. Deliberately **not** wired this slice: Invite Tenant wizard, Quick Collect, Change Rent, and the Actions sheet — each is its own multi-step, identity-token-gated mutation flow, scoped as a separate follow-up rather than bundled in. Verified against the live database (real empty-hostel tenant list, real 404 handling for a nonexistent tenant). See [[Frontend]].
- **2026-07-26**: Phase 2 (Hostel → real data) — Overview and Rooms sub-tabs of the Hostel Drill-down now read real data; `shared/mocks/rooms.ts` is no longer used for values (its `Floor`/`Room`/`RoomBed` type shapes are still reused for typing, since `RoomRow`/`FloorGroup`/`RoomLayoutCard`/`BedStatusDots` needed zero changes — the real data is mapped into the exact same shape). New `features/hostel-drilldown/hooks/useHostelRooms.ts` wraps the already-real, already-ready `GET /api/rooms?hostelId=&grouped=true` (per-floor rooms with real `occupied`/`reserved`/`capacity` counts and real occupant names/rent/dues — no separate per-bed lookup needed) plus `POST /api/floors`/`POST /api/rooms`/`PATCH /api/rooms/:id` for Add Floor, Add Room, and layout-mode drag-to-move-floor (previously local-state-only, now actually persists). Per-bed identity remains a permanent, documented limitation — no such entity exists in the schema — so `beds[]` is synthesized client-side from the real counts, same approach the design's own bed-dot UI already assumed. `HostelOverviewPage` reuses the same `portfolio/summary` query the Home dashboard (Phase 1) already fetches — free if the owner came from Home, one extra call if they linked in directly. Deliberately **not** wired this phase: `HostelTenantsPage`'s tenant list — it reuses the main Tenants tab's `TenantList` component, whose data shape is Phase 3's responsibility to define; wiring it here would mean redoing it in Phase 3, so it stays mock until then. Verified against the live database (real floor/room create, real floor-move PATCH) with the test hostel from Phase 0. See [[Frontend]].
- **2026-07-26**: Phase 1 (Dashboard → real data) — `/owner/home` now reads real data instead of `shared/mocks/dashboard.ts`. New `features/owner-dashboard/hooks/useOwnerDashboard.ts` composes `GET /api/owner/portfolio/summary` (per-hostel cards + owner-wide aggregate, computed server-side across all hostels — no client-side per-hostel fan-out needed here), `GET /api/tenants/pending-documents` and `GET /api/agreements/renewals` (both owner-wide, no hostelId required), and one real parallel per-hostel fan-out (`tenantService.getAll(hostelId, {status:'INVITED'})` per hostel, summed) for the one Action Center figure that has no owner-wide endpoint — matching the CLAUDE.md invariant against ever picking a single "first hostel." Two fields the backend genuinely doesn't track (daily revenue, reminder-sent status) are honestly relabeled (e.g. "Collected this month" instead of a fake "today's revenue") rather than faked. `OwnerHomeDashboard`/`AllActionsSheet` now take `actionCenter`/`snapshot`/`collection` as props (still defaulting to the mocks for standalone use) instead of importing them directly — completes the "pure presentational, real hook swap-in" shape both components' own doc comments already anticipated. Verified against the live database with the real test account from Phase 0. See [[Frontend]].
- **2026-07-26**: A full architecture audit (database/API/frontend, read-only, no code changed — see the report) surfaced the single biggest structural problem in the repo: two disjoint owner frontends existed. The real, `ProtectedRoute`-gated `/owner/*` tree was 100% blank `RouteScaffold` placeholders; all the actual built owner-app UI lived at `/get-started/*` behind a separate mock gate a real session never reached. Per explicit product direction to converge before adding any more features, migrated the tab pages, Tenant Detail, and Hostel Drill-down to render under `/owner/*` (same components, no duplication) behind the real auth stack; `/get-started/*` now only hosts the still-mock lead-capture funnel. Deleted the now-redundant `RequireOwnerSession` guard (the real `ProtectedRoute` already covers this, and more correctly — no hostel-count requirement, so a freshly-signed-up owner with zero hostels isn't bounced out). Fixed ~15 hardcoded `/get-started/...` navigation targets across the moved components. Pure routing consolidation — no data-wiring changed, all owner-app screens are still mock data pending the next phase. See [[Frontend]] for detail.

### Added
- **2026-07-25**: Real owner signup → onboarding → dashboard journey wired end-to-end against the live backend (Supabase-hosted Postgres, provisioned this session — see [[Backend]] for the `next.config.mjs` env-path bug found and fixed while setting it up). New public `POST /api/auth/owner-signup` (`apps/backend/app/api/auth/owner-signup/route.ts` + `authService.selfSignUpOwner()`) closes the gap where `/api/auth/register` was unreachable by any real code path — see [[APIs]] and ADR-028 in [[Decisions]] for why this is a separate, ungated method rather than a reuse of `registerOwner`'s bootstrap flag. Frontend: `features/owner-onboarding/api/onboardingApi.ts` (new) wraps OTP send/verify, signup, and hostel/floor/room creation; `AccountStep` gained a real password field and a real 6-digit OTP input (was decorative dots before); `useOnboardingSubmission.ts` (new hook) orchestrates OTP verify → signup → `AuthContext.login()` at the account step, then real `createHostel` → `createFloor`×N → `createRoom`×N on Publish. Route guards: `/onboarding` is no longer gated (real auth happens mid-wizard now, not before entry); the post-onboarding `/get-started/*` tree now uses a new `RequireOwnerSession` guard (real session + at least one real hostel, derived from the DB) instead of `RequireMockOwnerJourney`'s sessionStorage flags. `LandingPage.tsx`'s Google-mock step now leads straight into the real wizard instead of the dead-end `HostelLeadModal`. `MockOwnerJourneyContext`/`RequireMockOwnerJourney` are kept only for the two already-orphaned lead-capture pages (`/get-started/submitted`, `/get-started/activate/:token`), unchanged. Verified end-to-end at the API level (OTP send/verify, signup, login, hostel/floor/room create) against the live database with a fresh test account; full browser click-through not independently driven this session (no browser-automation tool available) — see [[Features]] for the file list and manual verification steps to run.


- **2026-07-25**: StayO owner app Frontend Completion Pass — declares the owner-app preview **feature-complete and frozen** ahead of backend integration. Built `/get-started/alerts` (Leads/Admin/Renewals/Requests, plus an added read/unread state not in the design) and `/get-started/more` + `/more/{settings,billing,help,about}` (fixing a real 404 — the bottom nav already linked to `/get-started/more` with no route behind it). Wired the Home FAB (Quick Actions: Collect Payment/Add Tenant/Add Expense/Create Food Poll — reusing `QuickCollectModal`/`InviteTenantWizard` directly and routing to Money/Food with router state for the other two, no duplicated flows) and "View all" Action Center sheet, plus fixed several previously-dead buttons (bell icon, property-card kebab menu, "+ Add hostel"). Fixed a returning-owner bug: `LandingPage.tsx` claimed "Go to My Hostel" for any authenticated mock session but clicking it re-opened lead capture, since `journey.activate()` was never called — now calls it and navigates straight to the dashboard. Added desktop centering (bordered `max-w-[480px]` frame on `sm:`+) to `OwnerAppShell`/`TenantDetailPage`/`HostelDrilldownLayout` after discovering the earlier tracker doc's claim of "85/22 unextracted desktop `@media` rules" was inaccurate — `Stayo App.dc.html` has zero `@media` rules, a fixed mobile-frame mockup end to end. Help & Support and About StayO are new additions not present in the design source, built with the identical `ListRow` pattern and flagged as such rather than presented as ported. See [[Features]] for the full file list.
- **2026-07-25**: StayO owner app Food tab shipped (Monthly Schedule meal planner + Food Polls) at `/get-started/food` — the last of the three tabs in the agreed build order (Tenants → Money → Food; `Stayo App.dc.html` lines 1087–1467). Food Library is real add/edit/delete/drag-reorder chip CRUD; Generate Menu builds a 4-week schedule from the library (1.5s fake delay, matching the design's own `setTimeout`), with per-meal replace/delete via an action sheet and drag-to-swap between same-slot cells. Food Polls: create (single/multi/rating/yes-no, with a live options editor for single/multi), close, and view results (stats + winner + vote-breakdown bars) against 4 seeded polls. New `shared/mocks/food.ts` with `getPollWinner()` deriving the leading option from a poll's own `options` array rather than hardcoding it. Confirmed `saveDraft`/`publishMenu`/poll `Edit`/`useWinner` are toast-only in the design source itself, not a scope cut on this pass. See [[Features]] for the full file list.
- **2026-07-24**: StayO owner app Money tab shipped (Overview/Collections/Expenses, plus Add Expense 3-step wizard, Filters, Export, and Expense Detail modals) at `/get-started/money` — the largest single tab so far (`Stayo App.dc.html` lines 267–866). New `shared/mocks/expenses.ts` with `getExpenseInsights()` deriving category breakdown/top vendor/largest expense/net profit from the mock records rather than hardcoding them. Every "Collect" button reuses the Tenants tab's `QuickCollectModal` rather than a new payment flow; Collections/Action Queue read `shared/mocks/tenants.ts` directly. See [[Features]] for the full file list.
- **2026-07-24**: Fixed the owner app's product-theme screens (`OwnerAppShell`, `TenantDetailPage`, `HostelDrilldownLayout`) missing the graph-paper background grid the design sets on its root (`Stayo App.dc.html` line 31) — the marketing-theme `LandingPage.tsx` had its equivalent already, the product-theme surfaces never did. This was reported as "the grid thing" and initially misread as a request for the Hostel Rooms layout-grid feature (built anyway, and still useful) before the actual background-pattern gap was clarified with a reference screenshot.
- **2026-07-24**: StayO owner app Hostel Drill-down shipped (Overview + Rooms grid/layout view, specifically requested, + hostel-scoped Tenants), reached by tapping a property card on Home. Rooms sub-tab has browse mode (floor-grouped rows, bed-status dots) and layout/reorder mode (native HTML5 drag-and-drop to move rooms between floors — same pattern already used for the Home tab's draggable property cards). Room Sheet, Add Room, and Add Floor modals included. New `shared/mocks/rooms.ts`, new `features/hostel-drilldown/` module, routes `/get-started/hostels/:hostelId/{overview,rooms,tenants}` mirroring the real still-scaffolded `/owner/hostels/:hostelId/*` paths exactly. `OwnerHomeDashboard`'s property cards had no click handler before this — added one. See [[Features]] for the full file list and design-source line references.
- **2026-07-24**: StayO owner app Tenants tab shipped as a full experience, per explicit direction to build production-quality frontend architecture rather than just screens: list (`/get-started/tenants`, hostel filter/search/status chips), 4-step Invite Tenant wizard, Tenant Detail as a **real route** (`/get-started/tenants/:tenantId`, not a modal — deliberately, so it becomes `/owner/tenants/:tenantId` later with minimal changes), Tenant Actions sheet, 5-step Quick Collect payment flow. New centralized `shared/mocks/{dashboard,tenants,payments}.ts` (migrated off the old per-feature `features/owner-dashboard/mockDashboardData.ts`, now deleted) so Money/Food/Reports can reuse the same tenant/property data later instead of duplicating it. Fixed a real bug found while building this: `OwnerAppShell`'s bottom nav was hardcoded to `/owner/*`, so the mock dashboard preview's own nav tabs bounced to `/` — added a `basePath` prop (default `/owner` unaffected; mock preview passes `/get-started`). `/get-started/dashboard` is now a redirect to the renamed `/get-started/home`. See [[Features]] for the full file list and design-source line references.
- **2026-07-24**: StayO owner acquisition journey shipped end-to-end (Landing → Get Started → mock Login → Lead Submitted → Activation Link → 12-step animated Onboarding → Dashboard preview), mock data/local state only, no backend wiring yet. `/` now serves the new `LandingPage.tsx` (legacy `HomePage.tsx` deprecated, not deleted). See [[Features]] for the full write-up and file list. Deliberately does not use the real `AuthContext`/`ProtectedRoute` — introduced `MockOwnerJourneyContext` instead; see `docs/migration/frontend-foundation-tracker.md` for why and what changes when backend integration happens. Also fixed two carried-over issues from the Phase-1 frontend foundation while touching adjacent files: `OwnerAppShell`'s bottom nav had a 6th "Alerts" tab not present in the design (`BottomNav.dc.html` has 5), and `tokens/fonts.css`'s Google Fonts `@import url(...)` was silently dropped by the browser because it wasn't the first statement in the merged stylesheet — moved to a `<link>` in `index.html`, matching the pattern the legacy fonts already used. Also fixed `vite.config.ts` reading `process.env.HMS_API_PROXY_TARGET` directly, which never picks up a real `.env` file's value inside the config function (only `loadEnv()`'s return value does) — switched to `loadEnv()`, otherwise a correctly-filled `.env` still failed with "not set".

### Changed
- **2026-07-24**: `LandingPage.tsx`'s owner entry point ("Get Started"/"Manage My Hostel"/"Book a demo" — now one path) swapped from the email/password `LoginModal` to a new mock-only `shared/ui-patterns/GoogleSignInModal.tsx` ("Continue with Google", no real OAuth). Superseded same day (see next entry).
- **2026-07-24**: Superseding the entry above, per further direction: the post-Google-sign-in step moved from a separate page (`LeadSubmittedPage`) to a single in-page popup, `features/owner-onboarding/components/HostelLeadModal.tsx` — Hostel Owner Name/Hostel Name/Phone Number, then a 6-digit phone OTP (mock-verified), then a "Thanks — we've got your details, the Stayo team will reach out" confirmation. No email/activation-link mention at this step, and this flow deliberately no longer auto-continues into Activation/Onboarding (`journey.activate()` is not called here) — it ends by closing back to the landing page, matching the real intended process of a human following up before any self-serve activation. `LeadSubmittedPage`/`ActivationLinkPage`/`OwnerOnboardingWizard`/`OwnerDashboardPreviewPage` are unchanged and still fully working, just no longer reachable from this landing-page click path. See [[Features]] for the full note.
- **2026-07-24**: Fixed `LandingPage.tsx` rendering with the legacy Shri Adithya color palette instead of StayO's — it was never wrapped in `<ThemeProvider theme="marketing">`, so its Tailwind color utilities resolved against the unscoped legacy `:root` tokens in `theme.css` rather than the StayO tokens scoped under `[data-app-theme="marketing"]`.

### Changed
- **2026-07-22**: Fixed repeated owner-direct frequency switching crashing with a unique-constraint error, and (once that was fixed) potentially leaving a live schedule mixing two cadences' amounts — see ADR-027 and [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-22**: Owner-direct frequency changes now search forward for the first genuinely clean billing period instead of failing outright when the immediate next one collides — fixes a real report of Quarterly→Monthly failing with `UNCLEAN_BILLING_PERIOD` when a later month would have worked fine. See ADR-026 and [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-22**: Disabled the 90-day frequency-change cooldown and minimum-commitment checks on the owner-direct path (`ownerInitiateChange`/`ownerSetCustomSchedule`) — explicitly temporary, per request while testing repeatedly on the same tenant. See ADR-025 and [[Bugs]] for how to re-enable.
- **2026-07-22**: Owner-direct frequency changes now actually regroup an agreement-based tenant's future rent into the new cadence (e.g. Monthly → Quarterly), instead of just updating a setting the already-pre-generated monthly obligations ignored — see ADR-024 and [[Bugs]]. Removed `ChangeFrequencyModal.tsx`'s agreement-tenant warning banner and the `hasActiveAgreement` prop since the limitation it described is resolved for this path.
- **2026-07-22**: Owner-direct frequency changes (`ownerInitiateChange`) no longer block just because the tenant has ordinary older overdue/partial rent — only a genuine period overlap with the new schedule's window blocks now, per direct report of a real `UNCLEAN_BILLING_PERIOD` hit on a tenant simply behind on rent. See ADR-023 and [[Bugs]]. Also fixed a same-day regression: the Waive button was disappearing (and Cancel wrongly appearing) for partially-paid obligations on the owner Tenant Profile's Charges tab, because that tab's obligation objects never carried the `payments[]` array the Cancel/Waive visibility logic relied on — see [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-22**: Owner can now define a Custom Dates installment schedule for a tenant — an explicit list of due-date + amount charges, not a recurring cadence (`BillingTransitionService.ownerSetCustomSchedule`, `POST /api/tenants/:id/change-frequency/custom`) — see ADR-022. Unlike the recurring Monthly/Quarterly/Half-Yearly path below, this works correctly today for agreement-based tenants too, since it creates/supersedes `rent_obligations` rows directly rather than depending on `agreement-rent-schedule-service.ts`. `ChangeFrequencyModal.tsx` gained a Recurring/Custom Dates toggle with a dynamic add/remove installment-row editor. Also fixed a transaction-nesting bug caught while building this: the two owner-direct frequency-change routes were each wrapping the service call in their own outer transaction for identity-token consumption, while the service methods *also* opened their own separate transaction internally — the token could be marked consumed even if the outer transaction later failed independently. Both service methods now take the identity JTI directly and consume it inside their own single transaction.
- **2026-07-22**: Owner can now change a tenant's billing frequency (Monthly/Quarterly/Half-Yearly) directly, without waiting for a tenant-submitted request — see ADR-021. Same safety guards as the existing tenant-request flow (90-day cooldown, "clean billing period" check, minimum commitment months), identity-confirmed like Change Rent. New `POST /api/tenants/:id/change-frequency`, `ChangeFrequencyModal.tsx` in the tenant profile's Agreement actions. Known limitation surfaced honestly in the modal: for tenants with a signed agreement, the change is recorded but doesn't yet alter actual obligation generation (see [[Bugs]]) — `agreement-rent-schedule-service.ts` always generates monthly today.
- **2026-07-22**: Owner can now cancel an `UPCOMING` charge (`ObligationCard.tsx` — `canCancel` only checked the frontend's own `ACTIONABLE_STATUSES` list, which excludes `UPCOMING`, even though the backend guard (`cancelObligationInTx`'s `ACTIONABLE_STATUSES`, aliased to `settlement-planner.ts::PAYABLE_STATUSES`) already permits it — an `UPCOMING` charge can never have payments against it, so allowing Cancel there is unconditionally safe). Also redesigned `SettlementPreview.tsx` (the "Collect Now" allocation modal) per direct feedback that the Received/Allocated/Future Credit/Remaining Due 4-box flow diagram wasn't useful and didn't fit mobile screens — removed the diagram and the "click to expand" gate on the breakdown (it's the useful part, now always visible), replaced with a lead-in line plus a compact 2-box Future Credit / Remaining Due summary shown only when non-zero. Also added `frontend-v2/src/lib/share.ts` (`copyToClipboard` with an `execCommand` fallback for browsers/WebViews without the async Clipboard API, `openWhatsAppShare`, `sharePaymentLink`) and switched all three existing "Share Payment Link" call sites (`ObligationCard.tsx`, `PrimaryActionsBar.tsx`, `useTenantActions.ts`) to copy the link to the clipboard *and* open WhatsApp, instead of only opening WhatsApp — works identically on Android and iOS since `wa.me` deep-links are platform-agnostic.
- **2026-07-22**: The public payment-link page (`GET /api/payments/pay/[token]`, `app/api/payments/pay/[token]/route.ts`) pre-filled "Amount to Pay" with `settlement-planner`'s `total_outstanding` whenever the link wasn't tied to one specific obligation (e.g. a generic tenant-scoped share link) — that figure sums **every** `PAYABLE_STATUSES` obligation including `UPCOMING`, i.e. the entire remaining lease, not what's actually owed now (reported live: a tenant with 11 months left on their lease saw a pre-filled ₹93,500 — 11 × ₹8,500 — instead of the ₹8,500 actually due). Replaced with a due-date-aware sum via `financialService.getTenantDues()`, mirroring the same "due today or overdue, excluding early-activated future obligations" filter added to the tenant portal fixes below. If nothing is due yet, the field now defaults to 0 instead of falling back to monthly rent, so the payer can freely enter whatever they want to pay ahead. `tests/payment-link-flow.test.ts` updated (mocked `rent_obligations.findMany` instead of the no-longer-called `previewSettlement` for this path) plus a new regression test asserting a mixed overdue+future-activated obligation set pre-fills only the overdue amount.
- **2026-07-22**: Tenant portal Home page (`TenantPriorityStrip.tsx`) rent-status card redesigned to stop showing the same figure twice under different labels ("Rent due" and "Total due" were often identical numbers, with a separately-listed "Overdue" that looked like it should subtract from one of them but didn't). Now leads with one bold "Total to pay" figure, followed by a plain-language sentence when part of it is overdue, with the rent/deposit/late-fee/maintenance category breakdown demoted to a secondary detail section below. **Superseded same day, see the "current_payable_amount bundles next cycle's rent" bug below** — the figure this card headlines was itself corrected after this first pass shipped. Also fixed the Home page's green "Bed Reservation: Move-in Ready! ... all onboarding financials are fully cleared" card reading as contradicting a concurrent red "Overdue" card directly above it — the claim was only ever about the one-time move-in deposit, not ongoing rent. Copy narrowed to say "your move-in deposit is fully cleared" and, per `[[Business-Rules]]`, the card now collapses to a one-line badge instead of a full reassurance card whenever the tenant has an active PENDING/PARTIAL/OVERDUE rent balance. See [[Bugs]] for both the reversal-labeling fix and the payable-amount fix.
- **2026-07-22**: Owner Tenant Profile's "Obligations" tab renamed to "Charges" throughout (`TenantProfilePage.tsx`, `CreateObligationModal.tsx`, `CancelObligationModal.tsx`, `WaiveObligationModal.tsx`, `ObligationHistorySheet.tsx`, `FinancialActivityCard.tsx`, `SettlementPreview.tsx`, `UnifiedActivityTimeline.tsx`) — per direct owner feedback that "obligation" isn't a term owners use or recognize. Internal identifiers (`obligation_id`, `ObligationCard`, API routes, etc.) are unchanged; only owner-visible copy (tab labels, modal titles, toasts, buttons) was reworded. Also fixed `RentObligationList.tsx`'s month grouping, which sorted furthest-future month first (e.g. May 2027 above Dec 2026) — the same descending-by-date sort made sense for a reverse-chronological activity feed but not for a tenant's charge list, where the owner needs what's overdue or due soon at the top. Now sorts by urgency first (overdue → due-now → upcoming → paid/waived/cancelled), then by date ascending within the actionable buckets and descending within resolved history.
- **2026-07-22**: Tenant Profile's "Actions" sheet (`PrimaryActionsBar.tsx`) had two entries — "Create Charge" and "Create Rent" — that opened the exact same `CreateObligationModal` with the exact same effective result: the modal's obligation-type selector already defaults to `RENT`, so "Create Rent" was passing `initialValues={{obligationType:'RENT'}}` to override a default that was already `'RENT'`. Removed "Create Rent" entirely (owners can still pick Rent, or any of the 10 other obligation types, from "Create Charge"'s own type dropdown) and grouped the remaining 7 actions into four labeled sections on the mobile sheet (Collect / Charges & Receipts / Agreement / Tenancy) instead of one flat list, with a matching separator added between agreement actions and Check-out/Exit in the desktop "More" dropdown. `onCreateRent` prop removed from `PrimaryActionsBarProps`.
- **2026-07-22**: Tenant Profile's obligation actions (`ObligationCard.tsx`) simplified after feedback that Cancel/Waive/"Delete" were hard to tell apart. There is no Delete action in the code — the perceived third option was almost certainly **Edit**, whose own copy admits it "creates a corrected replacement and cancels the original" (see `Business-Rules.md` — Obligation Lifecycle). The real, non-mergeable confusion was that **Cancel** and **Waive** could render simultaneously as two identical-looking red buttons on a `PENDING` obligation. Fixed by deriving `hasPayments` from `obligation.payments[]` and showing exactly one: Cancel Charge (zero payments) or Waive Balance (any payment made) — same precondition the backend already enforces, just surfaced once instead of as a guess. Also renamed the Waive modal's dismiss button from "Cancel" to "Back" to stop reusing "Cancel" for two different meanings. See [[Business-Rules]].
- **2026-07-22**: Owner Home (`PortfolioView.tsx`) decluttered and given a universal search bar, per direct feedback referencing an alternate design. Removed three sections found redundant once the single-state Priority Card already surfaces the top-priority action: "Today's actions" (a bulleted list of the same overdue/move-out/activation/vacancy signals already in the KPI grid and Priority Card), "Needs attention" (a per-tenant overdue detail list duplicating what `/alerts` already owns), and "Business Health Trend" (the `PortfolioRevenueChart` component, a 6-month revenue/expense/profit chart with Top Profit/Low Occupancy/Top Dues chips) — the last of these had no other consumer, so `PortfolioRevenueChart.tsx` (339 lines) was deleted outright rather than left as dead code. Added a small "Snapshot" eyebrow label above the Beds/Outstanding/Revenue tiles for scannability. The page's own hostel-name/city search box (for filtering the Properties list further down) is unchanged and unrelated to the new universal bar below.
- **2026-07-22**: The new universal search bar was rebuilt after first-pass feedback that it redirected to the Tenants page instead of actually searching — new `UniversalSearchBar.tsx` (`app/components/portfolio/`) reuses the existing owner-scoped, all-hostels `paymentService.quickCollectSearch()` (already used by `RecordPaymentModal.tsx` for the same name/phone/room_no matching, no new backend endpoint) and renders matches as an inline dropdown under the input — debounced 250ms, click-outside/Escape to close, no navigation until a specific result is clicked (→ `/hostels/:hostel_id/tenants/:id`). Known scope limit carried over from the reused endpoint: it only matches `status: 'ACTIVE'` tenants, so invited/inactive tenants won't surface here. See [[Frontend]].
- **2026-07-21**: `RenewalPipelineTracker.tsx` — replaced the horizontally-scrolling pipeline funnel (edge-peek + fade gradient) with an always-visible compact `grid-cols-4` on mobile, per direct user feedback that the scroll-to-see-all-4-stages pattern wasn't working. Mobile cards drop the icon/description and shrink to just the number + a short label (e.g. "Expiring" instead of "1. Expiring (Needs Offer)"); desktop (`sm:`) keeps the original fuller card layout unchanged.

### Fixed
- **2026-07-22**: Owner Tenant Profile's "has agreement" checks were all reading a field that never existed. Following up the same-day contradiction fix below: a live report on a real tenant (a verified "Hostel Residency Agreement" document, yet the profile still said "Missing"/"No Active Contract") revealed `getOwnerTenantOverview` never queried the `agreement` table at all — every tenant, not just edge cases, was affected. Fixed properly this time: the overview now queries the tenant's real current agreement (`status` in the same `SIGNED`/`EXPIRING_SOON`/`AGREEMENT_EXPIRED` set the renewal system uses) and returns `has_active_agreement` plus a real `current_agreement` object (status/dates/contract terms/pdf_url); the frontend's three "has agreement" spots read `has_active_agreement` directly instead of inferring from a duration field that was never populated. Confirmed tenant onboarding itself creates and signs agreements correctly (`activation-workflow-service.ts`, `tests/activation-workflow.test.ts` 6/6) — this was a read-side gap only. New `tests/tenant-overview-agreement.test.ts` (3/3). See [[Bugs]] (Owner Tenant Profile contradicted itself...), [[Backend]].
- **2026-07-22**: Owner Tenant Profile self-contradicted on agreement status (first pass — see the follow-up entry above for the real fix) — Risk & Compliance said "Agreement: Signed", the "Rent Agreement" summary chip said "No Active Contract", and the Financial Strip said "No active agreement", all on the same page for the same tenant. The first two derived `hasAgreement` from `Boolean(allocations?.length > 0)` (room-allocation history, a different fact from having a current signed agreement). Also fixed: "Hostel Location" was a hardcoded literal `"Hostel 2"` for every tenant — now resolves the real hostel name via the owner's hostels list, matched by the route's `hostelId`. See [[Bugs]] (Owner Tenant Profile contradicted itself...).

### Added
- **2026-07-22**: New `formatIST()` helper (`backend-next/lib/timezone.ts`) — a real bug fix, not a style tweak. Every backend "en-IN" date/time format call (`expense-export-service.ts`'s "Generated at"/footer date in CSV, XLSX, and PDF exports) was calling `.toLocaleString("en-IN")`/`.toLocaleDateString("en-IN")` with no explicit `timeZone`, which resolves to the *server process's own* timezone in Node (commonly UTC on cloud hosts) — silently mislabeling a UTC time with Indian formatting instead of actually converting it, so an export generated at, say, 1:00 AM IST could show "Generated at: 7:30 PM" the previous day. Also fixed a same-class bug in `reportDateLabel()`'s month-range check, which used server-local `.getFullYear()/.getMonth()` on a `@db.Date` (UTC-midnight-encoded) value — switched to UTC getters. Frontend: `ActivityLogsView.tsx`'s three date/time formatters (day grouping, per-item time, detail panel) gained explicit `timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata'` for the same reason (a non-IST browser was rendering Indian-locale-*formatted* but not actually IST-*converted* times); "Today"/"Yesterday" grouping now compares IST calendar-day keys instead of the browser's local `toDateString()`. Expenses: `ExpenseDetailsModal.tsx` gained a new "Added on" row showing `created_at` (when the expense was entered) alongside the existing "Date" row (the expense's own transaction date) — previously `created_at` wasn't surfaced anywhere in the expense UI. Both `ExpenseCard.tsx` and `ExpenseDetailsModal.tsx`'s existing date formatting also gained explicit `timeZone`. See [[Bugs]] (Expense/activity-log timestamps could silently show the wrong time on a non-IST server or browser).
- **2026-07-22**: Activity Logs — expense update/delete events were being silently dropped. `app/api/owner/activity-logs/route.ts`'s `activity_logs` query filtered `entity_type: { in: ['HOSTEL_POLICY', 'RENT'] }`, which excluded `EXPENSE` rows entirely (written correctly by `activityService.log()` on every expense update/delete, per `lib/events/index.ts`) and also excluded `AGREEMENT_TEMPLATE` rows despite the mapper below already having a branch to handle them — the same "written but never read" bug for two different entity types. Fixed by broadening the query to an `OR` covering both, scoping `EXPENSE` to `action_type: {in: ['UPDATE','DELETE']}` only (CREATE is already reconstructed from the live `expenses` table with cash-position enrichment the log's metadata doesn't have — duplicating it would double-count). Now editing or deleting an expense shows up in the Activity Log; previously an update silently changed the displayed entry in place with no edit trail, and a delete made the entire event vanish with zero record. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-22**: Hostel lifecycle confirmation modals now show the real blast radius before the owner confirms. New shared `HostelImpactSummary.tsx`, rendered in both `PauseHostelModal.tsx` and `CloseHostelModal.tsx`, sourced from stats already on the hostel's own portfolio card (`active_tenants`/`occupied_beds`/`pending_dues` — no new fetch). `CloseHostelModal` additionally disables its submit button and swaps in a "Move tenants out first" state whenever the hostel has active tenants, instead of letting the owner submit and only then hit the backend's `prevent_archive_with_active_allocations` trigger error — it now also points them at "Temporarily Close" as the non-blocking alternative. Wired from both card surfaces that render `HostelActionMenu` (`PortfolioView.tsx`, `HostelsView.tsx`). See [[Bugs]] (Pausing a hostel gave no warning it stops rent generation for active tenants).
- **2026-07-22**: Tenant-side renewal signing — the closing piece of the renewal lifecycle. New `POST /api/tenants/me/renewal-signature` (session-authenticated ImageKit signature upload, mirroring the activation-token-based one) and a new dedicated page `src/platforms/tenant/pages/TenantRenewalPage.tsx` (route `/tenant/renewal`) covering the full lifecycle: awaiting-offer → offer review (accept/decline/discuss, restyled with brand tokens) → **sign your renewed agreement** (new — `SignaturePad` + optional guardian signature, calling `agreementService.signRenewalAgreement()` → `POST /api/agreements/[id]/sign-renewal`, which already accepted a tenant session but had no frontend consumer) → **signed confirmation** (new — shows final terms + a link to the signed PDF). New lean `useTenantRenewal()` hook (2 queries) keeps the page from depending on the full 10-query dashboard bundle. `TenantDashboardPage.tsx`'s two ~265-line inline renewal cards were replaced with one ~55-line `TenantRenewalBanner` linking to the new page — critically, the banner now surfaces the "awaiting signature" and "signed" states that were previously completely invisible (both old cards `return null` once a successor agreement exists). See [[Bugs]] (Tenant had no way to actually finalize an accepted renewal), [[Decisions]] ADR-019, [[APIs]], [[Frontend]], [[Backend]].
- **2026-07-21**: Room/Floor filtering + explicit tenant selection for bulk renewal offers. `RenewalQueueList` gained two client-side filter selects (Room Type, Floor — derived from the queue rows already fetched, no new endpoint) and a checkbox per row (plus a per-group "select all" checkbox for Needs Attention / All Other Expiring Stays), with a sticky bulk-action bar ("N selected" + Generate Offers + Clear) that appears once anything is checked. A third filter — Room Number — was added right after, per direct feedback that "room" in the original ask meant filtering down to one specific room, not the room-type/category filter. Selecting rows and tapping "Generate Offers" opens the same `BulkCampaignSheet` used by the header's "+ Campaign" button, but scoped: its live preview only covers the selected tenants, and its submit payload now includes `filterCriteria.agreementIds`. Backend: `generateBulkOffers` ANDs an optional `filterCriteria.agreementIds` onto its existing hostel/status scoping — a bogus or foreign id just yields fewer matched rows (never a cross-tenant leak) since it's additive to the existing `hostel_id` scoping, not a replacement for it. New shared frontend helper `deriveRoomGroupings()` (`renewal/utils.ts`) replaces the category/floor derivation that used to be duplicated only inside the wizard. New backend test asserting the `id: {in: [...]}` where-clause shape (20/20 in `renewal-offer-service.test.ts`). See [[APIs]], [[Backend]].
- **2026-07-21**: Two new bulk renewal campaign strategies — `FLOOR_WISE` (different rent per floor, keyed by `floors.name` or a `"Floor <n>"` fallback for rooms with only the legacy `floor` integer) and `ROOM_WISE` (a bespoke rent per exact room number, for owners who want per-room bulk pricing in one pass instead of opening the single-offer flow per tenant). `renewal-offer-service.ts::generateBulkOffers` gained `floor_rents`/`room_rents` inputs alongside the existing `category_rents`, following the identical "unmatched rows fall back to template rent" pattern as `ROOM_CATEGORY`; `POST /api/agreements/renewal-offers` validates the corresponding field is present for each new strategy. `BulkCampaignSheet.tsx`'s wizard gained two more strategy tabs (now 5: FLAT/PERCENT/CATEGORY/FLOOR/ROOM) with the same live client-side preview (matched-tenant count, avg delta, revenue impact) already built for the other strategies, deriving `uniqueFloors`/`uniqueRooms` from the already-fetched queue rows. Plain string columns (`bulk_renewal_batch.renewal_strategy`/`strategy_type`, not a Prisma enum) meant no schema/migration change was needed. 4 new backend tests (19/19 in `renewal-offer-service.test.ts`). See [[Backend]].

### Fixed
- **2026-07-21**: Owner "Agreements" tab UX fixes found from real-device screenshots of the mobile rebuild above. (1) `agreementDecisionInclude()`/`tenantPayload()` in `renewal-decision-service.ts` never selected `room.room_type`, so every renewal queue row rendered "Room 401 (N/A)" — added `room_type: true` to the select and the returned shape (`tests/renewal-decision-service.test.ts` still green, 10/10). (2) `BottomNav.tsx`'s "Agreements" tab linked to `/agreements/lifecycle-recovery` and only highlighted as active on that exact path — tapping it landed on a one-time legacy-data-migration checklist instead of the day-to-day Renewal Pipeline, and the tab didn't show active at all while on `/agreements/renewals`.
