Now points at `/agreements/renewals` and highlights active for any `/agreements/*` path; `RenewalQueueView` gained a small "Legacy agreement data recovery →" link so Lifecycle Recovery stays reachable. (3) `OwnerQuickActions.tsx`'s floating add-tenant/payment/expense FAB visibly overlapped renewal queue rows and recovery progress cards in screenshots — excluded `/agreements/*` from where it renders, since those pages have their own primary actions (Campaign, Refresh, Export). (4) Queue/offer row action rows had grown to 3 buttons (Create Offer/Send/Revise + Workspace + Profile) after this session's Individual Renewal Workspace addition — dropped the standalone "Profile" link from both `RenewalQueueList` and `RenewalOffersList` since `RenewalWorkspaceView`'s header already links to the tenant profile one tap in. (5) Added a right-edge fade-gradient affordance to the horizontally-scrolling pipeline funnel and filter-chip rows so a partially-visible next card/chip reads as "swipe for more," not a rendering bug.
- **2026-07-21**: `AgreementLifecycleRecoveryView.tsx` redesigned — the page showed the identical 4 stat cards + 3 coverage cards + a duplicate progress card + 4 stacked full-width filter dropdowns + a desktop `<table>` regardless of whether recovery was 0% or 100% done, so a fully-recovered account ("100% recovered, 0 agreements remaining") still presented a full page of now-inert migration UI, and the worklist table was unusable on a phone (4 columns crammed into 375px, "Sri Adithya Boys Hostel-1" wrapping 3 lines). Now: the status banner is itself the expand/collapse control — once `r4Ready` is true it defaults to collapsed (a user can still expand it), and the 4 stat cards + duplicate progress card collapsed into one compact card (inline stats + one shared progress bar + 3 mini coverage bars); the worklist table became mobile-first card rows (same pattern as the renewal queue); the 4 stacked filters became a 2×2 grid; the recovery form modal moved onto the shared `ResponsiveDialog` sheet primitive. No backend or business-logic changes.

### Added
- **2026-07-21**: Individual Renewal Workspace — new `GET /api/agreements/renewals/[agreementId]` (`renewal-workspace-read-model.ts`, composes existing services rather than recalculating, see [[Decisions]] ADR-018) and a new owner-facing detail page at `/agreements/renewals/:agreementId` (`RenewalWorkspaceView.tsx`), reachable via a new "Workspace" link on every queue row and offer card. Shows current-vs-proposed/successor agreement comparison, a blocking-issues panel driven by real `evaluateActivationReadiness()` output (only evaluated once a successor draft exists), a financial summary (dues/overdue/deposit/future-credit via `financialReadModelService`), and three tabs — Timeline (first UI consumer of the `RenewalTimelineEvent` table built earlier this session), full Offer History, and Documents. Three new backend tests (`tests/renewal-workspace-read-model.test.ts`) run against a real test-schema Postgres connection with factories, matching `financial-read-model-service.test.ts`'s convention for composed read models. See [[APIs]], [[Frontend]], [[Backend]].
- **2026-07-20**: Added the `correction_cases`/`correction_case_events` schema — the foundation of a new Business Recovery Platform (Correction Case lifecycle: DRAFT→PREVIEW→VALIDATED→EXECUTING→COMPLETED/FAILED) for owner-facing undo/financial-correction workflows. Schema only in this change (Task 1 of a 17-task phased build) — see [[Database]], `docs/business-logic/business-recovery-platform-architecture.md`. Three enums control domain (PAYMENTS/ROOMS/AGREEMENTS/EXPENSES/ADMISSIONS/RENEWALS/SETTINGS/DOCUMENTS/KYC/RESERVATIONS), tier (OPERATIONAL_UNDO/FINANCIAL_CORRECTION/ADMINISTRATIVE_REVERSAL), and status; two tables support case lifecycle and append-only audit trail. `hostel_id` is non-nullable (per the repo's multi-hostel invariant), and `idempotency_key` is unique (safe retry on duplicate submission).
- Obsidian-compatible documentation workspace under `docs/obsidian/`.
- Expense export (CSV/Excel/PDF) on the Expenses Workspace — see the 2026-07-18 dated entry below for the full write-up ([[APIs]] `/api/expenses/export`, [[Decisions]] ADR-009).
- **2026-07-18**: Expense export report content overhaul — dynamic report title (date-range/category/selected-scope aware), full export metadata (generated by/at, hostel, reporting period + duration, export scope, sort order, report version `v1`), a labeled filter snapshot, a Financial Summary (revenue, net profit, expense ratio, average daily spend, transaction count, largest expense with full context, largest category/vendor), and Payment Insights (per-status amount breakdown). Financials are computed via new shared functions in `expense-service.ts` (`getBusinessRevenue`, `computeNetProfit`, `computeProfitMargin`, `computeExpenseRatio`, `withCategoryPercentages`) reused by both the dashboard KPI cards and the export — see [[Decisions]] ADR-010, [[Business-Rules]]. XLSX is now sectioned (Metadata → Financial Summary → Category Breakdown → Expense Table) instead of one flat table; PDF gained a footer (generated-by/on, page N of M). A revenue-lookup failure now degrades only the Financial Summary (marked "unavailable") instead of failing the export. Extended `tests/expense-export.test.ts` with a dashboard-vs-export financial parity test and a combined-filter (category+status+recurring+amount range+search) row-set parity test.

- **2026-07-20**: Renewal Timeline — new append-only `RenewalTimelineEvent` table + `renewal-timeline-service.ts`, giving the Agreement Renewal subsystem a queryable audit trail for the first time (previously owner-side offer actions had no DB record at all, only `logger.info()` lines). Instrumented every renewal write path: offer created/sent/discussed/revised/accepted/declined/expired, draft created, renewal activated/activation-blocked. Events are written inside the same transaction as the mutation they describe. See [[Decisions]] ADR-016, [[Database]].
- **2026-07-20**: Business Recovery Platform — first three payment-correction handlers, all registered into `correctionRegistry` and driven through the `correction_cases` lifecycle (`createCase → preview → validate → execute`): **Reverse Payment** (`PAYMENT_REVERSAL`, reverses one payment's effect on its obligation via a new offsetting `payments` row + `LEDGER_CORRECTION` ledger debit, never mutating the original payment), **Transfer Payment** (`PAYMENT_TRANSFER`, reverses the source payment and re-allocates the same amount to a target tenant's open obligations via the existing FIFO settlement engine), and **Edit Reference/Notes** (`PAYMENT_REFERENCE_EDIT`, the one handler that performs a direct update — but only to `payment_groups.reference_number`/`notes`, never to `payments`). Reverse/Transfer are one-case-per-payment (deterministic `PAYMENT_REVERSAL:<paymentId>`/`PAYMENT_TRANSFER:<paymentId>` idempotency keys); cross-hostel transfers are blocked by policy at both case-creation and execute-time; all three handlers' `createCase()` independently re-validate hostel ownership (not just `policy.canExecute()`) so a mismatched-hostel case can never be persisted. See [[Business-Rules]] (Correction Cases — Payment corrections), [[Database]].
- **2026-07-20**: Business Recovery Platform — `/api/recovery/cases/*` HTTP routes (`GET /api/recovery/cases` list, `POST /api/recovery/cases` create+auto-preview, `GET /api/recovery/cases/[id]` detail incl. event trail, `POST /api/recovery/cases/[id]/validate`, `POST /api/recovery/cases/[id]/execute` — also the retry entrypoint for `FAILED` cases), exposing the Reverse/Transfer/Edit-Reference correction handlers over HTTP for the first time. All five require an `OWNER`/`ADMIN` session (`resolveOwnerScope`), and every route verifies the target hostel (create) or the case's own `hostelId` (detail/validate/execute — read from the case row, never trusted from the client) actually belongs to the caller's owner via `requireHostelBelongsToOwner`, closing the same "trust client-supplied hostelId" gap flagged in earlier phase-1 tasks. See [[APIs]] (Recovery / Corrections), [[Business-Rules]].
- **2026-07-20**: Owner Action Registry — new `ownerActionRegistry` (`backend-next/src/services/owner-actions/`), a read-only catalog of which owner-facing actions are available per entity, plus its first endpoint, `GET /api/owner-actions?entity=<entity>&tenantId=<id>`. First registered actions: `TENANT_EDIT_PERSONAL_INFO` (entity `tenant`), `PAYMENT_RECEIVE` (entity `tenant`), `ROOM_MOVE` (entity `room`, no frontend consumer yet — deliberately out of scope). `TenantProfilePage.tsx` now sources the "Request Change"/"Receive Payment" button labels on `PrimaryActionsBar` from this catalog instead of hardcoding them, falling back to the same defaults if a lookup misses. Catalog-only in this slice — no action's execution routes through the registry yet; it exists so future `CORRECTION`-category actions can register into it later. See [[Features]] (Owner Action Registry), [[APIs]].
- **2026-07-20**: Correct Payment (Reverse only) — the first real UI consumer of the Business Recovery Platform's `/api/recovery/cases/*` routes. New `frontend-v2/src/features/recovery/api/index.ts` (`recoveryService.createCase/validate/execute/getById`) and `frontend-v2/src/app/components/modals/CorrectPaymentModal.tsx` (self-contained reason → preview → confirm flow: `createCase` runs create+preview in one round trip, then "Confirm Reversal" runs `validate` immediately followed by `execute` as one logical step — a 422 `VALIDATION_REJECTED` from `validate` stops `execute` from ever being called and surfaces the rejection reason inline via `ErrorCard`/`getHmsError`). Wired into `TenantProfilePage.tsx`'s Financial Activity feed: `FinancialActivityCard.tsx`/`FinancialActivity.tsx` gained an `onCorrectPayment` prop rendering a "Correct Payment" button next to "View Receipt" on payment entries; closing the modal invalidates `queryKeys.tenants.{obligations,financialTimeline,advance,full,overview}` and calls the page's existing `refetch()`. Per explicit product decision this bypasses both the Owner Action Registry (not sourced from `ownerActionsService`) and a separate Recovery Center dashboard (deferred to Phase 3) — it's a direct, self-contained wiring. Transfer (`PAYMENT_TRANSFER`) and Edit Reference/Notes (`PAYMENT_REFERENCE_EDIT`) UIs remain unbuilt — follow-ups once this pattern is proven. See [[Features]] (Correct Payment (Reverse / Transfer)), [[APIs]] (Recovery / Corrections).
- **2026-07-20**: `CorrectPaymentModal.tsx` gained a correction-type toggle ("Wrong / Reverse" vs "Wrong Tenant / Transfer", shown pre-preview only) wiring up the already-built-but-unused `PAYMENT_TRANSFER` correction handler for the first time from the UI. Transfer mode adds a destination-tenant picker reusing the existing `paymentService.quickCollectSearch` tenant-search pattern from `RecordPaymentModal.tsx` (debounced 250ms, fires only once the query is ≥2 characters and no tenant is yet selected), and requires a selected `toTenantId` before "Preview Impact" is allowed. `handlePreview` now sends `caseType: 'PAYMENT_TRANSFER'`/`input: {paymentId, toTenantId}` in Transfer mode vs. the existing `'PAYMENT_REVERSAL'`/`{paymentId}` in Reverse mode; header copy, the confirm button label ("Confirm Transfer"/"Confirm Reversal"), the success-state message, and the destructive-action warning banner all switch on `correctionType`. No backend or API-wrapper changes were needed — `recoveryService.createCase` already accepted an arbitrary `caseType`/`input`. See [[Features]] (Correct Payment (Reverse / Transfer)), [[APIs]] (Recovery / Corrections).
- **2026-07-21**: `CorrectPaymentModal` gained an optional `onSuccessReverse` callback, fired only after a successful REVERSE correction (never Transfer), that `TenantProfilePage.tsx` uses to reopen the existing Receive Payment flow (`RecordPaymentModal`) scoped to the same tenant about 1.3s after the reversal's own success state finishes displaying — closing the "wrong amount" correction loop (reverse the bad entry, then immediately guide the owner into re-recording the correct one) by reusing the page's existing payment-modal state rather than adding a new modal. See [[Features]] (Correct Payment (Reverse / Transfer)).
- **2026-07-21**: Change Rent — owner-facing entry point for the already-built `POST /api/tenants/:id/change-rent`. New `tenantService.changeRent` API wrapper (`frontend-v2/src/features/tenants/api/index.js`) and `frontend-v2/src/app/components/modals/ChangeRentModal.tsx` (new rent amount → effective month, sourced from the page's already-loaded zero-payment upcoming RENT obligations → reason → password re-entry via `identityService.confirmIdentity(password, 'CHANGE_RENT')`, mirroring `WaiveObligationModal.tsx`'s identity flow). Wired into `TenantProfilePage.tsx`: a "Change Rent" button next to `PrimaryActionsBar` (shown when tenant status is `ACTIVE`), and a new `upcomingRentObligations` derivation from the page's existing `dues.items` data (no second obligations fetch). Success invalidates `queryKeys.tenants.{obligations,financialTimeline,advance,full,overview}` and calls the page's `refetch()`. See [[Features]] (Change Rent), [[APIs]], [[Business-Rules]].
- **2026-07-21**: Change Rent — fixed two task-review findings. (1) `applyRentChangeInTx` now also writes `tenants.monthly_rent` (mirroring the `tenantContractSync` pattern from renewal activation) so the frontend's "Current rent" display no longer goes stale after a successful change — new test in `tests/integration/rent-change-service.test.ts`. (2) `ChangeRentModal.tsx`'s success screen now shows the server-reported `obligationsUpdated` count from the `changeRent` response instead of only the pre-submit client-computed preview, surfacing (not preventing) the edge case where a Payment-Reversal-corrected obligation nets to zero-paid and still appears in the client preview while the backend's stricter zero-payment-*records* guard correctly skips it. See [[Bugs]], [[Business-Rules]].
- **2026-07-21**: Final whole-branch review fixes (payment-correction + change-rent branch), frontend-only. `ChangeRentModal.tsx`: fixed the empty-upcoming-obligations dead end where the modal's copy promised the agreement would still be repriced but the form could never actually be submitted (`effectiveFromMonth` stuck at `''`) — now defaults to the first day of next calendar month (UTC) via a new `nextMonthStartIso()` helper, with empty-state copy naming that month; also made `affectedCount`'s date comparison explicit (`Date`-based instead of raw ISO-string `>=`), same behavior on current data. `TenantProfilePage.tsx`: corrected a comment on `upcomingRentObligations` that incorrectly asserted its net-`paid === 0` preview filter "mirrors" the backend's real zero-payment-*records* guard (it doesn't, post-Payment-Reversal — see [[Bugs]]); and corrected a stale comment near `CorrectPaymentModal`'s render that still said "Reverse only — Transfer ... future task" (Transfer shipped earlier in this branch; only Edit Reference/Notes remains unbuilt). See [[Bugs]] (Change Rent modal was unsubmittable...).
- **2026-07-21**: Flexible Payment Links — tenant self-service link generation, the final task of a multi-task branch. `POST /api/payments/pay-link` (pre-existing, owner-only) now also accepts a `TENANT`-role session, force-scoped to `session.tenant_id` and forbidding a caller-supplied `obligationId`/mismatched `tenantId` (owner behavior unchanged). New "Share Payment Link" button on the tenant portal's Financials page (`frontend-v2/src/portal/pages/TenantFinancialsPage.tsx`, next to "Make Payment") calls the existing `paymentService.generatePayLink({ tenantId })` wrapper and copies the returned `/pay/<token>` URL to the clipboard via a toast, so a tenant can hand a payable link to a guardian or anyone else paying on their behalf without needing the owner to generate it. No new files; the edit was made in-place to an existing allowlisted file under the frozen `src/portal/` tree. See [[Features]] (Flexible Payment Links (tenant self-service link generation)), [[APIs]].
- **2026-07-21**: Payment links are now amount-flexible and tenant-scoped instead of locked to one obligation's exact balance — the rest of the same multi-task branch, preceding the tenant self-service entry above. `payment_link_tokens.obligation_id` became an optional default-amount hint (was a required FK resolved from exactly one `PENDING`/`PARTIAL` obligation, throwing if none existed); `PaymentLinkService.getOrCreateToken` now unconditionally mints a tenant-scoped token, so "Share Payment Link" shows regardless of outstanding dues. The public payer page (`/api/payments/pay/[token]`) now renders an editable amount input with a live FIFO-allocation `preview` action (`financialPaymentFacade.previewSettlement`) instead of a fixed pre-picked obligation balance, and checkout now goes through a new amount-first `paymentService.createAmountPaymentIntent` (mirrors `createMultiObligationPaymentIntent`, feeds the entered amount through the same `buildSettlementPlan` FIFO engine the offline "Receive Payment" flow uses, crediting any leftover as future rent). See [[Features]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Database]], [[Decisions]] ADR-017, [[APIs]].

### Removed
- **2026-07-18**: The "Expense type (auto-detected)" chip row (Operational/Utility/Maintenance/Staff/Emergency) from the Add/Edit Expense modal — it asked the owner to manually confirm/override an internal analytics classification that has no bearing on the expense itself. `operational_type` is now always computed server-side from category via a new canonical map, `deriveOperationalType()` in `expense-service.ts` (`CATEGORY_TO_OPERATIONAL_TYPE`), replacing the old fuzzy title+category regex heuristic (`suggestedOperationalType`) that was duplicated on both frontend (`features/expenses/constants.ts`) and backend. `updateExpense` now recomputes it whenever `category` changes; existing rows work unmigrated and self-correct on next category edit. New tests: `tests/expense-operational-type.test.ts`. See [[Business-Rules]], [[Decisions]] ADR-011.

### Changed
- **2026-07-21**: Owner Renewal Workspace mobile optimization (`RenewalQueueView.tsx`, `/agreements/renewals`) — purely a frontend recomposition, **no backend or API changes** beyond two JSDoc-only type annotations (see Fixed, below). The 1,388-line single-file view was split into `app/components/views/renewal/{utils,RenewalPipelineTracker,RenewalQueueList,RenewalOffersList,BulkCampaignSheet,SingleOfferSheet,ReviseOfferSheet}.tsx`. Three hand-rolled fixed-overlay modals (Renewal Campaigns Wizard, Create Custom Stay Offer, Revise Renewal Offer) were rebuilt on the existing `ResponsiveDialog` primitive (`Dialog` on desktop, swipe-to-dismiss `Drawer` bottom sheet on mobile, `< 768px` via `useIsMobile`) — previously these were `fixed inset-0` centered dialogs that were cramped and hard to scroll on an iPhone-width viewport. Touch targets on primary actions bumped to 44px (`h-11`) on mobile, numeric fields got `inputMode="numeric"` for the correct iOS keyboard, and the pipeline funnel became a horizontally swipeable, scroll-snapped strip below `sm:`. The queue list now client-side-splits into a "Needs Your Attention" group (critical `decision_state`s: `EXPIRED_AND_RENT_OVERDUE`/`RENEWAL_OVERDUE_CRITICAL`) shown first, and everything else below — reusing data already fetched, no new endpoint. The Renewal Campaigns Wizard sheet gained a live, client-side-computed preview (matched-tenant count, average rent delta, monthly revenue impact) that recalculates as the strategy/amount/percent/category-rent fields change, computed from the already-fetched queue rows and explicitly labeled as an estimate (the actual bulk-generate endpoint's eligibility matching isn't reproduced client-side). This is a first design pass grounded in a larger, not-yet-implemented "Renewal Management Platform" concept (grouping by floor/building/deposit-status, a separate Batch Review screen, and a full Individual Renewal Workspace with timeline/readiness tabs) — those need new backend fields (room category/floor/building/deposit-status aren't returned by the renewal queue or offer-list APIs today) and are deliberately out of scope here. See [[Frontend]].

### Fixed
- **2026-07-21**: `RenewalQueueView`'s "Create Custom Stay Offer" sheet read the maintenance charge from `agreement.contract?.maintenance_charge`, which the renewal-queue API never populates (it returns the field as `contract.maintenance`) — always rendered ₹0. Fixed by reading `contract?.maintenance` first, falling back to the old field names. Also added JSDoc parameter types to `agreementService.getRenewalQueue`/`getRenewalOffers` (`frontend-v2/src/features/agreements/api/index.js`) — their un-annotated destructured-parameter-with-default signatures made TypeScript infer an incomplete parameter shape (e.g. dropping `hostelId` entirely) at call sites during the mobile-optimization rewrite above.

### Changed
- **2026-07-21**: Tenant Profile UI/UX consolidation (owner-facing `TenantProfilePage.tsx`), purely a frontend recomposition — **no backend or business-logic changes**. Four redundant-section problems fixed: (1) the fake scroll-into-view `FinancialWorkspaceNav` was replaced with real `Tabs` (Obligations/Activity/Documents/Stay); (2) three overlapping activity/ledger feeds — Financial Activity, Ledger & Accounting Statement, plus a standalone "Recent Activity" card and a standalone "Invitation History Logs" card — were merged into one filterable `UnifiedActivityTimeline` (the Activity tab), which still threads the existing `onCorrectPayment` prop for the per-payment "Correct Payment" button; (3) two overlapping risk/insight cards (`TenantHealthCard`'s composite score + checklist, `OwnerInsights`'s restated prose warnings) were merged into one `RiskComplianceCard`; (4) eight scattered action buttons across three groups (plus a separate adjacent "Change Rent" button) were consolidated into one `PrimaryActionsBar` — 1 primary + 3 secondary always-visible buttons, and a "More" overflow menu holding Share Payment Link/Request Change/Change Rent/Check-out; (5) a standalone KYC verification card and the existing `DocumentsHub` were merged into one `DocumentsTab`, and a new "Stay" tab surfaces room-allocation history + the move-out workflow that used to be a lower-page collapsible card. Deleted: `TenantHealthCard.tsx`, `OwnerInsights.tsx`, `FinancialActivity.tsx`, `LedgerStatement.tsx`, `ActivityTimeline.tsx`, `FinancialWorkspaceNav.tsx`. Added: `RiskComplianceCard.tsx`, `UnifiedActivityTimeline.tsx`, `DocumentsTab.tsx`. See [[Features]] (Tenant Profile UI/UX consolidation), [[Frontend]].
- **2026-07-20**: Consolidated renewal readiness/activation logic — previously hand-duplicated across `AgreementLifecycleService.activateScheduledRenewals` (cron), `AgreementRenewalSigningService.signRenewalAgreement` (manual signing), and `AgreementRenewalService.createRenewalDraft` (draft creation) — into two new shared modules, `renewal-readiness-engine.ts` and `renewal-activation-engine.ts`. Zero business-rule changes; each call site preserves its own existing error codes/messages and its own signature/rules-resolution behavior (cron still copies the predecessor's signature forward, manual signing still captures a fresh e-signature). One real gap closed along the way: manual signing now syncs `tenants.monthly_rent/security_deposit/maintenance_charge` like cron activation already did. See [[Decisions]] ADR-015, [[Backend]].
- **2026-07-18**: Full-codebase documentation audit. [[Architecture]], [[Backend]], [[Frontend]], [[Database]], [[APIs]], [[Business-Rules]], [[Features]], [[Decisions]], and [[README]] were rewritten from a direct read of `backend-next/` (294 API routes, ~140 services, full Prisma schema) and `frontend-v2/` (full `src/` tree) — previously these were generic starter templates. Confirmed the `docs/` reference tree (`docs/data-models/schema.md`, `docs/data-models/enums.md`) is out of date relative to the live schema (19 undocumented models, 13 undocumented enums, one stale rename — see [[Database]] §6). Also confirmed a decommissioned multi-hostel SaaS billing/subscription subsystem (37 routes returning 410) — see [[Decisions]] ADR-006.
- **2026-07-18**: Financial Control Center (owner Money screen, Overview tab) redesigned from a dense, chart-heavy layout into five focused sections — Collection Progress, Priority Collections, Smart Insights, Property Finance (all-hostels view only), Recent Transactions (last 5, with the existing searchable `PaymentLedger` demoted to an expandable "View All"). New pure-logic module `FinancialControlCenter`'s sibling `financeInsights.ts` computes urgency buckets, today's-collection, per-hostel ranking, and the Smart Insights list — all derived from data already fetched, no backend changes. Removed 13 pre-existing dead widget files (`CashPosition`, `CollectionPipeline`, `HealthBar`, `OverdueIntelligence`, `PaymentAttemptsIntelligence`, `RiskZone`, `RoomPerformance`, `SmartFilters`, `TodayPriorities`, `FinancialSummaryStrip`, `AdvancedPaymentTable`, `CashflowCharts`, `FinancialTimeline`) plus 3 newly-orphaned chart components (`CashflowForecast`, `CollectionAnalytics`, `ExpenseIntelligence`) whose only consumer was the removed charts section. See [[Features]].

### Fixed
- **2026-07-18**: Expenses Workspace search box unmounting the whole tab into a loading skeleton on every keystroke (`placeholderData: keepPreviousData`) — see [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-18**: Export button throwing in production only due to a Vite production-bundler mis-transform of a chained dynamic-import destructure — see [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-19**: Cron-activated renewal agreements (`AgreementLifecycleService.activateScheduledRenewals`) now generate a rent schedule inside the activation transaction, matching manual signing (`AgreementRenewalSigningService`) — previously cron-activated renewals shipped with zero rent obligations. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-19**: Manual renewal signing (`AgreementRenewalSigningService.signRenewalAgreement`) now blocks on an unpaid renewal security deposit obligation, matching the check cron activation already had — previously only the cron path enforced this. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-19**: Cron renewal activation (`AgreementLifecycleService.activateScheduledRenewals`) now checks predecessor renewability, active move-out, and lifecycle-metadata completeness before activating, and both it and `RenewalOfferService.acceptOffer` now use row-locked, count-checked conditional updates for every renewal-chain mutation, closing two concurrency races that could corrupt the renewal chain. See [[Bugs]], [[Decisions]] ADR-012.
- **2026-07-19**: `RenewalOfferService.expireStaleOffers()` — previously implemented but never called from anywhere despite its docstring — is now wired into `AgreementLifecycleService.processDailyLifecycle`. Also fixed two `vi.mock(...)` calls in `tests/agreement-renewal-activation.test.ts` that used test-file-relative paths instead of the mocked module's actual location, so `processDailyLifecycle` tests were silently making real WhatsApp API calls. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-19**: `RenewalStatusService.determineRenewalStage` now suppresses all WhatsApp expiry reminders once a successor agreement exists (offer accepted or manual draft created) — previously a tenant who had already renewed kept getting "please renew" reminders on the predecessor. See [[Bugs]], [[Decisions]] ADR-013.
- **2026-07-20**: `reverseObligationPayment` (Reverse/Transfer Payment corrections) no longer writes a `LEDGER_CORRECTION` debit when reversing a payment on a RENT (or other non-deposit) obligation — only ADVANCE/SECURITY_DEPOSIT obligations ever received a matching original ledger credit (per `settlement-engine.ts`), so reversing any other type had been silently eating into unrelated future-rent-credit balance. `computeImpact()` in both `payment-reversal-handler.ts` and `payment-transfer-handler.ts` updated to match, so the correction preview no longer promises a ledger entry execute won't create. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-20**: "Correct Payment" button on the Financial Activity feed no longer shows on grouped `PAYMENT_GROUP_SETTLED` cards representing more than one underlying `payments` row (a settlement split across obligations via FIFO) — `receiptPaymentId` on such a card only ever points at the first payment, so reversing it undid just a fraction of the amount shown. Gated the button on `payments.length <= 1` in `frontend-v2/src/features/tenants/components/financial/FinancialActivityCard.tsx`; "View Receipt" is unaffected. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-21**: iPhone Safari no longer auto-zooms into form fields on Add Expense / Add Tenant (and every other form). An unlayered `@media (max-width: 639.98px)` rule in `src/styles/globals.css` forces `input`/`select`/`textarea` to 16px on mobile — iOS only auto-zooms focused fields below 16px, and our fields defaulted to `text-sm` (14px). Desktop density unchanged. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-21**: Payment reversals now render as a distinct **"Payment Reversed"** event (red tone, undo icon, `-₹X` signed amount, "Reversal of ₹X payment" summary) in the tenant Activity feed and obligation history, instead of being indistinguishable from an incoming "Payment Received" with a confusing `₹-X paid via …` line. `financial-timeline-service.ts` now tags reversal `payments` rows (negative amount / `REVERSAL:` reference) with `metadata.is_reversal` + `reverses_payment_id`, and `getEventDisplay`/`FinancialActivityCard.tsx` branch on it. See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-19**: Renewal WhatsApp reminder stages (`RenewalStatusService.determineRenewalStage`) now match on threshold bands instead of exact-day equality — a stage that would have been permanently skipped by a single missed cron run now still fires on the next run, relying on the existing per-`(stage, agreement)` delivery idempotency to prevent duplicate sends. See [[Bugs]], [[Decisions]] ADR-014.
- **2026-07-20**: Tenant Financials page's "Payment Due Soon" card (`TenantFinancialsPage.tsx`) now shows the due date of the obligation its own displayed amount is drawn from, instead of a due date resolved via an unrelated "does today fall inside this billing cycle" match — the two could point at different obligations (e.g. an already-fully-paid cycle's due date next to a later, still-owing obligation's amount). See [[Bugs]].
- **2026-07-20**: `validatePaymentAttemptSettlementInTx` (`payment-service.ts`) no longer false-positives on gateway rent payments that allocate into a `SECURITY_DEPOSIT`/`ADVANCE` obligation — it now correctly distinguishes an unallocated future-rent-credit topup from a deposit "collected" ledger marker that mirrors money already counted elsewhere, instead of summing both into one check. Previously this rolled back an entirely correct settlement and returned a 500 despite the payment provider having captured the charge, leaving real captured payments with zero internal record. See [[Bugs]].

## 2026-07-18 — Expenses Workspace redesign

### Added
- Redesigned owner-facing "Expenses Workspace" tab (`frontend-v2/src/app/components/hostel-detail/tabs/ExpensesTab.tsx`, now a thin orchestrator) into a dedicated component set under `hostel-detail/tabs/expenses/`: `ExpenseDashboard` (consolidated Revenue/Expenses/Net Profit + Top Category/Unusual Spending/Top Vendor/Largest Expense action tiles + monthly-trend sparkline, replacing two previously-duplicated summary card sections), `ExpenseFilterBar` (search, status, category, vendor, payment method, recurring, amount range, saved filter views via `localStorage`), `ExpenseList`/`ExpenseCard` (dense rows, clickable-to-view + `•••` overflow menu instead of 4 permanent action buttons), `ExpenseDetailsModal` ("decision support": inline receipt preview/lightbox, category-anomaly trend banner, "similar past expenses" summary), `AddExpenseModal` (rebuilt on new sectioned Basic Information/Financial Details/Receipt/Advanced Options layout instead of one long scroll), `CategoryPicker` (searchable combobox with a personalized "recently used" section, replacing the old button-grid + redundant `<select>`), `ReceiptPreview`, `ExpenseCategoryBreakdown`, `ExpenseVendors`, `ExpenseInsightsPanel`.
- New shared primitive `frontend-v2/src/app/components/ui/responsive-dialog.tsx` (`ResponsiveDialog*`) — renders a wide desktop `Dialog` or a mobile bottom-sheet `Drawer` from one JSX tree, switching on the existing `useIsMobile()` hook. Re-exported from `shared/ui` and `shared/ui/modals`. `DialogContent` gained an optional `showCloseButton` prop (default `true`, backward compatible) to support it.
- `frontend-v2/src/features/expenses/constants.ts` — canonical category list, icon/tone maps, and client-side category-suggestion heuristics, consolidating 5 previously-duplicated copies across `ExpensesTab.tsx`, `FinancialControlCenter.tsx`, `OwnerQuickActions.tsx`, and `AddExpenseModal.tsx`.
- Backend: `GET /api/expenses` gained `recurring` (`true`/`false`) and `amountMin`/`amountMax` query params, implemented as additive Prisma `where` conditions in `expense-service.ts::getAllExpenses` (composable with all existing filters, applied before pagination). Response payload gained `vendor_breakdown` (server-side `groupBy` on `vendor_name` for the current month, mirroring the existing `category_breakdown` pattern) — replaces a client-side, page-limited vendor approximation with an exact figure. See [[APIs]], [[Decisions]] ADR-008.
- `backend-next/tests/expense-filters.test.ts` — integration tests for the new filters (composition with existing filters, pagination correctness, boundary values) and `vendor_breakdown` accuracy.

### Changed
- Color hierarchy in this feature area: solid `bg-accent` fill reserved for one primary action per screen (Add/Save, Details-panel Edit); selected/secondary states use the tinted `border-accent bg-accent/10 text-accent` treatment instead of solid fill.

### Added — Expense Export
- `GET /api/expenses/export` (`backend-next/app/api/expenses/export/route.ts`) — streams CSV/XLSX or generates a PDF report of expenses matching the same filters as `GET /api/expenses`. Built on a new shared query builder, `buildExpenseLedgerWhere()` (extracted from `expense-service.ts::getAllExpenses`, which now calls it too), so exported rows can never drift from what the UI's list/filters show. Rate-limited to 10 exports/min/owner.
- `lib/services/expense-export-service.ts` — format/scope-agnostic export core, deliberately decoupled from HTTP (no `NextRequest`/`Response`) so a future scheduled-export cron or saved-report-template runner can call the same generators without redesigning anything:
  - CSV and XLSX are true streams — batched `findMany` (500 rows/batch) feeds a `ReadableStream` (hand-rolled CSV) or an `exceljs` `WorkbookWriter` (genuine incremental zip writer, piped via a Node `PassThrough` → `Readable.toWeb()`) — memory stays bounded regardless of total export size.
  - PDF (`pdf-lib`, consistent with the existing receipt-PDF convention) is a business report — summary metrics, category breakdown, vendor summary, applied filters, and an expense-list table capped at 500 rows with an explicit truncation note (a report artifact, not an unbounded data dump; use CSV/XLSX for the full list).
  - Three export scopes: `current_view` (mirrors the on-screen page's `limit`/`offset`), `all_matching` (streams everything matching current filters, ignoring pagination), `selected` (exports exactly the given expense IDs, ignoring other filters — a distinct, much simpler `id IN (...)` query, not a variant of the filter builder).
- New dependency: `exceljs` (backend-next) — the existing `xlsx`/SheetJS package doesn't support true streaming writes for the xlsx binary format in its free edition; `exceljs`'s `WorkbookWriter` does. See [[Decisions]] ADR-009.
- Frontend: `ExpenseExportMenu.tsx` (format + scope picker, placed next to the search/filter bar via a new `trailingActions` slot on `ExpenseFilterBar`), lightweight row selection (checkbox per `ExpenseCard`, "Select all visible"/"Clear selection" in `ExpenseList`, selection state owned by `ExpensesTab`) to make the "Selected Expenses" scope actually usable rather than a permanent stub. `expenseService.export()` in `features/expenses/api/index.js` requests the file as a Blob (Bearer-token auth means a plain `<a href>` navigation won't carry credentials) and triggers a client-side download.
- `backend-next/tests/expense-export.test.ts` — 7 integration tests: CSV/XLSX/PDF correctness, filter/scope consistency with `getAllExpenses`, `selected`-scope ID-only behavior, `current_view` pagination, and summary-aggregate consistency.

### Fixed
- `FinancialControlCenter.tsx`'s "Add Expense" launcher was passing `defaultHostelId`/`defaultHostelLabel` props that `AddExpenseModal` never declared or consumed (dead props, pre-existing) — removed rather than wired up, since every expense created through this workspace is forced to portfolio-level `expense_scope: 'BUSINESS'` by `features/expenses/api/index.js` regardless (see [[APIs]] `/api/expenses`); wiring hostel-scoping through would be a business-logic change out of scope for this pass.

## 2026-07-16 — Owner Financial Workspace redesign

### Added
- Redesigned owner-facing tenant financial UI: Summary → Financial Health Banner → Primary Actions → Obligations → Financial Activity → Ledger (collapsed) → Documents.
- 5-step Receive Payment flow (Amount → Settlement Preview → Confirm → Success → Receipt).
- Canonical `FinancialReadModel` (`backend-next/src/services/payments/financial-read-model-service.ts`) composing `financialService.getTenantDues()` + `tenantFinancialLedgerService.getBalance()`. See [[Decisions]] ADR-001.
- New route `GET /api/tenants/[id]/financial-timeline` (tenant-level financial activity feed).

### Fixed
- `financial-timeline-service.ts`: field mismatch (`entry_type` vs actual Prisma field `type`) that crashed whenever ledger rows existed. See [[Bugs]].
- Owner/tenant surfaces disagreeing on Outstanding/Overdue/Future Credit due to duplicated calculators, one using the wrong column (`o.amount` vs `o.total_amount`). See [[Bugs]], `docs/business-logic/financial-consistency-investigation-report.md`.
- UTC-vs-local-timezone bug in `derivePresentationStatus()` (unused function, safe to fix in place).

### Changed
- `billing-timeline-service.ts` trimmed to the two fields (`items`, `next_rent_generation`) that had real frontend consumers.

## How to use this file

- Add entries under `[Unreleased]` as you work; cut a dated section when a meaningful batch of work lands (doesn't need to map 1:1 to a deploy).
- Use `Added` / `Changed` / `Fixed` / `Removed` / `Deprecated` / `Security` headers per Keep a Changelog.
- Link to [[Decisions]] for the ADR behind a change, [[Bugs]] for the bug being fixed, [[Features]] for the feature affected.
